




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saṃyuttanikāyau


saḷāyatanavaggau


1. saḷāyatanasaṃyuttaṃ

1. anic‍cavaggau

1. ajjhattānic‍casuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ| aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| sautaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāyau anic‍cau| yadanic‍caṃ…pai॰… manau anic‍cau| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ  passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, sautasmimpi nibbindati, ghānasmimpi nibbindati, jivhāyapi nibbindati, kāyasmimpi nibbindati, manasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ajjhattadukkhasuttaṃ

2. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| sautaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ dukkhaṃ… jivhā dukkhā… kāyau dukkhau… manau dukkhau| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ajjhattānattasuttaṃ

3. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| sautaṃ anattā…pai॰… ghānaṃ anattā… jivhā anattā… kāyau anattā… manau anattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bāhirānic‍casuttaṃ

4. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anic‍cā| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpaisupi nibbindati, saddaisupi nibbindati, gandhaisupi nibbindati, rasaisupi nibbindati, phauṭṭhabbaisupi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. bāhiradukkhasuttaṃ

5. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā dukkhā| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bāhirānattasuttaṃ

6. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā| yadanattā taṃ  ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ajjhattānic‍cātītānāgatasuttaṃ

7. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ cakkhusmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ cakkhuṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa cakkhussa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| sautaṃ anic‍caṃ… ghānaṃ anic‍caṃ… jivhā anic‍cā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nāya! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītāya jivhāya anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ jivhaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nāya jivhāya nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| kāyau anic‍cau…pai॰… manau anic‍cau atītānāgatau; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ manasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ manaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa manassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ajjhattadukkhātītānāgatasuttaṃ

8. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ cakkhusmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ cakkhuṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa cakkhussa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| sautaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… jivhā  dukkhā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nāya! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītāya jivhāya anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ jivhaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nāya jivhāya nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| kāyau dukkhau…pai॰… manau dukkhau atītānāgatau; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ manasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ manaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa manassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ajjhattānattātītānāgatasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau  atītasmiṃ cakkhusmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ cakkhuṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa cakkhussa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| sautaṃ anattā…pai॰… ghānaṃ anattā…pai॰… jivhā anattā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nāya! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītāya jivhāya anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ jivhaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nāya jivhāya nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| kāyau anattā…pai॰… manau anattā atītānāgatau; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ manasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ manaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa manassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bāhirānic‍cātītānāgatasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītaisu rūpaisu  anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai rūpai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ rūpānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anic‍cā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītaisu dhammaisu anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai dhammai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ dhammānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. bāhiradukkhātītānāgatasuttaṃ

11. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītaisu rūpaisu anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai rūpai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ rūpānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti…pai॰ …| aikādasamaṃ|

12. bāhirānattātītānāgatasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītaisu rūpaisu anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai rūpai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ rūpānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītaisu dhammaisu anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai dhammai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ dhammānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

anic‍cavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā ca, tayau ajjhattabāhirā|

yadanic‍caina tayau vuttā, tai tai ajjhattabāhirāti||

2. yamakavaggau

1. paṭhamapubbaisambaudhasuttaṃ

13. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pubbaiva  mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva  satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau cakkhussa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau sautassa…pai॰… kau ghānassa… kau jivhāya… kau kāyassa… kau manassa  assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃ khau cakkhuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ cakkhussa assādau| yaṃ cakkhuṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ cakkhussa ādīnavau| yau cakkhusmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ cakkhussa nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ sautaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ ghānaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ jivhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ jivhāya assādau| yaṃ yā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) jivhā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ jivhāya ādīnavau| yau jivhāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ jivhāya nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ kāyaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ manaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ manassa assādau| yaṃ yau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) manau anic‍cau dukkhau vipariṇāmadhammau, ayaṃ manassa ādīnavau| yau manasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ manassa nissaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai  sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti sabbatthāpi aivamaiva itisaddaina saha dissati pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca  pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) aivamuparipi, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyapubbaisambaudhasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘pubbaiva  mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau rūpānaṃ assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau saddānaṃ…pai॰… kau gandhānaṃ… kau rasānaṃ… kau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… kau dhammānaṃ assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃ khau rūpai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ rūpānaṃ assādau| yaṃ rūpā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ rūpānaṃ ādīnavau| yau rūpaisu chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ rūpānaṃ  nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… yaṃ dhammai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ dhammānaṃ assādau| yaṃ dhammā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavau| yau dhammaisu chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ dhammānaṃ nissaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamaassādapariyaisanasuttaṃ

15. ‘‘cakkhussāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau cakkhussa assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā cakkhussa assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| cakkhussāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau cakkhussa ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā cakkhussa ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| cakkhussāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ cakkhussa nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā cakkhussa nissaraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ| sautassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… ghānassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… jivhāyāhaṃ bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau jivhāya assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā jivhāya assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| jivhāyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau jivhāya ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā jivhāya ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| jivhāyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ jivhāya nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā jivhāya nissaraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ| manassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau manassa assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā manassa assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| manassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau manassa ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā manassa ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| manassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ manassa nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā manassa nissaraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ…pai॰… pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaassādapariyaisanasuttaṃ

16. ‘‘rūpānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau rūpānaṃ assādau  tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā rūpānaṃ assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| rūpānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ  acariṃ| yau rūpānaṃ ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā rūpānaṃ ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| rūpānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ| saddānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… gandhānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… rasānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… phauṭṭhabbānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… dhammānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau dhammānaṃ assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā dhammānaṃ assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| dhammānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau dhammānaṃ ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā dhammānaṃ ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| dhammānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ dhammānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā dhammānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya  mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ…pai॰… pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamanaucaiassādasuttaṃ

17. ‘‘nau  caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā cakkhusmiṃ sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi cakkhussa assādau  tasmā sattā cakkhusmiṃ sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā cakkhusmiṃ nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi cakkhussa ādīnavau tasmā sattā cakkhusmiṃ nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā cakkhusmā nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi cakkhussa nissaraṇaṃ tasmā sattā cakkhusmā nissaranti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, sautassa assādau abhavissa… nau  caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ghānassa assādau abhavissa… nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, jivhāya assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā jivhāya sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi jivhāya assādau, tasmā sattā jivhāya sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, jivhāya ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā jivhāya nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi jivhāya ādīnavau, tasmā sattā jivhāya nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, jivhāya nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā jivhāya nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi jivhāya nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā jivhāya nissaranti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāyassa assādau abhavissa… nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, manassa assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā manasmiṃ sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi manassa assādau, tasmā sattā manasmiṃ sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, manassa ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā manasmiṃ nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi manassa ādīnavau, tasmā sattā manasmiṃ nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, manassa nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā manasmā nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi manassa nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā manasmā nissaranti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sattā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ  assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñaṃsu, naiva tāva, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visañ‍ñuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina vipariyādikataina (sī॰ pī॰), vipariyādikataina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) caitasā vihariṃsu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sattā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca  ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, atha, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visañ‍ñuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyanaucaiassādasuttaṃ

18. ‘‘nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā rūpaisu sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi rūpānaṃ assādau, tasmā sattā rūpaisu sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā rūpaisu nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā  ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi rūpānaṃ ādīnavau, tasmā sattā rūpaisu nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā rūpaihi nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi rūpānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā rūpaihi nissaranti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, saddānaṃ… gandhānaṃ… rasānaṃ… phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… dhammānaṃ assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā dhammaisu sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi dhammānaṃ assādau, tasmā sattā dhammaisu sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā dhammaisu nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi dhammānaṃ ādīnavau, tasmā sattā dhammaisu nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā dhammaihi nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi dhammānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā dhammaihi nissaranti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sattā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñaṃsu, naiva tāva, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā  pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visañ‍ñuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina caitasā vihariṃsu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sattā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau, ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau, nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, atha, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visañ‍ñuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamābhinandasuttaṃ

19. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau sautaṃ…pai॰… yau ghānaṃ…pai॰… yau jivhaṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau kāyaṃ…pai॰… yau manaṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmā’’ti vadāmi|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau sautaṃ…pai॰… yau ghānaṃ…pai॰… yau jivhaṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau kāyaṃ…pai॰… yau manaṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmā’’ti vadāmi| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyābhinandasuttaṃ

20. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpai abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau saddai…pai॰… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… dhammai abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmā’’ti vadāmi|

‘‘yau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpai nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau saddai…pai॰… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… dhammai nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmā’’ti vadāmi| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamadukkhuppādasuttaṃ

21. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau sautassa…pai॰… yau ghānassa… yau jivhāya… yau kāyassa… yau manassa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau sautassa… yau ghānassa… yau jivhāya… yau kāyassa… yau manassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyadukkhuppādasuttaṃ

22. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau saddānaṃ…pai॰… yau gandhānaṃ… yau rasānaṃ… yau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… yau dhammānaṃ uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau  saddānaṃ…pai॰… yau gandhānaṃ… yau rasānaṃ… yau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… yau  dhammānaṃ niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

yamakavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sambaudhaina  duvai vuttā, assādaina aparai duvai|

nau caitaina duvai vuttā, abhinandaina aparai duvai|

uppādaina duvai vuttā, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

3. sabbavaggau

1. sabbasuttaṃ

23. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sabbaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ? cakkhuñ‍caiva rūpā ca, sautañ‍ca sautañ‍caiva (?) aivamitarayugalaisupi saddā ca, ghānañ‍ca gandhā ca, jivhā ca rasā ca, kāyau ca phauṭṭhabbā ca, manau ca dhammā ca – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ| yau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamaitaṃ sabbaṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ sabbaṃ pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, tassa vācāvatthukamaivassa vācāvatthuraivassa (sī॰ pī॰), vācāvatthudaivassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰); puṭṭhau ca na sampāyaiyya, uttariñ‍ca vighātaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ, bhikkhavai, avisayasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pahānasuttaṃ

24. ‘‘sabbappahānāya sabbaṃ pahānāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sabbappahānāya dhammau? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, pahātabbaṃ, rūpā pahātabbā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahātabbaṃ, cakkhusamphassau pahātabbau, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ sautasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi pahātabbaṃ… yampidaṃ ghānasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi pahātabbaṃ| jivhā pahātabbā, rasā pahātabbā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahātabbaṃ, jivhāsamphassau pahātabbau, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā  tampi pahātabbaṃ| kāyau pahātabbau… manau pahātabbau, dhammā pahātabbā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  pahātabbaṃ, manausamphassau pahātabbau, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi pahātabbaṃ| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbappahānāya dhammau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. abhiñ‍ñāpariñ‍ñāpahānasuttaṃ

25. ‘‘sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahānāya vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahānāya dhammau? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ, rūpā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ, cakkhusamphassau abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbau, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbā, rasā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ, jivhāsamphassau abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbau, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ| kāyau abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbau… manau abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbau, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā  pahātabbā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ, manausamphassau abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbau, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā  tampi abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahātabbaṃ| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahānāya dhammau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamaaparijānanasuttaṃ

26. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| rūpai anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| cakkhusamphassaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya…pai॰… jivhaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| rasai…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāsamphassaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| kāyaṃ…pai॰… manaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| dhammai…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ  appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘sabbañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau  dukkhakkhayāya| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| rūpai abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| cakkhusamphassaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya…pai॰…  jivhaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| rasai…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāsamphassaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| kāyaṃ…pai॰… manaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| dhammai…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaaparijānanasuttaṃ

27. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya? yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cakkhu, yai  ca rūpā, yañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā…pai॰… yā  ca jivhā, yai ca rasā, yañ‍ca jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā; yau ca kāyau, yai ca phauṭṭhabbā, yañ‍ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā; yau ca manau, yai ca dhammā, yañ‍ca manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya? yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cakkhu, yai ca rūpā, yañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā…pai॰… yā ca jivhā, yai ca rasā, yañ‍ca jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā; yau ca kāyau, yai ca phauṭṭhabbā, yañ‍ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā; yau ca manau, yai ca dhammā, yañ‍ca manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā – idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ādittasuttaṃ

28. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā gayāyaṃ viharati gayāsīsai saddhiṃ bhikkhusahassaina| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādittaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ ādittaṃ? cakkhu cakkhuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, ādittaṃ, rūpā ādittā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ādittaṃ, cakkhusamphassau ādittau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ  sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi ādittaṃ| kaina ādittaṃ? ‘rāgagginā, dausagginā, mauhagginā ādittaṃ, jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi āditta’nti vadāmi…pai॰… jivhā ādittā, rasā ādittā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ādittaṃ, jivhāsamphassau ādittau| yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi ādittaṃ| kaina ādittaṃ? ‘rāgagginā, dausagginā, mauhagginā ādittaṃ, jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi āditta’nti vadāmi…pai॰… manau ādittau, dhammā ādittā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ ādittaṃ, manausamphassau ādittau| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi ādittaṃ| kaina ādittaṃ? ‘rāgagginā, dausagginā, mauhagginā ādittaṃ, jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi āditta’nti vadāmi| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati  vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati  …pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā  vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanduṃ| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai tassa bhikkhusahassassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsūti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. addhabhūtasuttaṃ

29. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, addhabhūtaṃ andhabhūtaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ addhabhūtaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, addhabhūtaṃ, rūpā addhabhūtā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ addhabhūtaṃ, cakkhusamphassau addhabhūtau, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi addhabhūtaṃ| kaina addhabhūtaṃ? ‘jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi addhabhūta’nti vadāmi…pai॰… jivhā addhabhūtā, rasā addhabhūtā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ addhabhūtaṃ, jivhāsamphassau addhabhūtau, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi addhabhūtaṃ| kaina addhabhūtaṃ? ‘jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi addhabhūta’nti vadāmi| kāyau addhabhūtau…pai॰… manau addhabhūtau, dhammā addhabhūtā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ addhabhūtaṃ, manausamphassau addhabhūtau, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā  tampi addhabhūtaṃ| kaina addhabhūtaṃ? ‘jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi addhabhūta’nti vadāmi| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati…pai॰…  yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ ‘vimutta’miti ñāṇaṃ hauti, ‘khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. samugghātasāruppasuttaṃ

30. ‘‘sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasāruppaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ  daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmīti| katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasāruppā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhuṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhusmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhutau na mañ‍ñati, cakkhuṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| rūpai na mañ‍ñati, rūpaisu na mañ‍ñati, rūpatau na mañ‍ñati, rūpā maiti na mañ‍ñati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇatau na mañ‍ñati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| cakkhusamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhusamphassasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhusamphassatau na mañ‍ñati, cakkhusamphassau maiti na mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati…pai॰… jivhaṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāya na mañ‍ñati, jivhātau na mañ‍ñati, jivhā maiti na mañ‍ñati| rasai na mañ‍ñati, rasaisu na mañ‍ñati, rasatau na mañ‍ñati, rasā maiti na mañ‍ñati| jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇatau na mañ‍ñati, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| jivhāsamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāsamphassasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāsamphassatau na mañ‍ñati, jivhāsamphassau maiti na mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati…pai॰… manaṃ na mañ‍ñati, manasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, manatau na mañ‍ñati, manau maiti na mañ‍ñati| dhammai na mañ‍ñati, dhammaisu na mañ‍ñati, dhammatau na mañ‍ñati, dhammā maiti  na mañ‍ñati| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñati, manauviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, manauviñ‍ñāṇatau na mañ‍ñati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| manausamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñati, manausamphassasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, manausamphassatau na mañ‍ñati, manausamphassau maiti na mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na  mañ‍ñati| sabbaṃ na mañ‍ñati, sabbasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, sabbatau na mañ‍ñati, sabbaṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| sau aivaṃ amañ‍ñamānau  na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| anupādiyaṃ na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasāruppā paṭipadā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasamugghātasappāyasuttaṃ

31. ‘‘sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasappāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasappāyā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhuṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhusmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhutau na mañ‍ñati, cakkhuṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| rūpai na mañ‍ñati…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñati, cakkhusamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| yañhi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñati, yasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, yatau mañ‍ñati, yaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau bhavamaivābhinandati…pai॰… jivhaṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāya na mañ‍ñati, jivhātau na mañ‍ñati, jivhā maiti na mañ‍ñati| rasai na mañ‍ñati…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñati, jivhāsamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ  sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ  vā tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| yañhi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñati, yasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, yatau mañ‍ñati, yaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau bhavamaivābhinandati…pai॰… manaṃ na mañ‍ñati, manasmiṃ na mañ‍ñati, manatau na mañ‍ñati, manau maiti na mañ‍ñati| dhammai na mañ‍ñati…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñati, manausamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā  uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| yañhi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñati, yasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, yatau mañ‍ñati, yaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau bhavamaivābhinandati| yāvatā, bhikkhavai, khandhadhātuāyatanaṃ tampi na mañ‍ñati, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñati, tataupi na mañ‍ñati, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñati| sau aivaṃ amañ‍ñamānau na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| anupādiyaṃ na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasappāyā paṭipadā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasamugghātasappāyasuttaṃ

32. ‘‘sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasappāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasappāyā  paṭipadā?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, cakkhuṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ bhantai’’|

‘‘rūpā…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘yampidaṃ  cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘rasā… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāsamphassau…pai॰… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vāti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’|

‘‘yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ  nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ  passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… jivhāyapi nibbindati, rasaisupi…pai॰… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, manausamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, sabbamañ‍ñitasamugghātasappāyā paṭipadā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sabbavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sabbañ‍ca dvaipi pahānā, parijānāparai duvai|

ādittaṃ addhabhūtañ‍ca, sāruppā dvai ca sappāyā|

vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

4. jātidhammavaggau

1-10. jātidhammādisuttadasakaṃ

33. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau…pai॰… ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, jātidhammaṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ jātidhammaṃ? cakkhu,  bhikkhavai, jātidhammaṃ| rūpā… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau jātidhammau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi jātidhammaṃ…pai॰… jivhā… rasā… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāsamphassau… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi jātidhammaṃ| kāyau…pai॰.. manau jātidhammau, dhammā jātidhammā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jātidhammaṃ, manausamphassau jātidhammau| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi jātidhammaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi… cakkhusamphassaipi…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

34. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, jarādhammaṃ…pai॰… saṃkhittaṃ| dutiyaṃ|

35. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, byādhidhammaṃ…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

36. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, maraṇadhammaṃ…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

37. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, saukadhammaṃ…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

38. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃkilaisikadhammaṃ…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

39. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, khayadhammaṃ…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

40. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, vayadhammaṃ…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

41. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, samudayadhammaṃ…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

42. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, niraudhadhammaṃ…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

jātidhammavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

jātijarābyādhimaraṇaṃ, saukau ca saṃkilaisikaṃ|

khayavayasamudayaṃ, niraudhadhammaina tai dasāti||

5. sabbaanic‍cavaggau

1-9. anic‍cādisuttanavakaṃ

43. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau…pai॰… ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ anic‍caṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, rūpā anic‍cā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, cakkhusamphassau anic‍cau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā, rasā anic‍cā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, jivhāsamphassau anic‍cau| yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ| kāyau anic‍cau…pai॰… manau anic‍cau, dhammā anic‍cā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, manausamphassau anic‍cau| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, manausamphassaipi nibbindati, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti  ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

44. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

45. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

46. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

47. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

48. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, pahātabbaṃ…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

49. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbaṃ…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

50. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāpariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

51. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, upaddutaṃ…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. upassaṭṭhasuttaṃ

52. ‘‘sabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, upassaṭṭhaṃ upasaṭṭhaṃ (ka॰)| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ upassaṭṭhaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, upassaṭṭhaṃ, rūpā upassaṭṭhā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upassaṭṭhaṃ, cakkhusamphassau upassaṭṭhau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi upassaṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… jivhā upassaṭṭhā, rasā upassaṭṭhā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upassaṭṭhaṃ, jivhāsamphassau upassaṭṭhau| yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi upassaṭṭhaṃ| kāyau upassaṭṭhau… manau upassaṭṭhau, dhammā upassaṭṭhā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ upassaṭṭhaṃ, manausamphassau upassaṭṭhau| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ  vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi upassaṭṭhaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, manausamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sabbaanic‍cavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

anic‍caṃ  dukkhaṃ anattā, abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

pahātabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ, abhiñ‍ñaiyyapariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), abhiñ‍ñātaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰)|

upaddutaṃ upassaṭṭhaṃ, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

saḷāyatanavaggai paṭhamapaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa vagguddānaṃ –

anic‍cavaggaṃ  yamakaṃ, sabbaṃ vaggaṃ jātidhammaṃ|

anic‍cavaggaina pañ‍ñāsaṃ, pañ‍camau taina pavuc‍catīti||

6. avij‍jāvaggau

1. avij‍jāpahānasuttaṃ

53. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anic‍catau jānatau passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| rūpai anic‍catau jānatau passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… manaṃ anic‍catau jānatau passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| dhammai … manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā  uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. saṃyaujanapahānasuttaṃ

54. ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau, saṃyaujanā pahīyantī’’ti? ‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anic‍catau jānatau passatau saṃyaujanā pahīyanti| rūpai…  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau saṃyaujanā pahīyanti| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… manaṃ… dhammai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau saṃyaujanā pahīyanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau saṃyaujanā pahīyantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃyaujanasamugghātasuttaṃ

55. ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau saṃyaujanā samugghātaṃ gacchantī’’ti? ‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anattatau jānatau passatau saṃyaujanā  samugghātaṃ gacchanti| rūpai anattatau… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattatau jānatau passatau saṃyaujanā samugghātaṃ gacchanti| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… manaṃ… dhammai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattatau jānatau passatau saṃyaujanā samugghātaṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau saṃyaujanā samugghātaṃ gacchantī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. āsavapahānasuttaṃ

56. ‘‘kathaṃ  nu khau, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau āsavā pahīyantī’’ti…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. āsavasamugghātasuttaṃ

57. ‘‘kathaṃ  nu khau, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau āsavā samugghātaṃ gacchantī’’ti…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. anusayapahānasuttaṃ

58. ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau…pai॰…  anusayā pahīyantī’’ti…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anusayasamugghātasuttaṃ

59. ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau…pai॰… anusayā samugghātaṃ gacchantī’’ti? ‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anattatau jānatau passatau anusayā samugghātaṃ gacchanti…pai॰… sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… manaṃ… dhammai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattatau jānatau passatau anusayā samugghātaṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anusayā samugghātaṃ gacchantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sabbupādānapariñ‍ñāsuttaṃ

60. ‘‘sabbupādānapariñ‍ñāya  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sabbupādānapariñ‍ñāya dhammau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimaukkhā vimaukkhaṃ (ka॰), vimaukkha (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ‘pariñ‍ñātaṃ mai upādāna’nti pajānāti| sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati… ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, manausamphassaipi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimaukkhā ‘pariñ‍ñātaṃ mai upādāna’nti  pajānāti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbupādānapariñ‍ñāya dhammau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasabbupādānapariyādānasuttaṃ

61. ‘‘sabbupādānapariyādānāya vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sabbupādānapariyādānāya dhammau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimaukkhā ‘pariyādin‍naṃ sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati dantaja-nakārainaiva mai upādāna’nti pajānāti…pai॰…  jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati  manausamphassaipi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimaukkhā ‘pariyādin‍naṃ mai upādāna’nti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbupādānapariyādānāya dhammau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasabbupādānapariyādānasuttaṃ

62. ‘‘sabbupādānapariyādānāya vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sabbupādānapariyādānāya dhammau’’?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘rūpā…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘cakkhusamphassau  nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau… dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ  passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… jivhāyapi nibbindati, rasaisupi nibbindati, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaipi  nibbindati, jivhāsamphassaipi nibbindati, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, manausamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ  vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sabbupādānapariyādānāya dhammau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

avij‍jāvaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

avij‍jā saṃyaujanā dvai, āsavaina duvai vuttā|

anusayā aparai dvai, pariñ‍ñā dvai pariyādin‍naṃ|

vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

7. migajālavaggau

1. paṭhamamigajālasuttaṃ

63. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā migajālau yaina bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā migajālau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘aikavihārī, aikavihārī’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā  nu khau, bhantai, aikavihārī hauti, kittāvatā ca pana sadutiyavihārī hautī’’ti?

‘‘santi khau, migajāla, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya  tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī nandi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| nandiyā sati sārāgau hauti; sārāgai sati saṃyaugau hauti| nandisaṃyaujanasaṃyuttau khau, migajāla, bhikkhu sadutiyavihārīti vuc‍cati| santi…pai॰… santi khau, migajāla, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati  nandī| nandiyā sati sārāgau hauti; sārāgai sati saṃyaugau hauti| nandisaṃyaujanasaṃyuttau khau, migajāla, bhikkhu sadutiyavihārīti vuc‍cati| aivaṃvihārī ca, migajāla, bhikkhu kiñ‍cāpi arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati  appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni manussarāhasaiyyakāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni; atha khau sadutiyavihārīti vuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? taṇhā hissa dutiyā, sāssa appahīnā| tasmā sadutiyavihārī’’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘santi ca khau, migajāla, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nandī nirujjhati| nandiyā asati sārāgau na hauti; sārāgai asati saṃyaugau  na hauti| nandisaṃyaujanavisaṃyuttau khau, migajāla, bhikkhu aikavihārīti vuc‍cati…pai॰… santi ca khau, migajāla, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi ca khau, migajāla, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nandī nirujjhati| nandiyā asati sārāgau na hauti; sārāgai asati saṃyaugau na hauti| nandisaṃyaujanavippayuttau khau, migajāla, bhikkhu aikavihārīti vuc‍cati| aivaṃvihārī ca, migajāla, bhikkhu kiñ‍cāpi gāmantai viharati ākiṇṇau bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi upāsikāhi rājūhi rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| atha khau aikavihārīti vuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? taṇhā hissa dutiyā, sāssa pahīnā| tasmā aikavihārīti vuc‍catī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyamigajālasuttaṃ

64. atha  khau āyasmā migajālau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā migajālau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘santi  khau, migajāla, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| nandisamudayā dukkhasamudayau, migajālāti vadāmi…pai॰… santi ca khau, migajāla, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi ca khau, migajāla, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| nandisamudayā dukkhasamudayau, migajālāti vadāmi|

‘‘santi ca khau, migajāla, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya  tiṭṭhatau nandī nirujjhati| nandiniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau, migajālāti vadāmi…pai॰… santi ca khau, migajāla, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā iṭṭhā kantā…pai॰… santi ca khau, migajāla, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nandī nirujjhati| nandiniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau, migajālāti vadāmī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā migajālau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā migajālau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharatau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā migajālau arahataṃ ahausīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamasamiddhimārapañhāsuttaṃ

65. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau āyasmā samiddhi yaina bhagavā…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘mārau, mārau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, mārau vā assa mārapañ‍ñatti vā’’ti?

‘‘yattha khau, samiddhi, atthi  cakkhu, atthi rūpā, atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| atthi sautaṃ, atthi saddā, atthi sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi sautaviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| atthi ghānaṃ, atthi gandhā, atthi ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| atthi jivhā, atthi rasā, atthi jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| atthi kāyau, atthi phauṭṭhabbā, atthi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| atthi manau, atthi dhammā, atthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā|

‘‘yattha ca khau, samiddhi, natthi cakkhu, natthi rūpā, natthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, natthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, natthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| natthi sautaṃ…pai॰… natthi ghānaṃ…pai॰… natthi jivhā, natthi rasā, natthi jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, natthi jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, natthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā| natthi kāyau…pai॰…| natthi manau, natthi dhammā, natthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, natthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, natthi tattha mārau vā mārapañ‍ñatti vā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samiddhisattapañhāsuttaṃ

66. ‘‘‘sattau, sattau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sattau vā assa sattapañ‍ñatti vā’’ti…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. samiddhidukkhapañhāsuttaṃ

67. ‘‘‘dukkhaṃ, dukkha’nti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dukkhaṃ vā assa dukkhapañ‍ñatti vā’’ti…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samiddhilaukapañhāsuttaṃ

68. ‘‘‘laukau, laukau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, laukau vā assa laukapañ‍ñatti vā’’ti? yattha khau, samiddhi, atthi cakkhu, atthi rūpā, atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha laukau vā laukapañ‍ñatti vāti…pai॰… atthi jivhā…pai॰… atthi  manau, atthi dhammā, atthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, atthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, atthi tattha laukau vā laukapañ‍ñatti vā|

‘‘yattha ca khau, samiddhi, natthi cakkhu, natthi rūpā, natthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, natthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, natthi tattha laukau vā laukapañ‍ñatti vā…pai॰… natthi jivhā…pai॰… natthi manau, natthi dhammā, natthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, natthi manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbā dhammā, natthi tattha laukau vā laukapañ‍ñatti vā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. upasainaāsīvisasuttaṃ

69. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca upasainau rājagahai viharanti sītavanai sappasauṇḍikapabbhārai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau upasainassa kāyai āsīvisau patitau  hauti| atha khau āyasmā upasainau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha mai, āvusau, imaṃ kāyaṃ mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā bahiddhā nīharatha| purāyaṃ kāyau idhaiva vikirati; saiyyathāpi bhusamuṭṭhī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ upasainaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau pana mayaṃ passāma āyasmatau upasainassa kāyassa vā añ‍ñathattaṃ indriyānaṃ vā vipariṇāmaṃ| atha ca panāyasmā upasainau aivamāha – ‘aitha mai, āvusau, imaṃ kāyaṃ mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā bahiddhā nīharatha| purāyaṃ kāyau idhaiva vikirati; saiyyathāpi bhusamuṭṭhī’’’ti| ‘‘yassa nūna, āvusau sāriputta, aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ cakkhūti vā mama cakkhūti vā…pai॰… ahaṃ jivhāti vā mama  jivhāti vā… ahaṃ manauti vā mama manauti vā’| tassa, āvusau sāriputta, siyā kāyassa vā añ‍ñathattaṃ indriyānaṃ vā vipariṇāmau| mayhañ‍ca khau, āvusau sāriputta, na aivaṃ hauti  – ‘ahaṃ cakkhūti vā mama cakkhūti vā…pai॰… ahaṃ jivhāti vā mama jivhāti vā…pai॰… ahaṃ manauti vā mama manauti vā’| tassa mayhañ‍ca khau, āvusau sāriputta, kiṃ kāyassa vā añ‍ñathattaṃ bhavissati, indriyānaṃ vā vipariṇāmau’’ti!

‘‘tathā hi panāyasmatau upasainassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayau susamūhatau| tasmā āyasmatau upasainassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ cakkhūti vā mama cakkhūti vā…pai॰… ahaṃ jivhāti vā mama jivhāti vā…pai॰… ahaṃ manauti vā mama manauti vā’’’ti| atha  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau upasainassa kāyaṃ mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā bahiddhā nīhariṃsu| atha khau āyasmatau upasainassa kāyau tatthaiva vikiri; saiyyathāpi bhusamuṭṭhīti| sattamaṃ|

8. upavāṇasandiṭṭhikasuttaṃ

70. atha khau āyasmā upavāṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikau dhammau, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti, akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘idha pana, upavāṇa, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rūpappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti rūparāgappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| santañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rūpappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti rūparāgappaṭisaṃvaidī  ca| santañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| aivampi khau, upavāṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā rasappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti rasarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| santañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rasaisu rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rasaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā rasappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti rasarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī  ca| santañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rasaisu  rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rasaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| aivampi khau, upavāṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| santañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| santañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| aivampi khau, upavāṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘idha pana, upavāṇa, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rūpappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti, nau ca rūparāgappaṭisaṃvaidī| asantañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā rūpappaṭisaṃvaidīhi khau hauti, nau ca rūparāgappaṭisaṃvaidī| asantañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rūpaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| aivampi khau, upavāṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti, akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā rasappaṭisaṃvaidīhi khau hauti, nau ca rasarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī| asantañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ rasaisu rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rasaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya dhammappaṭisaṃvaidīhi khau hauti, nau ca dhammarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī| asantañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, upavāṇa, bhikkhu manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya dhammappaṭisaṃvaidīhi khau hauti, nau ca dhammarāgappaṭisaṃvaidī| asantañ‍ca ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu rāgau’ti pajānāti| aivampi khau, upavāṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti, akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamachaphassāyatanasuttaṃ

71. ‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| avusitaṃ  taina brahmacariyaṃ, ārakā sau imasmā dhammavinayā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitthāhaṃ, bhantai, anassasaṃ anassasiṃ (sī॰), anassāsaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), anassāsiṃ (pī॰)| ahañhi, bhantai, chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhikkhu, cakkhuṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhu, aittha ca tai, bhikkhu, cakkhu ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ bhavissati| aisaivantau dukkhassa…pai॰… jivhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhu, aittha ca tai, bhikkhu, jivhā ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ bhavissati| aisaivantau dukkhassa…pai॰… manaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhu, aittha ca tai, bhikkhu, manau ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti  aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ bhavissati| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyachaphassāyatanasuttaṃ

72. ‘‘yau  hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| avusitaṃ taina brahmacariyaṃ, ārakā sau imasmā dhammavinayā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitthāhaṃ, bhantai, anassasaṃ panassasaṃ| ahañhi, bhantai, chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhikkhu, cakkhuṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhu, aittha ca tai, bhikkhu, cakkhu ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ bhavissati| aivaṃ tai aitaṃ paṭhamaṃ phassāyatanaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati  āyatiṃ apunabbhavāya…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhu, aittha ca tai, bhikkhu, jivhā ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ bhavissati| aivaṃ tai aitaṃ catutthaṃ phassāyatanaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati āyatiṃ apunabbhavāya…pai॰…|

‘‘manaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhu, aittha ca tai, bhikkhu, manau ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ bhavissati| aivaṃ tai aitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ phassāyatanaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati āyatiṃ apunabbhavāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. tatiyachaphassāyatanasuttaṃ

73. ‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ  samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| avusitaṃ taina brahmacariyaṃ, ārakā sau imasmā dhammavinayā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitthāhaṃ, bhantai, anassasaṃ panassasaṃ| ahañhi, bhantai, chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca  atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhikkhu, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, sautasmimpi nibbindati, ghānasmimpi nibbindati, jivhāyapi nibbindati, kāyasmimpi nibbindati, manasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

migajālavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

migajālaina  dvai vuttā, cattārau ca samiddhinā|

upasainau upavāṇau, chaphassāyatanikā tayauti||

8. gilānavaggau

1. paṭhamagilānasuttaṃ

74. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘amukasmiṃ, bhantai, vihārai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu navau appañ‍ñātau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā navavādañ‍ca sutvā gilānavādañ‍ca, ‘‘appañ‍ñātau bhikkhū’’ti iti viditvā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna mañ‍cakai samadhausi samañ‍causi (sī॰), samataisi (syā॰ kaṃ॰), samañ‍caupi (pī॰)| atha khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā tvaṃ mañ‍cakai samadhausi| santimāni āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni, tatthāhaṃ nisīdissāmī’’ti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, bhikkhu, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti nau abhik‍kamanti, paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau abhik‍kamau’’ti?

‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti nau paṭik‍kamanti, abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

‘‘kac‍ci tai, bhikkhu, na kiñ‍ci kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, na kauci vippaṭisārau’’ti?

‘‘taggha mai, bhantai, anappakaṃ  kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, anappakau vippaṭisārau’’ti|

‘‘kac‍ci  pana taṃ tvaṃ (sī॰), tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhikkhu, attā sīlatau upavadatī’’ti?

‘‘na khau maṃ, bhantai, attā sīlatau upavadatī’’ti nau haitaṃ bhantai (pī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘nau cai kira tai, bhikkhu, attā sīlatau upavadati, atha kiñ‍ca atha kismiñ‍ca (sī॰), atha bhikkhu kismiñ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) tai kuk‍kuc‍caṃ kau ca vippaṭisārau’’ti?

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhantai, sīlavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘nau cai kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlavisuddhatthaṃ mayā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi, atha kimatthaṃ carahi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsī’’ti?

‘‘rāgavirāgatthaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, rāgavirāgatthaṃ mayā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi| rāgavirāgatthau hi, bhikkhu, mayā dhammau daisitau| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi bhikkhu, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ…pai॰… sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, sautasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandi| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai tassa bhikkhunau virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyagilānasuttaṃ

75. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘amukasmiṃ, bhantai, vihārai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu navau appañ‍ñātau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā navavādañ‍ca sutvā gilānavādañ‍ca, ‘‘appañ‍ñātau bhikkhū’’ti iti viditvā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna mañ‍cakai samadhausi| atha khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā tvaṃ mañ‍cakai samadhausi| santimāni āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni, tatthāhaṃ nisīdissāmī’’ti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, bhikkhu, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti nau abhik‍kamanti, paṭik‍kamausānaṃ  pañ‍ñāyati nau abhik‍kamau’’ti?

‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ…pai॰… na khau maṃ mai (sabbattha), bhantai, attā sīlatau upavadatī’’ti|

‘‘nau cai kira tai, bhikkhu, attā sīlatau upavadati, atha kiñ‍ca tai kuk‍kuc‍caṃ kau ca vippaṭisārau’’ti?

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhantai, sīlavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘nau  cai kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlavisuddhatthaṃ mayā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi, atha  kimatthaṃ carahi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsī’’ti?

‘‘anupādāparinibbānatthaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, anupādāparinibbānatthaṃ mayā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi| anupādāparinibbānatthau hi, bhikkhu, mayā dhammau daisitau|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhikkhu, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ…pai॰… sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi… manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi… manausamphassaipi nibbindati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandi| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai tassa bhikkhussa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cīti vimuc‍catīti (sabbattha)| dutiyaṃ|

3. rādhaanic‍casuttaṃ

76. atha  khau āyasmā rādhau…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, rādha, anic‍caṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, rādha, anic‍caṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau? cakkhu anic‍caṃ, rūpā anic‍cā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā… kāyau… manau anic‍cau| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yaṃ  khau, rādha, anic‍caṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. rādhadukkhasuttaṃ

77. ‘‘yaṃ khau, rādha, dukkhaṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, rādha, dukkhaṃ? cakkhu khau, rādha, dukkhaṃ| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| rūpā… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassa…pai॰… adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau dukkhau… dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ  sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yaṃ khau, rādha, dukkhaṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. rādhaanattasuttaṃ

78. ‘‘yau khau, rādha, anattā tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| kau ca, rādha, anattā? cakkhu khau, rādha, anattā| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| rūpā… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manau anattā… dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattā| tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yau khau, rādha, anattā tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamaavij‍jāpahānasuttaṃ

79. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘atthi khau, bhikkhu, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘avij‍jā  khau, bhikkhu, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā  pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?
