‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anic‍catau jānatau passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| rūpai… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ, cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati…pai॰… manaṃ anic‍catau jānatau passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| dhammai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaavij‍jāpahānasuttaṃ

80. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘atthi khau, bhikkhu, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘avij‍jā khau, bhikkhu, aikau dhammau yassa pahānā bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – ‘sabbai dhammā  nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| sau sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya sabbaṃ dhammaṃ parijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ pariñ‍ñāya sabbanimittāni añ‍ñatau passati, cakkhuṃ añ‍ñatau passati, rūpai… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi añ‍ñatau passati…pai॰… manaṃ añ‍ñatau passati, dhammai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi añ‍ñatau passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sambahulabhikkhusuttaṃ

81. atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ  – ‘‘idha nau, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā amhai aivaṃ pucchanti – ‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā mayaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākarauma – ‘dukkhassa khau, āvusau, pariñ‍ñatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| kac‍ci mayaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaramānā vuttavādinau caiva bhagavatau hauma, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarauma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti?

‘‘taggha tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaramānā vuttavādinau caiva mai hautha, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhatha, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarautha, na ca  kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati| dukkhassa hi, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñatthaṃ mayi brahmacariyaṃ vussati| sacai pana vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaṃ pana taṃ, āvusau, dukkhaṃ, yassa pariñ‍ñāya samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘cakkhu khau, āvusau, dukkhaṃ, tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| rūpā…pai॰… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ| tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati…pai॰… manau dukkhau…pai॰… yampidaṃ  manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ| tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| idaṃ khau taṃ, āvusau, dukkhaṃ, tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| aivaṃ  puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. laukapañhāsuttaṃ

82. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘laukau, laukau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, laukauti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘‘luj‍jatī’ti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā laukauti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca luj‍jati? cakkhu khau, bhikkhu, luj‍jati| rūpā luj‍janti, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ luj‍jati, cakkhusamphassau luj‍jati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi luj‍jati…pai॰… jivhā luj‍jati…pai॰… manau luj‍jati, dhammā luj‍janti, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ luj‍jati, manausamphassau luj‍jati, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi luj‍jati| luj‍jatīti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā laukauti vuc‍catī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. phaggunapañhāsuttaṃ

83. atha khau āyasmā phaggunau…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā phaggunau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, taṃ cakkhu, yaina cakkhunā atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivaṭṭai pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya…pai॰… atthi nu khau, bhantai, sā jivhā, yāya jivhāya atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivaṭṭai pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya…pai॰… atthi nu khau sau, bhantai, manau, yaina manaina atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivaṭṭai pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘natthi khau taṃ, phagguna, cakkhu, yaina cakkhunā atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivaṭṭai pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya  …pai॰… natthi khau sā, phagguna, jivhā, yāya jivhāya atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivaṭṭai pañ‍ñāpayamānau  pañ‍ñāpaiyya…pai॰… natthi  khau sau, phagguna, manau, yaina manaina atītai buddhai parinibbutai chin‍napapañ‍cai chin‍navaṭumai pariyādin‍navaṭṭai sabbadukkhavītivaṭṭai pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

gilānavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

gilānaina duvai vuttā, rādhaina aparai tayau|

avij‍jāya ca dvai vuttā, bhikkhu laukau ca phaggunauti||

9. chan‍navaggau

1. palaukadhammasuttaṃ

84. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘laukau, laukau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, laukauti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ khau, ānanda, palaukadhammaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ariyassa vinayai laukau| kiñ‍ca, ānanda, palaukadhammaṃ? cakkhu khau, ānanda, palaukadhammaṃ, rūpā palaukadhammā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ palaukadhammaṃ, cakkhusamphassau palaukadhammau, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… tampi palaukadhammaṃ…pai॰… jivhā palaukadhammā, rasā palaukadhammā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ palaukadhammaṃ, jivhāsamphassau palaukadhammau, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… tampi palaukadhammaṃ…pai॰…  manau palaukadhammau, dhammā palaukadhammā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ palaukadhammaṃ, manausamphassau palaukadhammau, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi palaukadhammaṃ| yaṃ khau, ānanda, palaukadhammaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ariyassa vinayai laukau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. suñ‍ñatalaukasuttaṃ

85. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘suñ‍ñau laukau, suñ‍ñau laukau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, suñ‍ñau laukauti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘yasmā ca khau, ānanda, suñ‍ñaṃ  attaina vā attaniyaina vā tasmā suñ‍ñau laukauti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca, ānanda, suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā? cakkhu khau, ānanda, suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā| rūpā suñ‍ñā attaina vā attaniyaina vā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā, cakkhusamphassau suñ‍ñau attaina vā attaniyaina vā…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā| yasmā ca khau, ānanda, suñ‍ñaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā, tasmā suñ‍ñau laukauti vuc‍catī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃkhittadhammasuttaṃ

86. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ānanda, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ  vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ  vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāsamphassau…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, ānanda, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. chan‍nasuttaṃ

87. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahācundau āyasmā ca chan‍nau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai viharanti| taina khau pana samayaina yaina āyasmā chan‍nau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā  vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahācundau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahācundaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāmāvusau cunda, yaināyasmā chan‍nau tainupasaṅkamissāma gilānapucchakā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā mahācundau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausi|

atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahācundau yaināyasmā chan‍nau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃsu| nisaj‍ja khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, āvusau chan‍na, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti nau abhik‍kamanti, paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau abhik‍kamau’’ti?

‘‘na  mai, āvusau sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti nau paṭik‍kamanti, abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, balavā purisau tiṇhaina sikharaina khaggaina (ka॰) muddhani muddhānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) abhimatthaiyya abhimanthaiyya (sī॰); aivamaiva khau, āvusau, adhimattā vātā muddhani muddhānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ūhananti upahananti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), uhananti (ka॰)| na mai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ…pai॰… nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, balavā purisau daḷhaina varattakkhaṇḍaina sīsai sīsavaiṭhaṃ dadaiyya; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, adhimattā sīsai sīsavaidanā| na mai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ…pai॰… nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā tiṇhaina gauvikantanaina kucchiṃ parikantaiyya; aivamaiva khau adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti| na mai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ…pai॰… nau paṭik‍kamau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, dvai balavantau purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpaiyyuṃ  samparitāpaiyyuṃ; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, adhimattau kāyasmiṃ ḍāhau| na mai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ, bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti nau paṭik‍kamanti, abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau| satthaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, āharissāmi, nāvakaṅkhāmi nāpi kaṅkhāmi (ka॰) jīvita’’nti|

‘‘mā āyasmā chan‍nau satthaṃ āharaisi| yāpaitāyasmā chan‍nau, yāpaintaṃ mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ icchāma| sacai āyasmatau chan‍nassa natthi sappāyāni bhaujanāni, ahaṃ āyasmatau chan‍nassa sappāyāni bhaujanāni pariyaisissāmi| sacai āyasmatau chan‍nassa natthi sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, ahaṃ āyasmatau chan‍nassa sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni pariyaisissāmi| sacai āyasmatau chan‍nassa natthi patirūpā upaṭṭhākā, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ upaṭṭhahissāmi| mā āyasmā chan‍nau satthaṃ āharaisi| yāpaitāyasmā chan‍nau, yāpaintaṃ mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ icchāmā’’ti|

‘‘na mai, āvusau sāriputta, natthi sappāyāni bhaujanāni; atthi mai sappāyāni  bhaujanāni| napi mai natthi sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni; atthi mai sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni| napi mai natthi patirūpā upaṭṭhākā; atthi mai patirūpā upaṭṭhākā| api ca mai, āvusau, satthā pariciṇṇau dīgharattaṃ manāpainaiva, nau amanāpaina| aitañhi, āvusau, sāvakassa patirūpaṃ yaṃ satthāraṃ  paricaraiyya manāpainaiva, nau amanāpaina| ‘anupavaj‍jaṃ taṃ anupavaj‍jaṃ (bahūsu) chan‍nau bhikkhu satthaṃ āharissatī’ti – aivamaitaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, dhāraihī’’ti|

‘‘pucchaiyyāma  mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ kañ‍cidaiva kiñ‍cidaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) daisaṃ, sacai āyasmā chan‍nau aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘pucchāvusau  sāriputta, sutvā vaidissāmā’’ti|

‘‘cakkhuṃ, āvusau chan‍na, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasi…pai॰… jivhaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasi…pai॰… manaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhuṃ, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmi…pai॰… jivhaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ  mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmi…pai॰… manaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmī’’ti|

‘‘cakkhusmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu kiṃ disvā kiṃ abhiñ‍ñāya cakkhuṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasi… jivhāya, āvusau chan‍na, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇai jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu kiṃ disvā kiṃ abhiñ‍ñāya jivhaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasi… manasmiṃ, āvusau chan‍na, manauviñ‍ñāṇai manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu kiṃ disvā kiṃ abhiñ‍ñāya manaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhusmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu niraudhaṃ disvā niraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya cakkhuṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti  samanupassāmi…pai॰… jivhāya, āvusau sāriputta, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇai jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu niraudhaṃ disvā niraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya jivhaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai  dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti  samanupassāmi…pai॰… manasmiṃ, āvusau sāriputta, manauviñ‍ñāṇai manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu niraudhaṃ disvā niraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya manaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassāmī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā mahācundau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tasmātiha, āvusau chan‍na, idampi tassa bhagavatau sāsanaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ – ‘nissitassa calitaṃ, anissitassa calitaṃ natthi| calitai asati passaddhi hauti| passaddhiyā sati nati na hauti| natiyā asati āgatigati na hauti| āgatigatiyā asati cutūpapātau na hauti| cutūpapātai asati naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahācundau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau acirapak‍kantaisu taisu āyasmantaisu satthaṃ āharaisi|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, chan‍naina satthaṃ āharitaṃ| tassa kā gati kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘nanu tai, sāriputta, chan‍naina bhikkhunā sammukhāyaiva anupavaj‍jatā byākatā’’ti? ‘‘atthi, bhantai, pubbavij‍janaṃ pubbaviciraṃ (sī॰), pubbavijjhanaṃ (pī॰), pubbajiraṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.394 nāma vaj‍jigāmau| tatthāyasmatau chan‍nassa mittakulāni suhaj‍jakulāni upavaj‍jakulānī’’ti| ‘‘haunti haitai, sāriputta, chan‍nassa bhikkhunau mittakulāni suhaj‍jakulāni upavaj‍jakulāni| na khau panāhaṃ, sāriputta, aittāvatā saupavaj‍jauti vadāmi| yau khau, sāriputta, tañ‍ca kāyaṃ nikkhipati, añ‍ñañ‍ca kāyaṃ  upādiyati, tamahaṃ saupavaj‍jauti vadāmi| taṃ chan‍nassa bhikkhunau natthi| ‘anupavaj‍jaṃ chan‍naina bhikkhunā satthaṃ āharita’nti – aivamaitaṃ, sāriputta, dhāraihī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. puṇṇasuttaṃ

88. atha  sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha (?) ma॰ ni॰ 3.395 passitabbaṃ khau āyasmā puṇṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā puṇṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘santi khau, puṇṇa, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| ‘nandisamudayā dukkhasamudayau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi…pai॰… santi khau, puṇṇa, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, puṇṇa, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai  bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| ‘nandisamudayā dukkhasamudayau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘santi khau, puṇṇa, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nirujjhati nandī| ‘nandiniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi…pai॰… santi khau, puṇṇa, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu  nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau nirujjhati nandī| ‘nandiniraudhā dukkhaniraudhau, puṇṇā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘iminā  tvaṃ iminā ca tvaṃ, puṇṇa, mayā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau katamasmiṃ katarasmiṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.395) janapadai viharissasī’’ti? ‘‘atthi, bhantai, sunāparantau nāma janapadau, tatthāhaṃ viharissāmī’’ti|

‘‘caṇḍā  khau, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā; pharusā khau, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā| sacai taṃ, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā ak‍kausissanti paribhāsissanti, tatra tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, sunāparantakā  manussā ak‍kausissanti paribhāsissanti, tatra mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai maṃ (sabbattha) nayimai pāṇinā pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha aivammaittha (?), bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā pāṇinā pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā pāṇinā pahāraṃ  dassanti, tatra mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai aivammaittha (?) nayimai laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai nayimai daṇḍaina pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai  pana puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā daṇḍaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā daṇḍaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ mai nayimai satthaina pahāraṃ daintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai pana tai, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā satthaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā satthaina pahāraṃ dassanti, tatra mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘bhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, subhaddakā vatimai sunāparantakā manussā, yaṃ maṃ nayimai  tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaintī’ti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sacai  pana taṃ, puṇṇa, sunāparantakā manussā tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaissanti, tatra pana tai, puṇṇa, kinti bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, sunāparantakā manussā tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaissanti, tatra mai aivaṃ bhavissati – ‘santi khau tassa bhagavatau sāvakā kāyaina ca jīvitaina ca aṭṭīyamānā harāyamānā jigucchamānā satthahārakaṃ pariyaisanti, taṃ mai idaṃ apariyiṭṭhañ‍ñaiva satthahārakaṃ laddha’nti| aivamaittha, bhagavā, bhavissati; aivamaittha, sugata, bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, puṇṇa! sakkhissasi khau tvaṃ, puṇṇa, iminā damūpasamaina saman‍nāgatau sunāparantasmiṃ janapadai vatthuṃ| yassa dāni tvaṃ, puṇṇa, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau bhagavatau vacanaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā  padakkhiṇaṃ katvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sunāparantau janapadau taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sunāparantau janapadau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇau sunāparantasmiṃ janapadai viharati| atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau tainaivantaravassaina pañ‍camattāni upāsakasatāni paṭivaidaisi paṭipādaisi (sī॰ pī॰), paṭidaisaisi (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tainaivantaravassaina pañ‍camattāni upāsikāsatāni paṭivaidaisi| tainaivantaravassaina tissau vij‍jā  sacchākāsi| tainaivantaravassaina parinibbāyi|

atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, puṇṇau nāma kulaputtau bhagavatā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau, sau kālaṅkatau| tassa kā gati kau abhisamparāyau’’ti?

‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, puṇṇau kulaputtau kulaputtau ahausi (sabbattha), pac‍capādi sac‍cavādī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) dhammassānudhammaṃ, na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ vihaisaisi vihaiṭhaisi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| parinibbutau, bhikkhavai, puṇṇau kulaputtau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bāhiyasuttaṃ

89. atha  khau āyasmā bāhiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bāhiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bāhiya, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… cakkhusamphassau…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu  taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bāhiya, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā bāhiyau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā bāhiyau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bāhiyau arahataṃ ahausīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaaijāsuttaṃ

90. ‘‘aijā, bhikkhavai, raugau, aijā gaṇḍau, aijā sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaijau viharati vītasal‍lau| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya ‘anaijau viharaiyyaṃ viharaiyya (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) vītasal‍lau’ti, cakkhuṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhusmiṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhutau na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhu  maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; rūpai na mañ‍ñaiyya, rūpaisu na mañ‍ñaiyya, rūpatau na mañ‍ñaiyya, rūpā maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇatau na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; cakkhusamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhusamphassasmiṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhusamphassatau na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhusamphassau maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya|

‘‘sautaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… ghānaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… jivhaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, jivhāya na mañ‍ñaiyya, jivhātau na mañ‍ñaiyya, jivhā maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; rasai na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… jivhāsamphassaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya|

‘‘kāyaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… manaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, manasmiṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, manatau na mañ‍ñaiyya, manau maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; dhammai na mañ‍ñaiyya…pai॰… manau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manausamphassaṃ…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; sabbaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, sabbasmiṃ  na mañ‍ñaiyya, sabbatau na mañ‍ñaiyya, sabbaṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ amañ‍ñamānau na kiñ‍cipi laukai upādiyati| anupādiyaṃ na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaaijāsuttaṃ

91. ‘‘aijā, bhikkhavai, raugau, aijā gaṇḍau, aijā sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau anaijau viharati vītasal‍lau| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya ‘anaijau viharaiyyaṃ vītasal‍lau’ti, cakkhuṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhusmiṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhutau na mañ‍ñaiyya, cakkhu maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; rūpai na mañ‍ñaiyya… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya| yañhi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñati, yasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, yatau mañ‍ñati, yaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau bhavamaiva abhinandati…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, jivhāya na mañ‍ñaiyya, jivhātau na mañ‍ñaiyya, jivhā maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya; rasai na mañ‍ñaiyya… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāsamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya| yañhi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñati, yasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, yatau mañ‍ñati, yaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau bhavamaiva abhinandati…pai॰…|

‘‘manaṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, manasmiṃ na mañ‍ñaiyya, manatau na mañ‍ñaiyya, manau maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi  na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya| yañhi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñati, yasmiṃ mañ‍ñati, yatau  mañ‍ñati, yaṃ maiti mañ‍ñati, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau bhavamaiva abhinandati|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, khandhadhātuāyatanā tampi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tasmimpi na mañ‍ñaiyya, tataupi na mañ‍ñaiyya, taṃ maiti na mañ‍ñaiyya| sau aivaṃ amañ‍ñamānau na kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| anupādiyaṃ na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamadvayasuttaṃ

92. ‘‘dvayaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ? cakkhuñ‍caiva rūpā ca, sautañ‍caiva saddā ca, ghānañ‍caiva gandhā ca, jivhā caiva rasā ca, kāyau caiva phauṭṭhabbā ca, manau caiva dhammā ca – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ|

‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamaitaṃ dvayaṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ dvayaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti, tassa vācāvatthukamaivassa| puṭṭhau ca na sampāyaiyya| uttariñ‍ca vighātaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ, bhikkhavai, avisayasmi’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyadvayasuttaṃ

93. ‘‘dvayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sambhauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ paṭic‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sambhauti? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| cakkhu anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| rūpā  anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| itthaitaṃ dvayaṃ calañ‍caiva byathañ‍ca anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, saupi haitu saupi pac‍cayau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| anic‍caṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ kutau nic‍caṃ bhavissati! yā khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅgati san‍nipātau samavāyau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati cakkhusamphassau| cakkhusamphassaupi anic‍cau  vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau cakkhusamphassassa uppādāya, saupi haitu saupi pac‍cayau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| anic‍caṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nau cakkhusamphassau kutau nic‍cau bhavissati! phuṭṭhau, bhikkhavai, vaidaiti, phuṭṭhau caitaiti, phuṭṭhau sañ‍jānāti| itthaitaipi dhammā calā caiva byathā ca anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| sautaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| jivhā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī vipariṇāminī añ‍ñathābhāvinī (?)| rasā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| itthaitaṃ dvayaṃ calañ‍caiva byathañ‍ca anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, saupi haitu saupi pac‍cayau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| anic‍caṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kutau nic‍caṃ bhavissati! yā khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅgati san‍nipātau samavāyau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati jivhāsamphassau| jivhāsamphassaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau jivhāsamphassassa uppādāya, saupi haitu saupi pac‍cayau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| anic‍caṃ  khau pana, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nau jivhāsamphassau, kutau nic‍cau bhavissati! phuṭṭhau, bhikkhavai, vaidaiti, phuṭṭhau caitaiti, phuṭṭhau sañ‍jānāti| itthaitaipi dhammā calā caiva byathā ca anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| kāyaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| manau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| dhammā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| itthaitaṃ dvayaṃ calañ‍caiva  byathañ‍ca anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau manauviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, saupi haitu saupi pac‍cayau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| anic‍caṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kutau nic‍caṃ bhavissati! yā khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅgati san‍nipātau samavāyau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati manausamphassau| manausamphassaupi anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau manausamphassassa uppādāya, saupi haitu saupi pac‍cayau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| anic‍caṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca  uppan‍nau manausamphassau, kutau nic‍cau bhavissati! phuṭṭhau, bhikkhavai, vaidaiti, phuṭṭhau caitaiti, phuṭṭhau sañ‍jānāti| itthaitaipi dhammā calā caiva byathā ca anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ paṭic‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sambhautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

chan‍navaggau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

palaukasuñ‍ñā saṃkhittaṃ, chan‍nau puṇṇau ca bāhiyau|

aijaina ca duvai vuttā, dvayaihi aparai duvaiti||

10. saḷavaggau

1. adantaaguttasuttaṃ

94. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanā adantā aguttā arakkhitā asaṃvutā dukkhādhivāhā haunti| katamai cha? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanaṃ adantaṃ aguttaṃ arakkhitaṃ asaṃvutaṃ dukkhādhivāhaṃ hauti…pai॰… jivhā, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanaṃ adantaṃ aguttaṃ arakkhitaṃ asaṃvutaṃ dukkhādhivāhaṃ hauti…pai॰… manau, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanaṃ adantaṃ aguttaṃ arakkhitaṃ asaṃvutaṃ dukkhādhivāhaṃ hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha phassāyatanā adantā aguttā arakkhitā asaṃvutā dukkhādhivāhā haunti’’|

‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanā sudantā suguttā surakkhitā susaṃvutā sukhādhivāhā haunti| katamai cha? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanaṃ sudantaṃ suguttaṃ surakkhitaṃ susaṃvutaṃ sukhādhivāhaṃ hauti…pai॰… jivhā, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanaṃ sudantaṃ suguttaṃ surakkhitaṃ susaṃvutaṃ sukhādhivāhaṃ hauti…pai॰… manau, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanaṃ sudantaṃ suguttaṃ surakkhitaṃ  susaṃvutaṃ sukhādhivāhaṃ hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha phassāyatanā sudantā suguttā surakkhitā susaṃvutā sukhādhivāhā hauntī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘saḷaiva chaḷaiva (ka॰) phassāyatanāni bhikkhavau,

asaṃvutau yattha dukkhaṃ nigacchati|

taisañ‍ca yai saṃvaraṇaṃ avaidisuṃ,

saddhādutiyā viharantānavassutā||

‘‘disvāna rūpāni manauramāni,

athaupi disvāna amanauramāni|

manauramai  rāgapathaṃ vinaudayai,

na cāppiyaṃ maiti manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘saddañ‍ca  sutvā dubhayaṃ piyāppiyaṃ,

piyamhi saddai na samucchitau siyā|

athauppiyai dausagataṃ vinaudayai,

na cāppiyaṃ maiti manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘gandhañ‍ca  ghatvā surabhiṃ manauramaṃ,

athaupi ghatvā asuciṃ akantiyaṃ|

akantiyasmiṃ paṭighaṃ vinaudayai,

chandānunītau na ca kantiyai siyā||

‘‘rasañ‍ca bhautvāna asāditañ‍ca sāduṃ,

athaupi bhautvāna asādumaikadā|

sāduṃ rasaṃ nājjhausāya bhuñ‍jai,

viraudhamāsādusu naupadaṃsayai||

‘‘phassaina  phuṭṭhau na sukhaina maj‍jai majjhai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰),

dukkhaina phuṭṭhaupi na sampavaidhai|

phassadvayaṃ sukhadukkhai upaikkhai,

anānuruddhau aviruddha kainaci||

‘‘papañ‍casañ‍ñā itarītarā narā,

papañ‍cayantā upayanti sañ‍ñinau|

manaumayaṃ gaihasitañ‍ca sabbaṃ,

panuj‍ja naikkhammasitaṃ irīyati||

‘‘aivaṃ manau chassu yadā subhāvitau,

phuṭṭhassa cittaṃ na vikampatai k‍vaci|

tai rāgadausai abhibhuyya bhikkhavau,

bhavattha bhavatha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) jātimaraṇassa pāragā’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. mālukyaputtasuttaṃ

95. atha  khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau māluṅkyaputtau (sī॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau  ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘aittha  dāni, mālukyaputta, kiṃ daharai bhikkhū vakkhāma! yatra hi nāma tvaṃ, bhikkhu, jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau saṃkhittaina auvādaṃ yācasī’’ti|

‘‘kiñ‍cāpāhaṃ, bhantai, jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| daisaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ, appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyyaṃ| appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa dāyādau assa’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mālukyaputta, yai tai cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā adiṭṭhā adiṭṭhapubbā, na ca passasi, na ca tai hauti passaiyyanti? atthi tai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yai tai sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā assutā assutapubbā, na ca suṇāsi, na ca tai hauti suṇaiyyanti? atthi tai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yai tai ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā aghāyitā aghāyitapubbā, na ca ghāyasi, na ca tai hauti ghāyaiyyanti? atthi tai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yai tai jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā asāyitā asāyitapubbā, na ca sāyasi, na ca tai hauti sāyaiyyanti? atthi tai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yai tai kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā asamphuṭṭhā asamphuṭṭhapubbā, na ca phusasi, na ca tai hauti phusaiyyanti? atthi tai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yai  tai manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā  aviñ‍ñātā aviñ‍ñātapubbā, na ca vijānāsi, na ca tai hauti vijānaiyyanti? atthi tai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aittha ca tai, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattaṃ bhavissati, sutai sutamattaṃ bhavissati, mutai mutamattaṃ bhavissati, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātamattaṃ bhavissati| yatau khau tai, mālukyaputta, diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattaṃ bhavissati, sutai sutamattaṃ bhavissati, mutai mutamattaṃ bhavissati, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātamattaṃ bhavissati; tatau tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, na taina| yatau tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, na taina; tatau tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, na tattha| yatau tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, na tattha; tatau tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, naividha, na huraṃ, na ubhayamantaraina| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti|

‘‘imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi –

‘‘rūpaṃ disvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa ajjhausāya (sī॰) tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā rūpasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘saddaṃ sutvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa  vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā saddasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘gandhaṃ  ghatvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā gandhasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘rasaṃ  bhautvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā rasasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘phassaṃ  phussa sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā phassasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘dhammaṃ ñatvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā dhammasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau  dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘na sau raj‍jati rūpaisu, rūpaṃ disvā paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa passatau rūpaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘na sau raj‍jati saddaisu, saddaṃ sutvā paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa  suṇatau saddaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘na  sau raj‍jati gandhaisu, gandhaṃ ghatvā paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa ghāyatau gandhaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘na sau raj‍jati rasaisu, rasaṃ bhautvā paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa  sāyatau rasaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘na  sau raj‍jati phassaisu, phassaṃ phussa paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa phusatau phassaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍cati||

‘‘na  sau raj‍jati dhammaisu, dhammaṃ ñatvā paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa  jānatau dhammaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mālukyaputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, mālukyaputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi –

‘‘rūpaṃ disvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā rūpasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivaṃ ācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbānamuc‍cati||…pai॰…||

‘‘na sau raj‍jati dhammaisu, dhammaṃ ñatvā paṭissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhausa tiṭṭhati||

‘‘yathāssa  vijānatau dhammaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbānamuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘imassa khau, mālukyaputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ  karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā mālukyaputtau arahataṃ ahausīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. parihānadhammasuttaṃ

96. ‘‘parihānadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi aparihānadhammañ‍ca cha ca abhibhāyatanāni| taṃ suṇātha| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, parihānadhammau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) upari āsīvisavaggai sattamasuttai pana ‘‘ākusalā sarasaṅkappā’’ tvaiva sabbattha dissati saṃyaujaniyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu adhivāsaiti nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantīkarauti byantikarauti (pī॰) byantiṃ karauti (ka॰) na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘parihāyāmi kusalaihi  dhammaihi’| parihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau manasā dhammaṃ  viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu adhivāsaiti nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantīkarauti na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| parihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, parihānadhammau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, aparihānadhammau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘na parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| aparihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā uppaj‍janti…pai॰… puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘na parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| aparihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aparihānadhammau hauti|

‘‘katamāni  ca, bhikkhavai, cha abhibhāyatanāni? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nuppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘abhibhūtamaitaṃ āyatanaṃ’| abhibhāyatanañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti…pai॰… puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya nuppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘abhibhūtamaitaṃ āyatanaṃ’| abhibhāyatanañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti| imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, cha abhibhāyatanānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pamādavihārīsuttaṃ

97. ‘‘pamādavihāriñ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi appamādavihāriñ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pamādavihārī hauti? cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutassa, bhikkhavai, viharatau cittaṃ byāsiñ‍cati byāsic‍cati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu tassa byāsittacittassa pāmauj‍jaṃ na hauti| pāmauj‍jai asati pīti na hauti| pītiyā asati passaddhi na hauti| passaddhiyā asati dukkhaṃ hauti| dukkhinau cittaṃ na samādhiyati| asamāhitai cittai dhammā na pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ apātubhāvā pamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ asaṃvutassa, bhikkhavai, viharatau cittaṃ byāsiñ‍cati jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu, tassa byāsittacittassa…pai॰… pamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati…pai॰… manindriyaṃ asaṃvutassa, bhikkhavai, viharatau cittaṃ byāsiñ‍cati manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu, tassa byāsittacittassa pāmauj‍jaṃ na hauti| pāmauj‍jai asati pīti na hauti| pītiyā asati passaddhi na hauti| passaddhiyā asati dukkhaṃ hauti| dukkhinau cittaṃ na samādhiyati| asamāhitai cittai dhammā na pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ apātubhāvā pamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pamādavihārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, appamādavihārī hauti? cakkhundriyaṃ saṃvutassa, bhikkhavai, viharatau  cittaṃ na byāsiñ‍cati cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu, tassa abyāsittacittassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ viharati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitai cittai dhammā  pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā appamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ saṃvutassa, bhikkhavai, viharatau cittaṃ na byāsiñ‍cati…pai॰… appamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| manindriyaṃ saṃvutassa, bhikkhavai, viharatau cittaṃ na byāsiñ‍cati, manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu, tassa abyāsittacittassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ viharati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitai cittai dhammā pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā appamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, appamādavihārī hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. saṃvarasuttaṃ

98. ‘‘saṃvarañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asaṃvarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṃvarau hauti? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā  rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| parihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| parihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asaṃvarau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃvarau hauti? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā – ‘na parihāyāmi kusalaihi  dhammaihi’| aparihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti  …pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā  rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, vaiditabbamaitaṃ bhikkhunā – ‘na parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| aparihānañhaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saṃvarau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samādhisuttaṃ

99. ‘‘samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? ‘cakkhu anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti; ‘rūpā anic‍cā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘cakkhusamphassau anic‍cau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘manau anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… ‘yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭisal‍lānasuttaṃ

100. ‘‘paṭisal‍lānai paṭisal‍lānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), paṭisal‍līnā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līnau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? ‘cakkhu anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘rūpā anic‍cā’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘cakkhusamphassau anic‍cau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍ca’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| paṭisal‍lānai, bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līnau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamanatumhākaṃsuttaṃ

101. ‘‘yaṃ  yampi (pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| rūpā na tumhākaṃ| tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| cakkhusamphassau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnau hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| sautaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| saddā na tumhākaṃ| tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| sautasamphassau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yampidaṃ sautasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| ghānaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| gandhā na tumhākaṃ| tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| ghānasamphassau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yampidaṃ ghānasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati|

jivhā na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sā vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| rasā na tumhākaṃ| tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| jivhāsamphassau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ  sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati  …pai॰…|

manau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnau hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| dhammā na tumhākaṃ| tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| manausamphassau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnau hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na  tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ imasmiṃ jaitavanai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ taṃ janau haraiyya vā ḍahaiyya vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaiyya, api nu tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘amhai janau harati vā ḍahati vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karautī’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’?

‘‘na hi nau aitaṃ, bhantai, attā vā attaniyaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhu na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| rūpā na tumhākaṃ… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyanatumhākaṃsuttaṃ

102. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| rūpā na tumhākaṃ| tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| cakkhusamphassau na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnau hitāya sukhāya bhavissati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi na tumhākaṃ| taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau  pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yampi, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. udakasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘udakau  uddakau (sī॰ pī॰) sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, rāmaputtau aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘idaṃ jātu vaidagū, idaṃ jātu sabbajī sabbaji (pī॰), idaṃ jātu apalikhataṃ gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhaṇi’nti| taṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, udakau rāmaputtau avaidagūyaiva samānau ‘vaidagūsmī’ti bhāsati, asabbajīyaiva samānau ‘sabbajīsmī’ti bhāsati, apalikhataṃyaiva gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhataṃ mai ‘gaṇḍamūla’nti bhāsati| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘idaṃ jātu vaidagū, idaṃ jātu sabbajī, idaṃ jātu apalikhataṃ gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhaṇi’’’nti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vaidagū hauti? yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidagū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbajī hauti? yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti; aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbajī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau apalikhataṃ gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhataṃ hauti? gaṇḍauti khau, bhikkhavai, imassaitaṃ cātumahābhūtikassa kāyassa adhivacanaṃ  mātāpaittikasambhavassa audanakummāsūpacayassa anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammassa| gaṇḍamūlanti khau, bhikkhavai, taṇhāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā  anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā; aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau apalikhataṃ gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhataṃ hauti|

‘‘udakau sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, rāmaputtau aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘idaṃ jātu vaidagū, idaṃ jātu sabbajī, idaṃ jātu apalikhataṃ gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhaṇi’nti| taṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, udakau rāmaputtau avaidagūyaiva samānau ‘vaidagūsmī’ti bhāsati, asabbajīyaiva samānau ‘sabbajīsmī’ti bhāsati; apalikhataṃyaiva gaṇḍamūlaṃ ‘palikhataṃ mai gaṇḍamūla’nti bhāsati| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘idaṃ jātu vaidagū, idaṃ jātu sabbajī, idaṃ jātu apalikhataṃ gaṇḍamūlaṃ palikhaṇi’’’nti| dasamaṃ|

saḷavaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai saṃgayhā parihānaṃ, pamādavihārī ca saṃvarau|

samādhi paṭisal‍lānaṃ, dvai natumhākaina uddakauti||

saḷāyatanavaggai dutiyapaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa vagguddānaṃ –

avij‍jā migajālañ‍ca, gilānaṃ chan‍naṃ catutthakaṃ|

saḷavaggaina pañ‍ñāsaṃ, dutiyau paṇṇāsakau ayanti||

paṭhamasatakaṃ|

11. yaugakkhaimivaggau

1. yaugakkhaimisuttaṃ

104. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaugakkhaimipariyāyaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, yaugakkhaimipariyāyau dhammapariyāyau? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā  piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| taisañ‍ca pahānāya akkhāsi yaugaṃ, tasmā tathāgatau ‘yaugakkhaimī’ti vuc‍cati…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| taisañ‍ca pahānāya akkhāsi yaugaṃ, tasmā tathāgatau ‘yaugakkhaimī’ti vuc‍cati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yaugakkhaimipariyāyau dhammapariyāyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. upādāyasuttaṃ

105. ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati kiṃ upādāya uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkha’’nti?

‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|

‘‘cakkhusmiṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sati cakkhuṃ upādāya uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… manasmiṃ sati manaṃ upādāya uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ| taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyya ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkha’’nti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhā  nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyya ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkha’’nti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyya ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkha’’nti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dukkhasamudayasuttaṃ

106. ‘‘dukkhassa, bhikkhavai, samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa samudayau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| ayaṃ dukkhassa samudayau…pai॰…  jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| ayaṃ dukkhassa samudayau…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa samudayau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa atthaṅgamau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ dukkhassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manañ‍ca  paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. laukasamudayasuttaṃ

107. ‘‘laukassa, bhikkhavai, samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, laukassa samudayau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa samudayau  …pai॰… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa samudayau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, laukassa atthaṅgamau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. saiyyauhamasmisuttaṃ

108. ‘‘kismiṃ  nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati kiṃ upādāya kiṃ abhinivissa saiyyauhamasmīti vā hauti, sadisauhamasmīti vā hauti, hīnauhamasmīti vā hautī’’ti?

‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā|

‘‘cakkhusmiṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sati cakkhuṃ upādāya cakkhuṃ abhinivissa saiyyauhamasmīti vā hauti, sadisauhamasmīti vā hauti, hīnauhamasmīti vā hauti…pai॰… jivhāya sati…pai॰… manasmiṃ sati manaṃ upādāya manaṃ abhinivissa saiyyauhamasmīti vā hauti, sadisauhamasmīti vā hauti, hīnauhamasmīti vā hauti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya saiyyauhamasmīti vā assa, sadisauhamasmīti vā assa, hīnauhamasmīti  vā assā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… jivhā… kāyau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā  taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya saiyyauhamasmīti vā assa, sadisauhamasmīti vā assa, hīnauhamasmīti vā assā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. saṃyaujaniyasuttaṃ

109. ‘‘saṃyaujaniyai  ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi saṃyaujanañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā, katamañ‍ca saṃyaujanaṃ? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyau dhammau| yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… jivhā saṃyaujaniyau dhammau…pai॰… manau saṃyaujaniyau dhammau| yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā, idaṃ saṃyaujana’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. upādāniyasuttaṃ

110. ‘‘upādāniyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi upādānañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā, katamañ‍ca upādānaṃ? cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, upādāniyau dhammau| yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha upādānaṃ…pai॰… jivhā upādāniyau dhammau…pai॰… manau upādāniyau dhammau| yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha upādānaṃ| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā, idaṃ upādāna’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. ajjhattikāyatanaparijānanasuttaṃ

111. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| sautaṃ… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… manaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| cakkhuñ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya…pai॰… jivhaṃ… kāyaṃ… manaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bāhirāyatanaparijānanasuttaṃ

112. ‘‘rūpai, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… dhammai anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ  avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| rūpai ca khau, bhikkhavai, abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… dhammai abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. upassutisuttaṃ

113. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai ñātikai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| atha khau bhagavā rahaugatau paṭisal‍līnau imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ abhāsi – ‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā  upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti…pai॰… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavatau upassuti upassutiṃ (sī॰ ka॰) ṭhitau hauti| addasā khau  bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ upassuti ṭhitaṃ| disvāna taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘assausi nau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāya’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘uggaṇhāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| pariyāpuṇāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| dhāraihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| atthasaṃhitauyaṃ, bhikkhu, dhammapariyāyau ādibrahmacariyakau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

yaugakkhaimivaggau aikādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

yaugakkhaimi upādāya, dukkhaṃ laukau ca saiyyau ca|

saṃyaujanaṃ upādānaṃ, dvai parijānaṃ upassutīti||

12. laukakāmaguṇavaggau

1. paṭhamamārapāsasuttaṃ

114. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai, bhikkhu, abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āvāsagatau mārassa, mārassa vasaṃ gatau vasagatau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰), paṭimuk‍kassa mārapāsau| baddhau sau mārabandhanaina yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āvāsagatau mārassa, mārassa vasaṃ gatau, paṭimuk‍kassa mārapāsau| baddhau sau mārabandhanaina…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āvāsagatau mārassa, mārassa vasaṃ gatau, paṭimuk‍kassa  mārapāsau| baddhau sau mārabandhanaina yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau|

‘‘santi ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nāvāsagatau mārassa, na mārassa vasaṃ gatau, ummuk‍kassa mārapāsau| muttau sau mārabandhanaina na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nāvāsagatau  mārassa, na mārassa vasaṃ gatau, ummuk‍kassa mārapāsau| muttau sau mārabandhanaina na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nāvāsagatau mārassa, na mārassa vasaṃ gatau, ummuk‍kassa mārapāsau| muttau sau mārabandhanaina na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyamārapāsasuttaṃ

115. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu baddhau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu, āvāsagatau mārassa, mārassa vasaṃ gatau, paṭimuk‍kassa mārapāsau| baddhau sau mārabandhanaina yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati  abhivadati  ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu baddhau manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu, āvāsagatau mārassa, mārassa vasaṃ gatau, paṭimuk‍kassa mārapāsau| baddhau sau mārabandhanaina yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu muttau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi, nāvāsagatau mārassa, na mārassa vasaṃ gatau, ummuk‍kassa mārapāsau| muttau sau mārabandhanaina na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai  bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu muttau manauviñ‍ñaiyyaihi dhammaihi, nāvāsagatau mārassa, na mārassa vasaṃ gatau, ummuk‍kassa mārapāsau| muttau sau mārabandhanaina na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. laukantagamanasuttaṃ

116. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ diṭṭhaiyyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’’ti| idaṃ vatvā bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti?

atha  khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca  viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ  aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau ānanda, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’ti| taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmīti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti? taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| vibhajatāyasmā ānandau’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva, mūlaṃ atik‍kammaiva, khandhaṃ sākhāpalāsai sāraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya; aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ āyasmantānaṃ satthari sammukhībhūtai taṃ bhagavantaṃ atisitvā  amhai aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatha mañ‍ñaitha (pī॰ ka॰)| sau hāvusau, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ  passati – cakkhubhūtau, ñāṇabhūtau, dhammabhūtau, brahmabhūtau, vattā, pavattā, atthassa nin‍naitā, amatassa dātā, dhammassāmī, tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā vau dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘addhāvusau ānanda, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati – cakkhubhūtau, ñāṇabhūtau, dhammabhūtau, brahmabhūtau, vattā, pavattā, atthassa nin‍naitā, amatassa dātā, dhammassāmī, tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāma| api cāyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| vibhajatāyasmā ānandau agaruṃ karitvā’’ti|

‘‘tainahāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘yaṃ khau vau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’ti, imassa khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| yaina khau, āvusau, laukasmiṃ laukasañ‍ñī hauti laukamānī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ariyassa vinayai laukau| kaina cāvusau, laukasmiṃ laukasañ‍ñī hauti laukamānī? cakkhunā khau, āvusau, laukasmiṃ laukasañ‍ñī hauti laukamānī| sautaina khau, āvusau… ghānaina khau, āvusau… jivhāya khau, āvusau, laukasmiṃ laukasañ‍ñī hauti laukamānī| kāyaina khau, āvusau… manaina khau, āvusau, laukasmiṃ laukasañ‍ñī hauti laukamānī| yaina khau, āvusau, laukasmiṃ laukasañ‍ñī  hauti laukamānī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ariyassa vinayai laukau| yaṃ  khau vau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’ti, imassa khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai āyasmantau bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’ti| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ, daṭṭhaiyyaṃ, pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmī’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyāti? taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau  āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā ānandaina imaihi ākāraihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau vibhattau’’ti|

‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, ānandau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, ānandau! maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi taṃ aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ ānandaina byākataṃ| aisau caivaitassa atthau, aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kāmaguṇasuttaṃ
