117. ‘‘pubbaiva mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva  satau aitadahausi – ‘yaimai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā caitasau samphuṭṭhapubbā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā, tatra mai cittaṃ bahulaṃ gacchamānaṃ gacchaiyya pac‍cuppan‍naisu vā appaṃ vā anāgataisu’| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaimai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā caitasau samphuṭṭhapubbā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā, tatra mai attarūpaina appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau’| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tumhākampi yai tai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā caitasau samphuṭṭhapubbā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā, tatra vau cittaṃ bahulaṃ gacchamānaṃ gacchaiyya pac‍cuppan‍naisu vā appaṃ vā anāgataisu| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tumhākampi yai tai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā caitasau samphuṭṭhapubbā  atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā, tatra vau attarūpaihi appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha jivhā ca nirujjhati, rasasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’’ti| idaṃ vatvā bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai  yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha jivhā ca nirujjhati, rasasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai  āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā  saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti?

atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau ānanda, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha jivhā ca nirujjhati, rasasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha jivhā ca nirujjhati, rasasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyāti? taisaṃ nau, āvusau, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina  uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau  tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| vibhajatāyasmā ānandau’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa…pai॰… vibhajatāyasmā ānandau agaruṃ karitvāti|

‘‘tainahāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘yaṃ khau vau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| imassa khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| saḷāyatananiraudhaṃ nau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai, yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| imassa khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa  aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai āyasmantau bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamatha; upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha jivhā ca nirujjhati, rasasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti, taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai yattha cakkhu ca nirujjhati, rūpasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai…pai॰… yattha manau ca nirujjhati, dhammasañ‍ñā ca nirujjhati, sai āyatanai vaiditabbai’ti| ‘kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina  uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti? taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau, sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā ānandau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā ānandaina imaihi ākāraihi, imaihi padaihi, imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau vibhattau’’ti|

‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, ānandau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, ānandau! maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi taṃ aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ ānandaina byākataṃ| aisau caivaitassa atthau| aivañ‍ca naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. sak‍kapañhasuttaṃ

118. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina  midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti?

‘‘santi khau, daivānaminda, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati…pai॰…|

‘‘santi khau, daivānaminda, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, daivānaminda, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, daivānaminda, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti|

‘‘santi ca khau, daivānaminda, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu parinibbāyati…pai॰…|

‘‘santi khau, daivānaminda, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, daivānaminda, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau  na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu  parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, daivānaminda, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pañ‍casikhasuttaṃ

119. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau pañ‍casikhau gandhabbadaivaputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau pañ‍casikhau gandhabbadaivaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti? ‘‘santi khau, pañ‍casikha, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā…pai॰… santi khau, pañ‍casikha, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, pañ‍casikha, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, pañ‍casikha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti’’|

‘‘santi ca khau, pañ‍casikha, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā…pai॰… santi khau, pañ‍casikha, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, pañ‍casikha, bhikkhu parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, pañ‍casikha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sāriputtasaddhivihārikasuttaṃ

120. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saddhivihārikau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, āvusau, hauti indriyaisu aguttadvārassa, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttassa| ‘sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu indriyaisu aguttadvārau bhaujanai amattañ‍ñū jāgariyaṃ  ananuyuttau yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ santānaissatī’ti  naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘sau vatāvusau, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ santānaissatī’ti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya, na madāya, na maṇḍanāya, na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya, vihiṃsūparatiyā, brahmacariyānuggahāya| iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati, anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya  āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau, uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, jāgariyaṃ  anuyuttau hauti| tasmātihāvusau, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘indriyaisu guttadvārā bhavissāma, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā’ti| aivañhi vau, āvusau, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. rāhulauvādasuttaṃ

121. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘paripak‍kā khau rāhulassa vimuttiparipācaniyā dhammā; yaṃnūnāhaṃ rāhulaṃ uttariṃ āsavānaṃ khayai vinaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhāhi, rāhula, nisīdanaṃ| yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nisīdanaṃ ādāya bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi|

taina khau pana samayaina anaikāni daivatāsahassāni bhagavantaṃ anubandhāni haunti – ‘‘aj‍ja bhagavā āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ uttariṃ āsavānaṃ khayai vinaissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau rāhulau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ( ) (taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi) aivamitaraisupi (ma॰ ni॰ 3.416-417)

‘‘rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘cakkhusamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ, sañ‍ñāgataṃ, saṅkhāragataṃ, viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhāsamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ, sañ‍ñāgataṃ, saṅkhāragataṃ, viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vāti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘dhammā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘manausamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘yampidaṃ  manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ, sañ‍ñāgataṃ, saṅkhāragataṃ, viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ  mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… jivhāyapi nibbindati, rasaisupi nibbindati, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, jivhāsamphassaipi nibbindati, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰…|

‘‘manasmimpi nibbindati, dhammaisupi nibbindati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, manausamphassaipi nibbindati, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandi| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai āyasmatau rāhulassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci| anaikānañ‍ca  daivatāsahassānaṃ virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. saṃyaujaniyadhammasuttaṃ

122. ‘‘saṃyaujaniyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi saṃyaujanañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā, katamañ‍ca saṃyaujanaṃ? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā  piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā| yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā| yau tattha chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujana’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. upādāniyadhammasuttaṃ

123. ‘‘upādāniyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi upādānañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā, katamañ‍ca upādānaṃ? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā| yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha upādānaṃ…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā| yau tattha chandarāgau taṃ tattha upādāna’’nti| dasamaṃ|

laukakāmaguṇavaggau dvādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

mārapāsaina dvai vuttā, laukakāmaguṇaina ca|

sak‍kau pañ‍casikhau caiva, sāriputtau ca rāhulau|

saṃyaujanaṃ upādānaṃ, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

13. gahapativaggau

1. vaisālīsuttaṃ

124. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau uggau gahapati vaisālikau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggau gahapati vaisālikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti?

‘‘santi khau, gahapati, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, gahapati, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti|

‘‘santi  ca khau, gahapati, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā  rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu parinibbāyati…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau| na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, gahapati, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vaj‍jīsuttaṃ

125. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaj‍jīsu viharati hatthigāmai| atha khau uggau gahapati hatthigāmakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggau gahapati hatthigāmakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti? (yathā purimasuttantaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| ayaṃ  khau, gahapati, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva  dhammai parinibbāyantīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. nāḷandasuttaṃ

126. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha khau, upāli gahapati, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau, upāli gahapati, bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti? (yathā purimasuttantaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| ayaṃ khau, gahapati, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bhāradvājasuttaṃ

127. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau rājā udainau yaināyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā udainau āyasmantaṃ piṇḍaulabhāradvājaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau bhāradvāja, haitu  kau pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū susu (sī॰ ka॰) kāḷakaisā bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā anikīḷitāvinau kāmaisu yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caranti, addhānañ‍ca āpādaintī’’ti? ‘‘vuttaṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, taina  bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, mātumattīsu mātucittaṃ upaṭṭhapaitha, bhaginimattīsu  bhaginicittaṃ upaṭṭhapaitha, dhītumattīsu dhītucittaṃ upaṭṭhapaithā’ti| ayaṃ khau, mahārāja, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū kāḷakaisā bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā anikīḷitāvinau kāmaisu yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caranti, addhānañ‍ca āpādaintī’’ti|

‘‘laulaṃ lauḷaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau, bhau bhāradvāja, cittaṃ| appaikadā mātumattīsupi laubhadhammā uppaj‍janti, bhaginimattīsupi laubhadhammā uppaj‍janti, dhītumattīsupi laubhadhammā uppaj‍janti| atthi nu khau, bhau bhāradvāja, añ‍ñau ca haitu, añ‍ñau ca pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū kāḷakaisā…pai॰… addhānañ‍ca āpādaintī’’ti?

‘‘vuttaṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhatha – atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jā (sī॰) vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti| ayampi khau, mahārāja, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū kāḷakaisā…pai॰…  addhānañ‍ca āpādaintī’’ti| ‘‘yai tai, bhau bhāradvāja, bhikkhū bhāvitakāyā bhāvitasīlā bhāvitacittā bhāvitapañ‍ñā, taisaṃ taṃ sukaraṃ hauti| yai ca khau tai, bhau bhāradvāja, bhikkhū abhāvitakāyā abhāvitasīlā abhāvitacittā abhāvitapañ‍ñā, taisaṃ taṃ duk‍karaṃ hauti| appaikadā, bhau bhāradvāja, asubhatau manasi  karissāmīti manasi karissāmāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) subhatauva subhatau vā (sī॰), subhatau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āgacchati| atthi nu khau, bhau bhāradvāja, añ‍ñau ca khau haitu añ‍ñau ca pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū kāḷakaisā…pai॰… addhānañ‍ca āpādaintī’’ti?

‘‘vuttaṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, indriyaisu guttadvārā viharatha| cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā mā nimittaggāhinau ahuvattha, mānubyañ‍janaggāhinau| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jatha| rakkhatha cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatha| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya mā nimittaggāhinau ahuvattha, mānubyañ‍janaggāhinau| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jatha| rakkhatha manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jathā’ti| ayampi khau, mahārāja, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū kāḷakaisā bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā anikīḷitāvinau kāmaisu yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caranti, addhānañ‍ca āpādaintī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja; abbhutaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ yāva subhāsitamidaṃ (sī॰), bhau bhāradvāja, taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina| aisauva khau, bhau bhāradvāja, haitu, aisa pac‍cayau yainimai daharā bhikkhū susū kāḷakaisā bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā anikīḷitāvinau kāmaisu yāvajīvaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caranti, addhānañ‍ca āpādaintīti| ahampi khau, bhau ahampi bhau (sī॰ pī॰) bhāradvāja, yasmiṃ samayai arakkhitainaiva kāyaina, arakkhitāya vācāya, arakkhitaina cittaina, anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā, asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi antaipuraṃ pavisāmi, ativiya maṃ tasmiṃ samayai laubhadhammā parisahanti| yasmiñ‍ca khvāhaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja, samayai rakkhitainaiva kāyaina, rakkhitāya vācāya, rakkhitaina cittaina, upaṭṭhitāya satiyā, saṃvutaihi indriyaihi antaipuraṃ pavisāmi, na maṃ tathā  tasmiṃ samayai laubhadhammā parisahanti| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja; abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja! saiyyathāpi, bhau bhāradvāja, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ nikuj‍jitaṃ (pī॰) vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā bhāradvājaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhau bhāradvāja, taṃ bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ bhāradvājau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. sauṇasuttaṃ

128. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau sauṇau gahapatiputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sauṇau gahapatiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti? (yathā purimasuttantaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| ayaṃ khau, sauṇa, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantīti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ghausitasuttaṃ

129. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau ghausitau gahapati yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ghausitau gahapati āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, dhātunānatta’nti, bhantai ānanda, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, cakkhudhātu, rūpā ca manāpā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca  sukhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, cakkhudhātu, rūpā ca amanāpā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca dukkhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, cakkhudhātu, rūpā ca manāpā upaikkhāvaidaniyā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā  vaidanā…pai॰… saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, jivhādhātu, rasā ca manāpā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sukhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati  sukhā vaidanā| saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, jivhādhātu, rasā ca amanāpā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca dukkhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, jivhādhātu, rasā ca upaikkhāvaidaniyā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā…pai॰… saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, manaudhātu, dhammā ca manāpā, manauviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sukhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, manaudhātu, dhammā ca amanāpā, manauviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca dukkhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| saṃvij‍jati khau, gahapati, manaudhātu, dhammā ca upaikkhāvaidaniyā, manauviñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| aittāvatā khau, gahapati, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. hāliddikānisuttaṃ

130. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cānau avantīsu viharati kuraragharai kulagharai (syā॰ ka॰) papātai pavattai (sī॰ pī॰), sampavattai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aitthaiva aṭṭhamapiṭṭhaipi pabbatai| atha khau hāliddikāni hāliddakāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) gahapati yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau hāliddikāni gahapati āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ; phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānatta’nti| kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ; phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānatta’’nti? ‘‘idha, gahapati, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā ‘manāpaṃ itthaita’nti pajānāti cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sukhavaidaniyañ‍ca sukhavaidaniyaṃ, sukhavaidaniyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), sukhavaidaniyañ‍ca, sukhavaidaniyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aivaṃ ‘‘dukkhavaidaniyañ‍ca adukkhamasukhavaidaniyañ‍cā’’ti padaisupi| aṭṭhakathāṭīkā aulaukaitabbā| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| cakkhunā khau panaiva panaivaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) rūpaṃ disvā ‘amanāpaṃ itthaita’nti pajānāti cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhavaidaniyañ‍ca| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| cakkhunā khau panaiva rūpaṃ disvā ‘upaikkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ  upaikkhāvaidaniyaṃ (ka॰) itthaita’nti pajānāti cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyañ‍ca| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya ‘manāpaṃ itthaita’nti pajānāti manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ sukhavaidaniyañ‍ca| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| manasā khau panaiva dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya  ‘amanāpaṃ itthaita’nti pajānāti manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  dukkhavaidaniyañ‍ca| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| manasā khau panaiva dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya ‘upaikkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ itthaita’nti pajānāti manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyañ‍ca| phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ; phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānatta’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. nakulapitusuttaṃ

131. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| atha khau nakulapitā gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau nakulapitā gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti? ‘‘santi khau, gahapati, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti tadupādānaṃ| saupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu nau parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, gahapati, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai nau parinibbāyanti’’|

‘‘santi ca khau, gahapati, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhunābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, na tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu parinibbāyati…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi khau, gahapati, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ nābhinandatau nābhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau na tan‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti na  tadupādānaṃ| anupādānau, gahapati, bhikkhu parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, gahapati, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. lauhic‍casuttaṃ

132. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cānau avantīsu viharati mak‍karakatai mak‍karakaṭai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ| atha  khau lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa sambahulā  antaivāsikā kaṭṭhahārakā māṇavakā yaināyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa arañ‍ñakuṭikā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā paritau paritau kuṭikāya anucaṅkamanti anuvicaranti uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā kānici kānici sailaiyyakāni karaunti sailissakāni karauntā (sī॰) – ‘‘imai pana muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kaṇhā kiṇhā (sī॰ pī॰) bandhupādāpac‍cā, imaisaṃ bharatakānaṃ sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā apacitā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau vihārā nikkhamitvā tai māṇavakai aitadavauca – ‘‘mā māṇavakā saddamakattha; dhammaṃ vau bhāsissāmī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, tai māṇavakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau tai māṇavakai gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘sīluttamā pubbatarā ahaisuṃ,

tai brāhmaṇā yai purāṇaṃ saranti|

guttāni dvārāni surakkhitāni,

ahaisuṃ taisaṃ abhibhuyya kaudhaṃ||

‘‘dhammai  ca jhānai ca ratā ahaisuṃ,

tai brāhmaṇā yai purāṇaṃ saranti|

imai ca vauk‍kamma japāmasaiti,

gauttaina mattā visamaṃ caranti||

‘‘kaudhābhibhūtā puthuattadaṇḍā kaudhābhibhūtāsuputhuttadaṇḍā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰),

viraj‍jamānā sataṇhātaṇhaisu|

aguttadvārassa  bhavanti maughā,

supinaiva laddhaṃ purisassa vittaṃ||

‘‘anāsakā  thaṇḍilasāyikā ca|

pātau sinānañ‍ca tayau ca vaidā||

‘‘kharājinaṃ jaṭāpaṅkau, mantā sīlabbataṃ tapau|

kuhanā vaṅkadaṇḍā ca, udakācamanāni ca||

‘‘vaṇṇā  aitai brāhmaṇānaṃ, katā kiñ‍cikkhabhāvanā|

cittañ‍ca susamāhitaṃ, vippasan‍namanāvilaṃ|

akhilaṃ sabbabhūtaisu, sau maggau brahmapattiyā’’ti||

atha khau tai māṇavakā kupitā anattamanā yaina lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā lauhic‍caṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yagghai! bhavaṃ jānaiyya, samaṇau mahākac‍cānau brāhmaṇānaṃ mantai mantaṃ (ka॰) aikaṃsaina apavadati, paṭik‍kausatī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau kupitau ahausi anattamanau| atha khau lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau pana maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yauhaṃ añ‍ñadatthu māṇavakānaṃyaiva sutvā samaṇaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ ak‍kausaiyyaṃ ak‍kausaiyyaṃ virujjhaiyyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paribhāsaiyyaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchaiyya’’nti|

atha khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau  āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaṃsu nu khvidha, bhau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ sambahulā antaivāsikā kaṭṭhahārakā māṇavakā’’ti? ‘‘āgamaṃsu khvidha tai, brāhmaṇa, sambahulā antaivāsikā kaṭṭhahārakā māṇavakā’’ti| ‘‘ahu pana bhautau kac‍cānassa taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? ‘‘ahu khau mai, brāhmaṇa, taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana bhautau kac‍cānassa taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ ahausi  kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ ahausi kathāsal‍lāpau –

‘‘sīluttamā pubbatarā ahaisuṃ,

tai brāhmaṇā yai purāṇaṃ saranti|…pai॰…|

akhilaṃ sabbabhūtaisu,

sau maggau brahmapattiyā’’ti||

‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ ahausi kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti|

‘‘‘aguttadvārau’ti aguttadvārau aguttadvārauti (ka॰) bhavaṃ kac‍cānau āha| kittāvatā nu khau, bhau kac‍cāna, aguttadvārau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) upari āsīvisavaggai avassutasuttai pana ‘‘anupaṭṭhitakāyassatī’’tvaiva sabbattha dissati ca viharati, parittacaitasau tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai ca dhammai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati, parittacaitasau tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aguttadvārau hautī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna; abbhutaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna! yāvañ‍cidaṃ bhautā  kac‍cānaina aguttadvārauva samānau aguttadvārauti akkhātau|

‘‘‘guttadvārau’ti  bhavaṃ kac‍cānau āha| kittāvatā nu khau, bhau kac‍cāna, guttadvārau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati, appamāṇacaitasau tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai  nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati, appamāṇacaitasau tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, guttadvārau hautī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna; abbhutaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna! yāvañ‍cidaṃ  bhautā kac‍cānaina guttadvārauva samānau guttadvārauti akkhātau| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna; abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna! saiyyathāpi, bhau kac‍cāna, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā kac‍cānaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhau kac‍cāna, taṃ bhagavantaṃ  saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ kac‍cānau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| yathā ca bhavaṃ kac‍cānau mak‍karakatai upāsakakulāni upasaṅkamati; aivamaiva lauhic‍cakulaṃ upasaṅkamatu| tattha yai māṇavakā vā māṇavikā vā bhavantaṃ kac‍cānaṃ abhivādaissanti pac‍cuṭṭhissanti āsanaṃ vā udakaṃ vā dassanti, taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vairahac‍cānisuttaṃ

133. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā udāyī kāmaṇḍāyaṃ viharati taudaiyyassa brāhmaṇassa ambavanai| atha khau vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā antaivāsī māṇavakau yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā udāyinā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ māṇavakaṃ  āyasmā udāyī dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau sau māṇavakau āyasmatā udāyinā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vairahac‍cānigauttaṃ brāhmaṇiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yagghai, bhauti, jānaiyyāsi bhauti jānaiyya (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhautī jānaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰)! samaṇau udāyī dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitī’’ti|

‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, māṇavaka, mama vacanaina samaṇaṃ udāyiṃ nimantaihi svātanāya bhattainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhautī’’ti khau  sau māṇavakau vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā paṭissutvā yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu kira, bhavaṃ, udāyi, amhākaṃ ācariyabhariyāya vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā svātanāya bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā udāyī tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau āyasmā udāyī tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ pādukā ārauhitvā uc‍cai āsanai nisīditvā sīsaṃ auguṇṭhitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhaṇa, samaṇa, dhamma’’nti| ‘‘bhavissati, bhagini, samayau’’ti vatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kami pak‍kāmi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

dutiyampi khau sau māṇavakau yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā udāyinā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ māṇavakaṃ āyasmā udāyī dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| dutiyampi khau sau māṇavakau āyasmatā udāyinā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau  sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  vairahac‍cānigauttaṃ brāhmaṇiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yagghai, bhauti, jānaiyyāsi! samaṇau udāyī dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ pana tvaṃ, māṇavaka, samaṇassa udāyissa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsasi| samaṇau panudāyī ‘bhaṇa, samaṇa, dhamma’nti vuttau samānau ‘bhavissati, bhagini, samayau’ti vatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kantau’’ti| ‘‘tathā hi pana tvaṃ, bhauti, pādukā ārauhitvā uc‍cai āsanai nisīditvā sīsaṃ auguṇṭhitvā aitadavauca – ‘bhaṇa, samaṇa, dhamma’nti| dhammagarunau hi tai bhavantau dhammagāravā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, māṇavaka, mama vacanaina samaṇaṃ udāyiṃ nimantaihi svātanāya bhattainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhautī’’ti khau sau māṇavakau vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā paṭissutvā yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu kira  bhavaṃ udāyī amhākaṃ ācariyabhariyāya vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā svātanāya bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā udāyī tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau āyasmā udāyī tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina vairahac‍cānigauttāya brāhmaṇiyā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī āyasmantaṃ  udāyiṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ pādukā aurauhitvā nīcai āsanai nisīditvā sīsaṃ vivaritvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhantai, sati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, kismiṃ asati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ na pañ‍ñapaintī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhusmiṃ khau, bhagini, sati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, cakkhusmiṃ asati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ  na pañ‍ñapainti…pai॰… jivhāya sati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, jivhāya asati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ na pañ‍ñapainti…pai॰…| manasmiṃ sati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, manasmiṃ asati arahantau sukhadukkhaṃ na pañ‍ñapaintī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, vairahac‍cānigauttā brāhmaṇī āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai; abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ ayyaina udāyinā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, ayya udāyi, taṃ bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsikaṃ maṃ ayyau udāyī dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dasamaṃ|

gahapativaggau tairasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

vaisālī  vaj‍ji nāḷandā, bhāradvāja sauṇau ca ghausitau|

hāliddikau nakulapitā, lauhic‍cau vairahac‍cānīti||

14. daivadahavaggau

1. daivadahasuttaṃ

134. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati daivadahaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbaisaṃyaiva bhikkhūnaṃ chasu phassāyatanaisu appamādaina karaṇīyanti vadāmi, na ca panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbaisaṃyaiva bhikkhūnaṃ chasu phassāyatanaisu  nāppamādaina karaṇīyanti vadāmi| yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, taisāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ chasu phassāyatanaisu nāppamādaina karaṇīyanti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? kataṃ taisaṃ appamādaina, abhabbā tai pamaj‍jituṃ| yai ca khau tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saikkhā saikhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, taisāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ chasu phassāyatanaisu appamādaina karaṇīyanti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā manauramāpi, amanauramāpi| tyāssa phussa phussa cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau apariyādānā āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā apammuṭṭhā (sī॰), appamuṭṭhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appamādaphalaṃ  sampassamānau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ chasu  phassāyatanaisu appamādaina karaṇīyanti vadāmi…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manauramāpi amanauramāpi| tyāssa phussa phussa cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau apariyādānā āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, appamādaphalaṃ sampassamānau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ chasu chassu (sī॰) phassāyatanaisu appamādaina karaṇīyanti vadāmī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. khaṇasuttaṃ

135. ‘‘lābhā  vau, bhikkhavai, suladdhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau vau paṭiladdhau brahmacariyavāsāya| diṭṭhā mayā, bhikkhavai, chaphassāyatanikā nāma nirayā| tattha yaṃ kiñ‍ci cakkhunā  rūpaṃ passati aniṭṭharūpaṃyaiva passati, nau iṭṭharūpaṃ; akantarūpaṃyaiva passati, nau kantarūpaṃ; amanāparūpaṃyaiva passati, nau manāparūpaṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sautaina saddaṃ suṇāti…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyati…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci jivhāya rasaṃ sāyati…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusati…pai॰… yaṃ  kiñ‍ci manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti aniṭṭharūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau iṭṭharūpaṃ; akantarūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau kantarūpaṃ; amanāparūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau manāparūpaṃ| lābhā vau, bhikkhavai, suladdhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau vau paṭiladdhau brahmacariyavāsāya| diṭṭhā mayā, bhikkhavai, chaphassāyatanikā nāma saggā| tattha yaṃ kiñ‍ci cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati iṭṭharūpaṃyaiva passati, nau aniṭṭharūpaṃ; kantarūpaṃyaiva passati, nau akantarūpaṃ; manāparūpaṃyaiva passati, nau amanāparūpaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci jivhāya rasaṃ sāyati…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti iṭṭharūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau aniṭṭharūpaṃ; kantarūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau akantarūpaṃ; manāparūpaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau amanāparūpaṃ| lābhā vau, bhikkhavai, suladdhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau vau paṭiladdhau brahmacariyavāsāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamarūpārāmasuttaṃ

136. ‘‘rūpārāmā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā rūparatā rūpasammuditā| rūpavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā dukkhā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā viharanti| saddārāmā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā saddaratā saddasammuditā| saddavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā dukkhā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā viharanti| gandhārāmā… rasārāmā… phauṭṭhabbārāmā… dhammārāmā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā  dhammaratā dhammasammuditā| dhammavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā dukkhā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā viharanti| tathāgatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  rūpānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavaṃ ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā na rūpārāmau na rūparatau na rūpasammuditau| rūpavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā sukhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau viharati| saddānaṃ… gandhānaṃ… rasānaṃ… phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… dhammānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā na dhammārāmau, na dhammaratau, na dhammasammuditau| dhammavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā sukhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau viharati’’| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘rūpā  saddā rasā gandhā, phassā dhammā ca kaivalā|

iṭṭhā kantā manāpā ca, yāvatatthīti vuc‍cati||

‘‘sadaivakassa laukassa, aitai vau sukhasammatā|

yattha caitai nirujjhanti, taṃ taisaṃ dukkhasammataṃ||

‘‘sukhaṃ sukhanti (sī॰) diṭṭhamariyaibhi, sak‍kāyassa niraudhanaṃ|

pac‍canīkamidaṃ hauti, sabbalaukaina passataṃ||

‘‘yaṃ parai sukhatau āhu, tadariyā āhu dukkhatau|

yaṃ parai dukkhatau āhu, tadariyā sukhatau vidū||

‘‘passa dhammaṃ durājānaṃ, sammūḷhaittha aviddasu|

nivutānaṃ tamau hauti, andhakārau apassataṃ||

‘‘satañ‍ca  vivaṭaṃ hauti, ālaukau passatāmi|

santikai na vijānanti, maggā magā (sī॰) dhammassa akauvidā||

‘‘bhavarāgaparaitaibhi, bhavarāgānusārībhi bhavasautānusāribhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhavasautānusārihi (sī॰)|

māradhaiyyānupan‍naihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘‘kau  nu añ‍ñatra mariyaibhi, padaṃ sambuddhumarahati|

yaṃ padaṃ sammadañ‍ñāya, parinibbanti anāsavā’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyarūpārāmasuttaṃ

137. ‘‘rūpārāmā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā rūparatā rūpasammuditā| rūpavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā dukkhā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā viharanti| saddārāmā… gandhārāmā… rasārāmā … phauṭṭhabbārāmā… dhammārāmā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā dhammaratā dhammasammuditā| dhammavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā dukkhā, bhikkhavai, daivamanussā viharanti| tathāgatau ca, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau rūpānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā na rūpārāmau na rūparatau na rūpasammuditau| rūpavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā sukhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau viharati| saddānaṃ… gandhānaṃ… rasānaṃ… phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… dhammānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā na dhammārāmau na dhammaratau na dhammasammuditau| dhammavipariṇāmavirāganiraudhā sukhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau viharatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamanatumhākaṃsuttaṃ

138. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ; taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati…pai॰…  jivhā na tumhākaṃ; taṃ pajahatha| sā vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati…pai॰… manau na tumhākaṃ; taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnau hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ imasmiṃ jaitavanai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ taṃ janau haraiyya vā ḍahaiyya vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaiyya, api nu tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘amhai janau harati vā ḍahati vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karautī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘na hi nau aitaṃ, bhantai, attā vā attaniyaṃ vā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhu na tumhākaṃ; taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati…pai॰… jivhā na tumhākaṃ; taṃ pajahatha| sā vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati…pai॰… manau na tumhākaṃ; taṃ pajahatha| sau vau pahīnau hitāya sukhāya bhavissatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyanatumhākaṃsuttaṃ

139. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? rūpā, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ; tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā  na tumhākaṃ; tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ imasmiṃ jaitavanai…pai॰… aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, rūpā na tumhākaṃ; tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ajjhattānic‍cahaitusuttaṃ

140. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau cakkhussa uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, cakkhu kutau nic‍caṃ bhavissati…pai॰…  jivhā anic‍cā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau jivhāya uppādāya saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtā, bhikkhavai, jivhā kutau nic‍cā bhavissati…pai॰… manau anic‍cau| yaupi, bhikkhavai, haitu yaupi pac‍cayau manassa uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtau, bhikkhavai, manau kutau nic‍cau bhavissati! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… jivhāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ajjhattadukkhahaitusuttaṃ

141. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau cakkhussa uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, cakkhu kutau sukhaṃ bhavissati…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau jivhāya uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtā, bhikkhavai, jivhā kutau sukhā bhavissati…pai॰… manau dukkhau| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau manassa uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtau, bhikkhavai, manau kutau sukhau bhavissati! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ajjhattānattahaitusuttaṃ

142. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau cakkhussa uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, cakkhu kutau attā bhavissati…pai॰… jivhā anattā| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau jivhāya uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtā, bhikkhavai, jivhā kutau attā bhavissati…pai॰… manau anattā| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau manassa uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtau, bhikkhavai, manau kutau attā bhavissati! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bāhirānic‍cahaitusuttaṃ

143. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau rūpānaṃ uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtā, bhikkhavai, rūpā kutau nic‍cā bhavissanti! saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anic‍cā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau dhammānaṃ uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtā, bhikkhavai, dhammā kutau nic‍cā bhavissanti! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. bāhiradukkhahaitusuttaṃ

144. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau rūpānaṃ uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtā, bhikkhavai, rūpā kutau sukhā bhavissanti! saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā dukkhā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau dhammānaṃ uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtā, bhikkhavai, dhammā kutau sukhā bhavissanti! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. bāhirānattahaitusuttaṃ

145. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau rūpānaṃ uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtā, bhikkhavai, rūpā kutau attā bhavissanti! saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau dhammānaṃ uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtā, bhikkhavai, dhammā  kutau attā bhavissanti! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpaisupi nibbindati, saddaisupi… gandhaisupi… rasaisupi… phauṭṭhabbaisupi… dhammaisupi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

daivadahavaggau cuddasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

daivadahau khaṇau rūpā, dvai natumhākamaiva ca|

haitunāpi tayau vuttā, duvai ajjhattabāhirāti||

15. navapurāṇavaggau

1. kammaniraudhasuttaṃ

146. ‘‘navapurāṇāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni daisaissāmi kammaniraudhaṃ kammaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmīti| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purāṇakammaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, purāṇakammaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ vaidaniyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā purāṇakammā abhisaṅkhatā abhisañ‍caitayitā vaidaniyā daṭṭhabbā…pai॰… manau purāṇakammau abhisaṅkhatau abhisañ‍caitayitau vaidaniyau daṭṭhabbau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, purāṇakammaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, navakammaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aitarahi  kammaṃ karauti kāyaina vācāya manasā, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, navakammaṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kammaniraudhau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, kāyakammavacīkammamanaukammassa niraudhā vimuttiṃ  phusati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaniraudhau| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, kammaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ mayā purāṇakammaṃ, daisitaṃ  navakammaṃ, daisitau kammaniraudhau, daisitā kammaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| yaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchāvippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anic‍canibbānasappāyasuttaṃ

147. ‘‘nibbānasappāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhuṃ anic‍canti passati, rūpā anic‍cāti passati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti passati, cakkhusamphassau anic‍cauti passati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti passati…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cāti passati, rasā anic‍cāti passati, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti passati, jivhāsamphassau anic‍cauti passati, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti passati…pai॰… manau anic‍cauti passati, dhammā anic‍cāti passati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti passati, manausamphassau anic‍cauti passati, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti passati| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dukkhanibbānasappāyasuttaṃ

148. ‘‘nibbānasappāyaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, cakkhuṃ dukkhanti passati, rūpā dukkhāti passati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhanti passati, cakkhusamphassau dukkhauti passati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhanti passati…pai॰… jivhā dukkhāti passati…pai॰… manau dukkhauti passati, dhammā dukkhāti passati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhanti passati, manausamphassau dukkhauti passati, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi  dukkhanti passati| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. anattanibbānasappāyasuttaṃ

149. ‘‘nibbānasappāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhuṃ anattāti passati, rūpā anattāti passati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattāti passati, cakkhusamphassau anattāti passati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā  uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattāti passati…pai॰… manau anattāti passati, dhammā anattāti passati, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattāti passati, manausamphassau anattāti passati, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ  vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattāti passati| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. nibbānasappāyapaṭipadāsuttaṃ

150. ‘‘nibbānasappāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā? taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, cakkhu nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰…|

‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… cakkhusamphassau…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati  …pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, nibbānasappāyā paṭipadā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. antaivāsikasuttaṃ

151. ‘‘anantaivāsikamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussati anācariyakaṃ| santaivāsikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu phāsuṃ (sī॰ pī॰) viharati| anantaivāsikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anācariyakau sukhaṃ phāsu viharati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, santaivāsikau sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tyāssa antau vasanti, antassa vasanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā santaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ samudācaranti, samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tyāssa antau vasanti, antassa vasanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā santaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ samudācaranti, samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tyāssa antau vasanti, antassa vasanti  pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā santaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ samudācaranti, samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santaivāsikau sācariyakau dukkhaṃ, na phāsu viharati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anantaivāsikau anācariyakau sukhaṃ phāsu viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tyāssa na antau vasanti, nāssa antau vasanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā anantaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ na samudācaranti, na samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā anācariyakauti vuc‍cati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā na uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tyāssa na antau vasanti, nāssa  antau vasanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā anantaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ na samudācaranti, na samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā anācariyakauti vuc‍cati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā| tyāssa na antau vasanti, nāssa antau vasanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā anantaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| tai naṃ na samudācaranti, na samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā anācariyakauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anantaivāsikau anācariyakau sukhaṃ phāsu viharati| anantaivāsikamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussati| anācariyakaṃ santaivāsikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sācariyakau dukkhaṃ, na phāsu viharati| anantaivāsikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anācariyakau sukhaṃ phāsu viharatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kimatthiyabrahmacariyasuttaṃ

152. ‘‘sacai vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘dukkhassa khau, āvusau, pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| sacai pana vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaṃ panāvusau, dukkhaṃ, yassa pariñ‍ñāya samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha –

‘‘cakkhu khau, āvusau, dukkhaṃ; tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| rūpā dukkhā; taisaṃ pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ; tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| cakkhusamphassau dukkhau; tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ; tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā… manau dukkhau; tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ  vā tampi dukkhaṃ; tassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| idaṃ khau, āvusau, dukkhaṃ; yassa pariñ‍ñāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. atthinukhaupariyāyasuttaṃ

153. ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma bhikkhu añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya, añ‍ñatra ruciyā, añ‍ñatra anussavā, añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā, añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā  añ‍ñaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti pajānātīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā, bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma bhikkhu añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya, añ‍ñatra ruciyā, añ‍ñatra anussavā, añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā, añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā añ‍ñaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau, yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma bhikkhu añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya…pai॰… añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā añ‍ñaṃ  byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti| api numai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhāya vā vaiditabbā, ruciyā vā vaiditabbā, anussavaina vā vaiditabbā, ākāraparivitak‍kaina vā vaiditabbā, diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā vā vaiditabbā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nanumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāya disvā vaiditabbā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma bhikkhu añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya, añ‍ñatra ruciyā, añ‍ñatra anussavā, añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā, añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ…pai॰… rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; api numai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhāya vā vaiditabbā, ruciyā vā vaiditabbā, anussavaina vā vaiditabbā, ākāraparivitak‍kaina vā vaiditabbā, diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā vā vaiditabbā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nanumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāya disvā vaiditabbā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma bhikkhu añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya, añ‍ñatra ruciyā, añ‍ñatra anussavā, añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā, añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti pajānātīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) …pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti| yaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu manasā  dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, atthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhaṃ, natthi mai ajjhattaṃ rāgadausamauhauti pajānāti; api numai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhāya vā vaiditabbā, ruciyā vā vaiditabbā, anussavaina  vā vaiditabbā, ākāraparivitak‍kaina vā vaiditabbā, diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā vā vaiditabbā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nanumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāya disvā vaiditabbā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma bhikkhu  añ‍ñatraiva saddhāya, añ‍ñatra ruciyā, añ‍ñatra anussavā, añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā, añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. indriyasampan‍nasuttaṃ

154. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘indriyasampan‍nau, indriyasampan‍nau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, indriyasampan‍nau hautī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhundriyai cai, bhikkhu, udayabbayānupassī viharantau cakkhundriyai nibbindati…pai॰… jivhindriyai cai, bhikkhu, udayabbayānupassī viharantau jivhindriyai nibbindati…pai॰… manindriyai cai, bhikkhu, udayabbayānupassī viharantau manindriyai nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati…pai॰… vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhu, indriyasampan‍nau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dhammakathikapucchasuttaṃ

155. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhammakathikau, dhammakathikau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhammakathikau hautī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhussa  cai, bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāya| cakkhussa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāya| cakkhussa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāya…pai॰… jivhāya cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāya…pai॰… manassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāya| manassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāya| manassa  cai, bhikkhu, nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃvacanāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

navapurāṇavaggau pañ‍cadasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kammaṃ cattāri sappāyā, anantaivāsi kimatthiyā|

atthi nu khau pariyāyau, indriyakathikaina cāti||

saḷāyatanavaggai tatiyapaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa vagguddānaṃ –

yaugakkhaimi  ca laukau ca, gahapati daivadahaina ca|

navapurāṇaina paṇṇāsau, tatiyau taina vuc‍catīti||

16. nandikkhayavaggau

1. ajjhattanandikkhayasuttaṃ

156. ‘‘anic‍caṃyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhuṃ anic‍canti passati, sāssa sāyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati…pai॰… anic‍caṃyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jivhaṃ anic‍canti passati, sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā…pai॰… cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati…pai॰… anic‍caṃyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu manaṃ anic‍canti passati, sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍catī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. bāhiranandikkhayasuttaṃ

157. ‘‘anic‍caiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpai anic‍cāti passati, sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| anic‍caiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… dhammai anic‍cāti passati, sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍catī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ajjhattaanic‍canandikkhayasuttaṃ

158. ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha; cakkhānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ  samanupassatha| cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasikarauntau, cakkhānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| sautaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha… ghānaṃ… jivhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha; jivhānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| jivhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasikarauntau, jivhānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau jivhāyapi nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| kāyaṃ… manaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha; manānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| manaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasikarauntau, manānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau manasmimpi nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍catī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bāhiraanic‍canandikkhayasuttaṃ

159. ‘‘rūpai, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha; rūpānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| rūpai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasikarauntau, rūpānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau rūpaisupi nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| saddai… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai… dhammai, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha; dhammānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| dhammai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasikarauntau, dhammānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau dhammaisupi nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau; rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍catī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. jīvakambavanasamādhisuttaṃ

160. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati jīvakambavanai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| samāhitassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati? cakkhuṃ anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, rūpā anic‍cāti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, cakkhusamphassau anic‍cauti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cāti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati…pai॰… manau anic‍cauti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, dhammā anic‍cāti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati| samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| samāhitassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. jīvakambavanapaṭisal‍lānasuttaṃ

161. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati jīvakambavanai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… ‘‘paṭisal‍lānai, bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līnassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati? cakkhuṃ anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, rūpā anic‍cāti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti  yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, cakkhusamphassau anic‍cauti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati…pai॰… manau anic‍cauti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati, dhammā… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā  adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyati| paṭisal‍lānai bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līnassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yathābhūtaṃ aukkhāyatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kauṭṭhikaanic‍casuttaṃ

162. atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kauṭṭhikau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|
