‘‘yaṃ khau, kauṭṭhika, anic‍caṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, kauṭṭhika, anic‍caṃ? cakkhu khau, kauṭṭhika, anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| rūpā anic‍cā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| cakkhusamphassau anic‍cau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| rasā  anic‍cā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| jivhāsamphassau anic‍cau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yampidaṃ  jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau anic‍cau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| dhammā anic‍cā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| manausamphassau anic‍cau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yaṃ khau, kauṭṭhika, anic‍caṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. kauṭṭhikadukkhasuttaṃ

163. atha  khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai…pai॰… viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, kauṭṭhika, dukkhaṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, kauṭṭhika, dukkhaṃ? cakkhu khau, kauṭṭhika, dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| rūpā dukkhā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| cakkhusamphassau dukkhau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau dukkhau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| dhammā dukkhā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| manausamphassau dukkhau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yaṃ khau, kauṭṭhika, dukkhaṃ tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kauṭṭhikaanattasuttaṃ

164. aikamantaṃ…pai॰… viharaiyyanti| ‘‘yau khau, kauṭṭhika, anattā tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| kau ca, kauṭṭhika, anattā? cakkhu khau, kauṭṭhika, anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| rūpā anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| cakkhusamphassau anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau  …pai॰… jivhā anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| dhammā anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassau… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā  uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| yau khau, kauṭṭhika, anattā, tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. micchādiṭṭhipahānasuttaṃ

165. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyatī’’ti?

‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anic‍catau jānatau passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyati| rūpai anic‍catau jānatau passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍catau jānatau passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyati| cakkhusamphassaṃ anic‍catau jānatau passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍catau jānatau passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau micchādiṭṭhi pahīyatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. sak‍kāyadiṭṭhipahānasuttaṃ

166. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyatī’’ti? ‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, dukkhatau jānatau passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyati| rūpai dukkhatau jānatau passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhatau jānatau passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyati| cakkhusamphassaṃ dukkhatau jānatau passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi dukkhatau jānatau passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pahīyatī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. attānudiṭṭhipahānasuttaṃ

167. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aitadavauca  – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyatī’’ti? ‘‘cakkhuṃ khau, bhikkhu, anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati| rūpai anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati| cakkhusamphassaṃ anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati| yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati…pai॰… jivhaṃ anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati…pai॰… manaṃ anattatau  jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyati| dhammai… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manausamphassaṃ… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anattatau jānatau passatau attānudiṭṭhi pahīyatī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

nandikkhayavaggau sauḷasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nandikkhayaina cattārau, jīvakambavanai duvai|

kauṭṭhikaina tayau vuttā, micchā sak‍kāya attanauti||

17. saṭṭhipaiyyālavaggau

1. ajjhattaanic‍cachandasuttaṃ

168. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau anic‍cau; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau’’ti|

2. ajjhattaanic‍carāgasuttaṃ

169. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau anic‍cau; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

3. ajjhattaanic‍cachandarāgasuttaṃ

170. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau anic‍cau; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

4-6. dukkhachandādisuttaṃ

171-173. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā…pai॰…  manau dukkhau; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

7-9. anattachandādisuttaṃ

174-176. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, anattā, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, bhikkhavai, anattā? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… jivhā anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… manau anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| yau, bhikkhavai, anattā tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

10-12. bāhirānic‍cachandādisuttaṃ

177-179. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau  pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| saddā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| gandhā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau  pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| rasā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| phauṭṭhabbā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| dhammā anic‍cā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

13-15. bāhiradukkhachandādisuttaṃ

180-182. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ? rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā dukkhā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

16-18. bāhirānattachandādisuttaṃ

183-185. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, anattā, tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, bhikkhavai, anattā? rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| yau, bhikkhavai, anattā  tatra vau chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

19. ajjhattātītānic‍casuttaṃ

186. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ atītaṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā atītā…pai॰… manau anic‍cau atītau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… jivhāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… manasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

20. ajjhattānāgatānic‍casuttaṃ

187. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ anāgataṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā anāgatā…pai॰… manau anic‍cau anāgatau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

21. ajjhattapac‍cuppan‍nānic‍casuttaṃ

188. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā pac‍cuppan‍nā…pai॰… manau anic‍cau pac‍cuppan‍nau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

22-24. ajjhattātītādidukkhasuttaṃ

189-191. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ atītaṃ anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā…pai॰… manau dukkhau atītau anāgatau pac‍cuppan‍nau| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

25-27. ajjhattātītādianattasuttaṃ

192-194. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anattā atītaṃ anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… jivhā anattā…pai॰… manau anattā atītau anāgatau pac‍cuppan‍nau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

28-30. bāhirātītādianic‍casuttaṃ

195-197. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anic‍cā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

31-33. bāhirātītādidukkhasuttaṃ

198-200. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā dukkhā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

34-36. bāhirātītādianattasuttaṃ

201-203. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

37. ajjhattātītayadanic‍casuttaṃ

204. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ atītaṃ| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ  dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā atītā| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… manau anic‍cau atītau| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

38. ajjhattānāgatayadanic‍casuttaṃ

205. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ anāgataṃ| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā anāgatā| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… manau anic‍cau anāgatau| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

39. ajjhattapac‍cuppan‍nayadanic‍casuttaṃ

206. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… manau anic‍cau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

40-42. ajjhattātītādiyaṃdukkhasuttaṃ

207-209. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ atītaṃ anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| yaṃ  dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā…pai॰… manau dukkhau atītau anāgatau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

43-45. ajjhattātītādiyadanattasuttaṃ

210-212. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anattā atītaṃ anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… jivhā anattā…pai॰… manau anattā atītau anāgatau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

46-48. bāhirātītādiyadanic‍casuttaṃ

213-215. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anic‍cā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ  itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

49-51. bāhirātītādiyaṃdukkhasuttaṃ

216-218. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā dukkhā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

52-54. bāhirātītādiyadanattasuttaṃ

219-221. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā atītā anāgatā pac‍cuppan‍nā| yadanattā, taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

55. ajjhattāyatanaanic‍casuttaṃ

222. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ…pai॰… jivhā anic‍cā…pai॰… manau anic‍cau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

56. ajjhattāyatanadukkhasuttaṃ

223. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ…pai॰… jivhā dukkhā…pai॰… manau dukkhau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

57. ajjhattāyatanaanattasuttaṃ

224. ‘‘cakkhu, bhikkhavai, anattā…pai॰… jivhā anattā…pai॰… manau anattā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

58. bāhirāyatanaanic‍casuttaṃ

225. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anic‍cā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

59. bāhirāyatanadukkhasuttaṃ

226. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, dukkhā| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā dukkhā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

60. bāhirāyatanaanattasuttaṃ

227. ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anattā| saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā anattā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

saṭṭhipaiyyālavaggau sattarasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

chandainaṭṭhārasa haunti, atītaina ca dvai nava|

yadanic‍cāṭṭhārasa vuttā, tayau ajjhattabāhirā|

paiyyālau saṭṭhikau vuttau, buddhainādic‍cabandhunāti||

suttantāni saṭṭhi|

18. samuddavaggau

1. paṭhamasamuddasuttaṃ

228. ‘‘‘samuddau, samuddau’ti, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati| naisau, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai samuddau| mahā aisau, bhikkhavai, udakarāsi mahāudakaṇṇavau| cakkhu, bhikkhavai, purisassa samuddau; tassa rūpamayau vaigau| yau taṃ rūpamayaṃ vaigaṃ sahati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atari cakkhusamuddaṃ saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sagāhaṃ sarakkhasaṃ; tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau  thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau…pai॰… jivhā, bhikkhavai, purisassa samuddau; tassa rasamayau vaigau| yau taṃ rasamayaṃ vaigaṃ sahati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atari jivhāsamuddaṃ saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sagāhaṃ sarakkhasaṃ; tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau…pai॰… manau, bhikkhavai, purisassa samuddau; tassa dhammamayau vaigau| yau taṃ dhammamayaṃ vaigaṃ sahati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atari manausamuddaṃ saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sagāhaṃ sarakkhasaṃ; tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau’’ti| idamavauca…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘yau imaṃ samuddaṃ sagāhaṃ sarakkhasaṃ,

saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sabhayaṃ duttaraṃ ac‍catari|

sa vaidagū vusitabrahmacariyau,

laukantagū pāragatauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasamuddasuttaṃ

229. ‘‘samuddau, samuddau’ti, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati| naisau, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai samuddau| mahā aisau, bhikkhavai, udakarāsi mahāudakaṇṇavau| santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā  iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai samuddau| aitthāyaṃ sadaivakau laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā yaibhuyyaina samun‍nā tantākulakajātā kulagaṇṭhikajātā guḷāguṇṭhikajātā (sī॰), kulaguṇḍikajātā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), guṇaguṇikajātā (pī॰), kulāguṇḍikajātā (ka॰) muñ‍japabbajabhūtā, apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattati…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai samuddau| aitthāyaṃ sadaivakau laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā yaibhuyyaina samun‍nā tantākulakajātā kulagaṇṭhikajātā muñ‍japabbajabhūtā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattatī’’ti|

‘‘yassa  rāgau ca dausau ca, avij‍jā ca virājitā|

sau imaṃ samuddaṃ sagāhaṃ sarakkhasaṃ, saūmibhayaṃ duttaraṃ ac‍catari||

‘‘saṅgātigau mac‍cujahau nirupadhi, pahāsi dukkhaṃ apunabbhavāya|

atthaṅgatau sau na punaiti na pamāṇamaiti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), amauhayī, mac‍curājanti brūmī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. bāḷisikaupamasuttaṃ

230. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, bāḷisikau āmisagatabaḷisaṃ gambhīrai udakarahadai pakkhipaiyya| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau  āmisacakkhu macchau gilaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, macchau gilitabaḷisau bāḷisikassa anayaṃ  āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau bāḷisikassa|

aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, chayimai baḷisā laukasmiṃ anayāya sattānaṃ vadhāya byābādhāya (sī॰ pī॰) pāṇinaṃ| katamai cha? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai, bhikkhu, abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilitabaḷisau, mārassa anayaṃ āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰… santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰…|

santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai, bhikkhu, abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilitabaḷisau mārassa anayaṃ āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau|

‘‘santi  ca, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na gilitabaḷisau mārassa abhaidi baḷisaṃ paribhaidi baḷisaṃ na anayaṃ āpan‍nau na byasanaṃ āpan‍nau na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau…pai॰…|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰…| santi, bhikkhavai, manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya  tiṭṭhati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na gilitabaḷisau mārassa abhaidi baḷisaṃ paribhaidi baḷisaṃ na anayaṃ āpan‍nau na byasanaṃ āpan‍nau na yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. khīrarukkhaupamasuttaṃ

231. ‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā  cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu yau rāgau sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau tassa parittā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti pariyādiyantaivassa cittaṃ; kau pana vādau adhimattānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau, sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu yau rāgau sau atthi…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu yau rāgau sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau, tassa parittā caipi manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti pariyādiyantaivassa cittaṃ; kau pana vādau adhimattānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau, sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau  atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, khīrarukkhau assatthau vā nigraudhau vā pilakkhau vā udumbarau vā daharau taruṇau kaumārakau| tamainaṃ purisau tiṇhāya kuṭhāriyā yatau yatau ābhindaiyya bhindaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰), abhindaiyya (katthaci) āgacchaiyya  khīra’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘yañhi, bhantai, khīraṃ taṃ atthī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu yau rāgau sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau, tassa parittā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti pariyādiyantaivassa cittaṃ; kau pana vādau adhimattānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu yau rāgau sau atthi…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu yau rāgau sau atthi, yau dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau, tassa parittā caipi manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti pariyādiyantaivassa cittaṃ; kau pana vādau adhimattānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau sau atthi, yau  dausau sau atthi, yau mauhau sau atthi, yau rāgau sau appahīnau, yau dausau sau appahīnau, yau mauhau sau appahīnau|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu yau rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau  pahīnau, tassa adhimattā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; kau pana vādau parittānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa  kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu yau rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau, tassa adhimattā caipi manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; kau pana vādau parittānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, khīrarukkhau assatthau vā nigraudhau vā pilakkhau vā udumbarau vā sukkhau kaulāpau tairauvassikau| tamainaṃ purisau tiṇhāya kuṭhāriyā yatau yatau ābhindaiyya āgacchaiyya khīra’’nti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘yañhi, bhantai, khīraṃ taṃ natthī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu yau rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau, tassa adhimattā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; kau pana vādau parittānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu…pai॰…|

‘‘yassa  kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu yau rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau, tassa adhimattā caipi manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; kau pana vādau parittānaṃ! taṃ kissa haitu? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgau sau natthi, yau dausau sau natthi, yau mauhau sau natthi, yau rāgau sau pahīnau, yau dausau sau pahīnau, yau mauhau sau pahīnau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

232. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… jivhā rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, rasā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ …pai॰… manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, dhammā manassa saṃyaujana’’nti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhā rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rasā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kāḷau ca balībaddau balivaddau (sī॰ pī॰), balibaddau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) audātau ca balībaddau aikaina dāmaina vā yauttaina vā saṃyuttā assu| yau nu khau aivaṃ  vadaiyya – ‘kāḷau balībaddau audātassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ, audātau balībaddau kāḷassa balībaddassa saṃyaujana’nti, sammā nu khau sau vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘na khau, āvusau, kāḷau balībaddau audātassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ, na audātau balībaddau kāḷassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ| yaina ca khau tai aikaina dāmaina vā yauttaina vā saṃyuttā taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, āvusau, na cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhā rasānaṃ  saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ|

‘‘cakkhu vā, āvusau, rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ abhavissa, rūpā vā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ, nayidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha pañ‍ñāyati (ka॰) sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, āvusau, na cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā  dukkhakkhayāya…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhā, āvusau, rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ abhavissa, rasā vā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ, nayidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, āvusau, na jivhā rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rasā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya…pai॰…|

‘‘manau vā, āvusau, dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ abhavissa, dhammā vā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ, nayidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, āvusau, na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘imināpaitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā na cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhā rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ|

‘‘saṃvij‍jati khau, āvusau, bhagavatau cakkhu| passati bhagavā cakkhunā rūpaṃ| chandarāgau bhagavatau natthi| suvimuttacittau bhagavā| saṃvij‍jati khau, āvusau, bhagavatau sautaṃ| suṇāti bhagavā sautaina saddaṃ| chandarāgau  bhagavatau natthi| suvimuttacittau bhagavā| saṃvij‍jati khau, āvusau, bhagavatau ghānaṃ| ghāyati bhagavā ghānaina gandhaṃ| chandarāgau bhagavatau natthi| suvimuttacittau  bhagavā| saṃvij‍jati khau, āvusau, bhagavatau jivhā| sāyati bhagavā jivhāya rasaṃ| chandarāgau bhagavatau natthi| suvimuttacittau bhagavā| saṃvij‍jati khau, āvusau, bhagavatau kāyau| phusati bhagavā kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ| chandarāgau bhagavatau natthi| suvimuttacittau bhagavā| saṃvij‍jati khau, āvusau, bhagavatau manau| vijānāti bhagavā manasā  dhammaṃ| chandarāgau bhagavatau natthi| suvimuttacittau bhagavā|

‘‘iminā khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā na cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| na sautaṃ… na ghānaṃ… na jivhā rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rasā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| na kāyau… na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujana’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kāmabhūsuttaṃ

233. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca kāmabhū kausambiyaṃ viharanti ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā kāmabhū sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ  kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kāmabhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau ānanda, cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… jivhā rasānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, rasā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, dhammā manassa saṃyaujana’’nti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau kāmabhū kāmabhu (sī॰) mauggal‍lānai 65-gai vāti suttaṃ passitabbaṃ, cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhā rasānaṃ  saṃyaujanaṃ, na rasā jivhāya saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ| yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kāḷau ca balībaddau audātau ca balībaddau aikaina dāmaina vā yauttaina vā saṃyuttā assu| yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kāḷau balībaddau audātassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ, audātau balībaddau kāḷassa balībaddassa saṃyaujana’nti, sammā nu khau sau vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘na khau, āvusau, kāḷau balībaddau audātassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ, napi audātau balībaddau kāḷassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ| yaina ca khau tai aikaina dāmaina vā yauttaina vā saṃyuttā, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, na cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhā…pai॰… na manau…pai॰… yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujana’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. udāyīsuttaṃ

234. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca udāyī kausambiyaṃ viharanti ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā udāyī sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yathaiva  nu khau, āvusau ānanda, ayaṃ kāyau bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina akkhātau vivaṭau pakāsitau – ‘itipāyaṃ kāyau anattā’ti, sak‍kā aivamaiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pidaṃ ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ – ‘itipidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā’’’ti?

‘‘yathaiva khau, āvusau udāyī, ayaṃ kāyau bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina akkhātau vivaṭau pakāsitau – ‘itipāyaṃ kāyau anattā’ti, sak‍kā aivamaiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pidaṃ ācikkhituṃ  daisaituṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ – ‘itipidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā’’’ti|

‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca, āvusau, paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇa’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘yau cāvusau, haitu, yau ca pac‍cayau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, sau ca haitu, sau ca pac‍cayau sabbaina sabbaṃ  sabbathā sabbaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhaiyya| api nu khau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina bhagavatā akkhātaṃ vivaṭaṃ pakāsitaṃ – ‘itipidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā’’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘jivhañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇa’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘yau cāvusau, haitu yau ca pac‍cayau jivhāviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, sau ca haitu, sau ca pac‍cayau sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhaiyya, api nu khau jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina bhagavatā akkhātaṃ vivaṭaṃ pakāsitaṃ – ‘itipidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā’’’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘manañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇa’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘yau cāvusau, haitu, yau ca pac‍cayau manauviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, sau ca haitu, sau ca pac‍cayau sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhaiyya, api nu khau manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina bhagavatā akkhātaṃ vivaṭaṃ pakāsitaṃ – ‘itipidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ ādāya vanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau tattha  passaiyya mahantaṃ kadalikkhandhaṃ ujuṃ navaṃ akuk‍kukajātaṃ akuk‍kuṭakajātaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), akuk‍kajaṭajātaṃ (ka॰)| tamainaṃ mūlai chindaiyya; mūlai chaitvā aggai chindaiyya; aggai chaitvā pattavaṭṭiṃ vinibbhujaiyya vinibbhuj‍jaiyya (pī॰), vinibbhaj‍jaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| sau tattha phaiggumpi nādhigacchaiyya, kutau sāraṃ! aivamaiva khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chasu phassāyatanaisu naivattānaṃ na attaniyaṃ samanupassati| sau aivaṃ asamanupassantau aivaṃ samanupassantau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) na kiñ‍ci laukai  upādiyati| anupādiyaṃ na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ādittapariyāyasuttaṃ

235. ‘‘ādittapariyāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ādittapariyāyau, dhammapariyāyau? varaṃ, bhikkhavai, tattāya ayausalākāya ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya sañ‍jautibhūtāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) cakkhundriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ, na tvaiva cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu anubyañ‍janasau nimittaggāhau| nimittassādagathitaṃ nimittassādagadhitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.316-317 vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, anubyañ‍janassādagathitaṃ vā tasmiñ‍cai samayai kālaṃ karaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ gacchaiyya – nirayaṃ vā, tiracchānayauniṃ vā| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavaṃ disvā aivaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiṇhaina ayausaṅkunā ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina sautindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ, na tvaiva sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu saddaisu anubyañ‍janasau nimittaggāhau| nimittassādagathitaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, anubyañ‍janassādagathitaṃ vā tasmiñ‍cai samayai kālaṅkaraiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ gacchaiyya – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavaṃ disvā aivaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiṇhaina nakhacchaidanaina ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina ghānindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ, na tvaiva ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu gandhaisu anubyañ‍janasau nimittaggāhau| nimittassādagathitaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, anubyañ‍janassādagathitaṃ vā tasmiñ‍cai samayai kālaṃ karaiyya| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ gacchaiyya – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavaṃ disvā aivaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiṇhaina khuraina ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina jivhindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ, na tvaiva jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu anubyañ‍janasau nimittaggāhau| nimittassādagathitaṃ  vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, anubyañ‍janassādagathitaṃ vā tasmiñ‍cai samayai kālaṃ karaiyya| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ gacchaiyya – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavaṃ disvā aivaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiṇhāya sattiyā ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya kāyindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ, na tvaiva kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu phauṭṭhabbaisu anubyañ‍janasau nimittaggāhau| nimittassādagathitaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, anubyañ‍janassādagathitaṃ vā tasmiñ‍cai  samayai kālaṃ karaiyya| ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ gacchaiyya – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavaṃ disvā aivaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, sauttaṃ| sauttaṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vañjhaṃ jīvitānaṃ vadāmi, aphalaṃ jīvitānaṃ vadāmi, maumūhaṃ jīvitānaṃ vadāmi, na tvaiva tathārūpai vitak‍kai vitak‍kaiyya yathārūpānaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ vasaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ bhindaiyya| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vañjhaṃ jīvitānaṃ ādīnavaṃ disvā aivaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘tattha, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘tiṭṭhatu tāva tattāya ayausalākāya ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya cakkhundriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ| handāhaṃ idamaiva manasi karaumi – iti cakkhu anic‍caṃ, rūpā anic‍cā, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, cakkhusamphassau anic‍cau, yampidaṃ cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ’’’ anic‍ca’’nti (?)|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu  tāva tiṇhaina ayausaṅkunā ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina sautindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ| handāhaṃ idamaiva manasi karaumi – iti sautaṃ anic‍caṃ, saddā anic‍cā, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, sautasamphassau anic‍cau, yampidaṃ sautasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu  tāva tiṇhaina nakhacchaidanaina ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina ghānindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ| handāhaṃ idamaiva manasi karaumi – iti  ghānaṃ anic‍caṃ, gandhā anic‍cā, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, ghānasamphassau anic‍cau, yampidaṃ ghānasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ…pai॰… tampi anic‍caṃ|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu tāva tiṇhaina khuraina ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina jivhindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ| handāhaṃ idamaiva manasi karaumi – iti jivhā anic‍cā, rasā anic‍cā, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, jivhāsamphassau anic‍cau, yampidaṃ jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati…pai॰… tampi anic‍caṃ|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu tāva tiṇhāya sattiyā ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya kāyindriyaṃ sampalimaṭṭhaṃ| handāhaṃ idamaiva manasi karaumi – iti kāyau anic‍cau, phauṭṭhabbā anic‍cā, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, kāyasamphassau anic‍cau, yampidaṃ kāyasamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ…pai॰… tampi anic‍caṃ|

‘‘tiṭṭhatu tāva sauttaṃ| handāhaṃ idamaiva manasi karaumi – iti manau anic‍cau, dhammā anic‍cā, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, manausamphassau anic‍cau, yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tampi anic‍caṃ’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati, rūpaisupi nibbindati, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaipi nibbindati, cakkhusamphassaipi nibbindati…pai॰… yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā tasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ  karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ādittapariyāyau, dhammapariyāyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamahatthapādaupamasuttaṃ

236. ‘‘hatthaisu, bhikkhavai, sati ādānanikkhaipanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati; pādaisu sati abhik‍kamapaṭik‍kamau pañ‍ñāyati; pabbaisu sati samiñ‍janapasāraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyati; kucchismiṃ sati jighacchā pipāsā pañ‍ñāyati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusmiṃ sati cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… jivhāya sati jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… manasmiṃ sati manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘hatthaisu, bhikkhavai, asati ādānanikkhaipanaṃ na pañ‍ñāyati; pādaisu asati abhik‍kamapaṭik‍kamau na pañ‍ñāyati; pabbaisu asati samiñ‍janapasāraṇaṃ na pañ‍ñāyati; kucchismiṃ asati jighacchā pipāsā na pañ‍ñāyati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusmiṃ asati cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā  nuppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… jivhāya asati jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā nuppaj‍jati…pai॰… manasmiṃ asati manausamphassapac‍cayā nuppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkha’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyahatthapādaupamasuttaṃ

237. ‘‘hatthaisu, bhikkhavai, sati ādānanikkhaipanaṃ hauti; pādaisu sati abhik‍kamapaṭik‍kamau hauti; pabbaisu sati samiñ‍janapasāraṇaṃ hauti; kucchismiṃ sati jighacchā pipāsā hauti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusmiṃ sati cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰…  jivhāya sati…pai॰… manasmiṃ sati manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘hatthaisu, bhikkhavai, asati ādānanikkhaipanaṃ na hauti; pādaisu asati abhik‍kamapaṭik‍kamau na hauti; pabbaisu asati samiñ‍janapasāraṇaṃ na hauti; kucchismiṃ asati jighacchā pipāsā na hauti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusmiṃ asati cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā nuppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ…pai॰… jivhāya asati jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā  nuppaj‍jati…pai॰… manasmiṃ asati manausamphassapac‍cayā nuppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhaṃ dukkha’’nti| dasamaṃ|

samuddavaggau aṭṭharasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  samuddā bāḷisikau, khīrarukkhaina kauṭṭhikau|

kāmabhū udāyī caiva, ādittaina ca aṭṭhamaṃ|

hatthapādūpamā dvaiti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

19. āsīvisavaggau

1. āsīvisaupamasuttaṃ

238. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cattārau āsīvisā uggataijā ghauravisā| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘imai tai, ambhau purisa, cattārau āsīvisā uggataijā ghauravisā kālaina  kālaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitabbā, kālaina kālaṃ nhāpaitabbā, kālaina kālaṃ bhaujaitabbā, kālaina kālaṃ saṃvaisaitabbā pavaisaitabbā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)| yadā ca khau tai, ambhau purisa, imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ uggataijānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ añ‍ñatarau vā añ‍ñatarau vā kuppissati, tatau tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchasi, maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| yaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, karaṇīyaṃ taṃ karauhī’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ uggataijānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ yaina vā taina vā palāyaitha| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘imai khau, ambhau purisa, pañ‍ca vadhakā pac‍catthikā piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā, yatthaiva naṃ passissāma tatthaiva jīvitā vauraupaissāmāti| yaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, karaṇīyaṃ taṃ karauhī’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ uggataijānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ, bhītau pañ‍can‍naṃ vadhakānaṃ pac‍catthikānaṃ yaina vā  taina vā palāyaitha| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, chaṭṭhau antaracarau vadhakau ukkhittāsikau piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhau yatthaiva naṃ passissāmi tatthaiva sirau pātaissāmīti| yaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, karaṇīyaṃ taṃ karauhī’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ uggataijānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ, bhītau pañ‍can‍naṃ vadhakānaṃ pac‍catthikānaṃ, bhītau chaṭṭhassa antaracarassa vadhakassa ukkhittāsikassa yaina vā taina vā palāyaitha| sau passaiyya suñ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ| yañ‍ñadaiva gharaṃ pavisaiyya rittakañ‍ñaiva pavisaiyya tucchakañ‍ñaiva pavisaiyya suñ‍ñakañ‍ñaiva pavisaiyya| yañ‍ñadaiva bhājanaṃ parimasaiyya rittakañ‍ñaiva parimasaiyya tucchakañ‍ñaiva parimasaiyya suñ‍ñakañ‍ñaiva parimasaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘idāni, ambhau purisa, imaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ caurā gāmaghātakā pavisanti vadhissanti (sī॰ pī॰)| yaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, karaṇīyaṃ taṃ karauhī’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ uggataijānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ, bhītau pañ‍can‍naṃ vadhakānaṃ pac‍catthikānaṃ, bhītau chaṭṭhassa antaracarassa vadhakassa ukkhittāsikassa, bhītau caurānaṃ gāmaghātakānaṃ yaina vā taina vā palāyaitha| sau passaiyya mahantaṃ udakaṇṇavaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ sāsaṅkaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ, pārimaṃ tīraṃ khaimaṃ appaṭibhayaṃ| na cassa nāvā santāraṇī uttarasaitu vā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya| atha  khau, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau mahāudakaṇṇavau aurimaṃ tīraṃ sāsaṅkaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ, pārimaṃ tīraṃ khaimaṃ appaṭibhayaṃ, natthi ca na cassa (sī॰ ka॰), natthassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāvā santāraṇī uttarasaitu vā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya| yaṃnūnāhaṃ tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ saṃkaḍḍhitvā kul‍laṃ bandhitvā taṃ kul‍laṃ nissāya hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyamamānau sautthinā pāraṃ gacchaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ saṃkaḍḍhitvā kul‍laṃ bandhitvā taṃ kul‍laṃ nissāya hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyamamānau sautthinā pāraṃ gacchaiyya, tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau pāragatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau|

‘‘upamā  khau myāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayañ‍caittha ayaṃ caivaittha (sī॰) atthau – cattārau āsīvisā uggataijā ghauravisāti khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naitaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ adhivacanaṃ – pathavīdhātuyā, āpaudhātuyā, taijaudhātuyā, vāyaudhātuyā|

‘‘pañ‍ca  vadhakā pac‍catthikāti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhassa, vaidanupādānakkhandhassa, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhassa, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhassa, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhassa|

‘‘chaṭṭhau antaracarau vadhakau ukkhittāsikauti khau, bhikkhavai, nandīrāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘suñ‍ñau gāmauti khau, bhikkhavai, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| cakkhutau caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī upaparikkhati rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, tucchakañ‍ñaiva  khāyati, suñ‍ñakañ‍ñaiva  khāyati…pai॰… jivhātau caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī upaparikkhati rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, suñ‍ñakañ‍ñaiva khāyati|

‘‘caurā gāmaghātakāti khau, bhikkhavai, chan‍naitaṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| cakkhu, bhikkhavai, hañ‍ñati manāpāmanāpaisu rūpaisu; sautaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ghānaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… jivhā, bhikkhavai, hañ‍ñati manāpāmanāpaisu rasaisu; kāyau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… manau, bhikkhavai, hañ‍ñati manāpāmanāpaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘mahā udakaṇṇavauti khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naitaṃ aughānaṃ adhivacanaṃ – kāmaughassa, bhavaughassa, diṭṭhaughassa, avij‍jaughassa|

‘‘aurimaṃ tīraṃ sāsaṅkaṃ sappaṭibhayanti khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘pārimaṃ tīraṃ khaimaṃ appaṭibhayanti khau, bhikkhavai, nibbānassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘kul‍lanti  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyassaitaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘tassa hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyāmauti khau, bhikkhavai, vīriyārambhassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇauti khau, bhikkhavai, arahatau aitaṃ adhivacana’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rathaupamasuttaṃ

239. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhasaumanassabahulau viharati, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamaihi tīhi? indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti, nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ| tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, subhūmiyaṃ cātumahāpathai ājañ‍ñarathau yuttau assa ṭhitau audhastapataudau audhatapataudau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), audhasatapataudau (pī॰)| tamainaṃ dakkhau yauggācariyau assadammasārathi abhiruhitvā vāmaina hatthaina rasmiyau gahaitvā, dakkhiṇaina hatthaina pataudaṃ gahaitvā, yainicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ sāraiyyapi pac‍cāsāraiyyapi| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ ārakkhāya  sikkhati, saṃyamāya sikkhati, damāya sikkhati, upasamāya sikkhati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya, na madāya, na maṇḍanāya, na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā, yāpanāya, vihiṃsūparatiyā, brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati, anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau vaṇaṃ ālimpaiyya  yāvadaiva rauhanatthāya raupanatthāya (sī॰ pī॰), saivanatthāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), gaupanatthāya (ka॰), saiyyathā vā pana akkhaṃ abbhañ‍jaiyya yāvadaiva bhārassa nittharaṇatthāya; aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya, na madāya, na maṇḍanāya, na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā, yāpanāya, vihiṃsūparatiyā, brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati, anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ  kappaiti pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhasaumanassabahulau viharati, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kummaupamasuttaṃ

240. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, kummau kacchapau sāyanhasamayaṃ anunadītīrai gaucarapasutau ahausi| siṅgālaupi sigālaupi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khau, bhikkhavai, sāyanhasamayaṃ anunadītīrai gaucarapasutau ahausi| addasā  khau, bhikkhavai, kummau kacchapau siṅgālaṃ dūratauva gaucarapasutaṃ| disvāna sauṇḍipañ‍camāni aṅgāni  sakai kapālai samaudahitvā appaussuk‍kau tuṇhībhūtau saṅkasāyati| siṅgālaupi khau, bhikkhavai, addasa kummaṃ kacchapaṃ dūratauva gaucarapasutaṃ| disvāna yaina kummau kacchapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kummaṃ kacchapaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau ahausi – ‘yadāyaṃ kummau kacchapau sauṇḍipañ‍camānaṃ aṅgānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā aṅgaṃ abhinin‍nāmaissati, tatthaiva naṃ gahaitvā uddālitvā khādissāmī’ti| yadā khau, bhikkhavai, kummau kacchapau  sauṇḍipañ‍camānaṃ aṅgānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā aṅgaṃ na abhinin‍nāmi, atha siṅgālau kummamhā nibbij‍ja pak‍kāmi, autāraṃ alabhamānau|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi mārau pāpimā satataṃ samitaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau – ‘appaiva nāmāhaṃ imaisaṃ cakkhutau  vā autāraṃ labhaiyyaṃ…pai॰… jivhātau vā autāraṃ labhaiyyaṃ…pai॰… manatau vā autāraṃ labhaiyya’nti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, indriyaisu guttadvārā viharatha| cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā mā nimittaggāhinau ahuvattha, mā anubyañ‍janaggāhinau| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jatha, rakkhatha cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatha| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya mā nimittaggāhinau ahuvattha, mā anubyañ‍janaggāhinau| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jatha, rakkhatha manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatha| yatau tumhai, bhikkhavai, indriyaisu guttadvārā viharissatha, atha tumhaihipi mārau pāpimā nibbij‍ja pak‍kamissati, autāraṃ alabhamānau – kummamhāva siṅgālau’’ti|

‘‘kummau  aṅgāni sakai kapālai,

samaudahaṃ bhikkhu manauvitak‍kai|

anissitau añ‍ñamahaiṭhayānau,

parinibbutau nūpavadaiyya kañ‍cī’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamadārukkhandhaupamasuttaṃ

241. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati gaṅgāya nadiyā tīrai| addasā khau bhagavā mahantaṃ dārukkhandhaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā sautaina vuyhamānaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, amuṃ  mahantaṃ dārukkhandhaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā sautaina vuyhamāna’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sacai sau, bhikkhavai, dārukkhandhau na aurimaṃ tīraṃ upagacchati, na pārimaṃ tīraṃ upagacchati, na majjhai saṃsīdissati, na thalai ussīdissati, na manussaggāhau gahaissati, na  amanussaggāhau gahaissati, na āvaṭṭaggāhau gahaissati, na antaupūti bhavissati; aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, dārukkhandhau samuddanin‍nau bhavissati samuddapauṇau samuddapabbhārau| taṃ kissa haitu? samuddanin‍nau, bhikkhavai, gaṅgāya nadiyā sautau samuddapauṇau samuddapabbhārau|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sacai tumhaipi na aurimaṃ tīraṃ upagacchatha, na pārimaṃ tīraṃ upagacchatha; na majjhai saṃsīdissatha, na thalai ussīdissatha, na manussaggāhau gahaissati, na amanussaggāhau gahaissati, na āvaṭṭaggāhau gahaissati, na antaupūtī bhavissatha; aivaṃ tumhai, bhikkhavai, nibbānanin‍nā bhavissatha nibbānapauṇā nibbānapabbhārā| taṃ kissa haitu? nibbānanin‍nā, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi nibbānapauṇā nibbānapabbhārā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ, kiṃ pārimaṃ tīraṃ, kau majjhai saṃsādau saṃsīdau (ka॰), saṃsīditau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), kau thalai ussādau, kau manussaggāhau, kau amanussaggāhau, kau āvaṭṭaggāhau, kau antaupūtibhāvau’’ti?

‘‘‘aurimaṃ tīra’nti khau, bhikkhu, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘pārimaṃ tīra’nti khau, bhikkhu, chan‍naitaṃ bāhirānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘majjhai saṃsādau’ti khau, bhikkhu, nandīrāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘thalai ussādau’ti khau, bhikkhu, asmimānassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhu, manussaggāhau? idha, bhikkhu, gihīhi saṃsaṭṭhau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau (ka॰) viharati, sahanandī  sahasaukī, sukhitaisu sukhitau, dukkhitaisu dukkhitau, uppan‍naisu kic‍cakaraṇīyaisu attanā taisu yaugaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, manussaggāhau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhu, amanussaggāhau? idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, amanussaggāhau| ‘āvaṭṭaggāhau’ti khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhu, antaupūtibhāvau? idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau asucisaṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau  samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī  brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, ‘antaupūtibhāvau’’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina nandau gaupālakau bhagavatau avidūrai ṭhitau hauti| atha khau nandau gaupālakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, na aurimaṃ tīraṃ upagacchāmi, na pārimaṃ tīraṃ upagacchāmi, na majjhai saṃsīdissāmi, na  thalai ussīdissāmi, na maṃ manussaggāhau gahaissati, na amanussaggāhau gahaissati, na āvaṭṭaggāhau gahaissati, na antaupūti bhavissāmi| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, nanda, sāmikānaṃ gāvau niyyātaihī’’ti nīyyādaihīti (sī॰), niyyādaihīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| ‘‘gamissanti, bhantai, gāvau vacchagiddhiniyau’’ti| ‘‘niyyātaihaiva tvaṃ, nanda, sāmikānaṃ gāvau’’ti| atha khau nandau gaupālakau sāmikānaṃ gāvau niyyātaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘niyyātitā niyyātā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰), bhantai, sāmikānaṃ gāvau| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| alattha khau nandau gaupālakau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau ca panāyasmā nandau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā nandau arahataṃ ahausīti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyadārukkhandhaupamasuttaṃ

242. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kimilāyaṃ kimbilāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), kimmilāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharati gaṅgāya nadiyā tīrai| addasā khau bhagavā mahantaṃ dārukkhandhaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā sautaina vuyhamānaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, amuṃ mahantaṃ  dārukkhandhaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā sautaina vuyhamāna’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’…pai॰… aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā kimilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, aurimaṃ tīraṃ…pai॰… katamau ca, kimila, antaupūtibhāvau| idha, kimila, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ  āpan‍nau hauti yathārūpāya āpattiyā na vuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kimila, antaupūtibhāvauti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. avassutapariyāyasuttaṃ

243. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ navaṃ santhāgāraṃ sandhāgāraṃ (ka॰) acirakāritaṃ hauti anajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ anajjhāvutthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā kainaci vā manussabhūtaina| atha khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ navaṃ santhāgāraṃ acirakāritaṃ acirakāritaṃ hauti (ka॰) anajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā kainaci vā manussabhūtaina| taṃ, bhantai, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ paribhuñ‍jatu| bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ paribhuttaṃ pacchā kāpilavatthavā sakyā paribhuñ‍jissanti| tadassa kāpilavatthavānaṃ sakyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina navaṃ santhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sabbasanthariṃ sabbasanthariṃ santhataṃ (ka॰) santhāgāraṃ santharitvā āsanāni pañ‍ñāpaitvā udakamaṇikaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā tailappadīpaṃ āraupaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sabbasantharisanthataṃ sabbasanthariṃ santhataṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhantai, santhāgāraṃ, āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni, udakamaṇikau patiṭṭhāpitau, tailappadīpau  āraupitau| yassa dāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina navaṃ santhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pādai pakkhālaitvā santhāgāraṃ pavisitvā majjhimaṃ thambhaṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisīdi| bhikkhusaṅghaupi khau pādai pakkhālaitvā santhāgāraṃ pavisitvā pacchimaṃ bhittiṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisīdi bhagavantaṃyaiva purakkhatvā| kāpilavatthavā sakyā pādai pakkhālaitvā santhāgāraṃ pavisitvā puratthimaṃ bhittiṃ nissāya pacchimābhimukhā nisīdiṃsu bhagavantaṃyaiva purakkhatvā| atha khau bhagavā kāpilavatthavai sakyai bahudaiva rattiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uyyaujaisi – ‘‘abhik‍kantā khau, gautamā, ratti| yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñathā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantaisu kāpilavatthavaisu sakyaisu āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘vigatathinamiddhau khau, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhusaṅghau| paṭibhātu taṃ, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammī kathā| piṭṭhi mai āgilāyati; tamahaṃ āyamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi, pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya, satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| tatra khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca – ‘‘avassutapariyāyañ‍ca vau, āvusau, daisaissāmi, anavassutapariyāyañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘kathaṃ, āvusau, avassutau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati viharati parittacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti …pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati  parittacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, āvusau, bhikkhu avassutau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu…pai॰… avassutau jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu…pai॰… avassutau manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃvihāriñ‍cāvusau, bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati  labhataiva mārau autāraṃ, labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhātau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhataiva labhaitha (ka॰) mārau autāraṃ, labhati labhaitha (ka॰) mārau ārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhataiva mārau autāraṃ, labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, naḷāgāraṃ vā tiṇāgāraṃ vā sukkhaṃ kaulāpaṃ tairauvassikaṃ| puratthimāya caipi naṃ disāya purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, labhaithaiva labhaitha (ka॰) aggi autāraṃ, labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ; pacchimāya caipi naṃ disāya purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi naṃ disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi naṃ disāya…pai॰… haiṭṭhimatau caipi naṃ…pai॰… uparimatau caipi naṃ… yatau kutauci caipi naṃ purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, labhaithaiva aggi autāraṃ labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, aivaṃvihāriṃ bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhataiva mārau autāraṃ, labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhātau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhataiva mārau autāraṃ, labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| aivaṃvihāriñ‍cāvusau, bhikkhuṃ rūpā adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu rūpai adhibhausi; saddā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu  saddai adhibhausi; gandhā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu gandhai adhibhausi; rasā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu rasai adhibhausi; phauṭṭhabbā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu phauṭṭhabbai adhibhausi; dhammā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu dhammai adhibhausi| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu rūpādhibhūtau, saddādhibhūtau, gandhādhibhūtau, rasādhibhūtau, phauṭṭhabbādhibhūtau, dhammādhibhūtau, adhibhūtau, anadhibhū, anadhibhūtau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) adhibhaṃsu naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, avassutau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, anavassutau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… manasā  dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu anavassutau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu…pai॰… anavassutau manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃvihāriñ‍cāvusau, bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhati mārau autāraṃ, na labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhātau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati…pai॰… manatau caipi  naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhati mārau autāraṃ, na labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kūṭāgāraṃ vā sālā vā bahalamattikā  addāvalaipanā| puratthimāya caipi naṃ disāya purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, naiva labhaitha aggi autāraṃ, na labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… pacchimāya caipi naṃ… uttarāya caipi naṃ… dakkhiṇāya caipi naṃ… haiṭṭhimatau caipi naṃ… uparimatau caipi naṃ… yatau kutauci caipi naṃ purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, naiva labhaitha aggi autāraṃ, na labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, aivaṃvihāriṃ bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhati mārau autāraṃ, na labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhati mārau autāraṃ, na labhati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| aivaṃvihārī cāvusau, bhikkhu rūpai adhibhausi, na rūpā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; saddai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na saddā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; gandhai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na gandhā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; rasai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na rasā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; phauṭṭhabbai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na phauṭṭhabbā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; dhammai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na dhammā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu rūpādhibhū, saddādhibhū, gandhādhibhū, rasādhibhū, phauṭṭhabbādhibhū, dhammādhibhū, adhibhū, anadhibhūtau anadhibhūtau kaihici kilaisaihi (ka॰), adhibhausi tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai saṃkilaisikai paunaubbhavikai sadarai  dukkhavipākai āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyai| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anavassutau hautī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā vuṭṭhahitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mauggal‍lāna! sādhu khau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhūnaṃ avassutapariyāyañ‍ca anavassutapariyāyañ‍ca abhāsī’’ti|

idamavauca āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā  ahausi| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dukkhadhammasuttaṃ

244. yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbaisaṃyaiva dukkhadhammānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tathā khau panassa kāmā diṭṭhā haunti, yathāssa kāmai passatau, yau kāmaisu kāmacchandau kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapariḷāhau, sau nānusaiti| tathā khau panassa cārau ca vihārau ca anubuddhau hauti, yathā carantaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā nānusainti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbaisaṃyaiva dukkhadhammānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbaisaṃyaiva dukkhadhammānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmā diṭṭhā haunti? yathāssa kāmai  passatau, yau kāmaisu kāmacchandau kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapariḷāhau, sau nānusaiti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aṅgārakāsu sādhikapaurisā puṇṇā aṅgārānaṃ vītac‍cikānaṃ vītadhūmānaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhapaṭikūlau| tamainaṃ dvai balavantau purisā nānābāhāsu gahaitvā, taṃ aṅgārakāsuṃ upakaḍḍhaiyyuṃ| sau iticīticaiva kāyaṃ san‍nāmaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? ñāta ñāṇaṃ (ka॰) ñhi, bhikkhavai, tassa  purisassa purisassa hauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), purisassa haitu hauti (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.45 imaṃ cāhaṃ aṅgārakāsuṃ papatissāmi, tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchissāmi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā diṭṭhā haunti, yathāssa kāmai passatau, yau kāmaisu kāmacchandau kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapariḷāhau, sau nānusaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cārau ca vihārau ca anubuddhau hauti, yathā carantaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā nānussavanti nānusainti (ka॰)? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau bahukaṇṭakaṃ dāyaṃ pavisaiyya| tassa purataupi kaṇṭakau, pacchataupi kaṇṭakau, uttarataupi kaṇṭakau, dakkhiṇataupi kaṇṭakau, haiṭṭhataupi kaṇṭakau, uparitaupi kaṇṭakau| sau satauva abhik‍kamaiyya, satauva paṭik‍kamaiyya – ‘mā maṃ kaṇṭakau’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ariyassa vinayai kaṇṭakau’’ti| iti viditvā kaṇḍakau| taṃ kaṇḍakauti iti viditvā (sī॰) saṃvarau ca asaṃvarau ca  vaiditabbau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṃvarau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati parittacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati parittacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asaṃvarau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃvarau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā  akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti…pai॰… jivhā rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā  akusalā dhammā  aparisaisā nirujjhanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saṃvarau hauti|

‘‘tassa  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃ caratau aivaṃ viharatau kadāci karahaci satisammausā uppaj‍janti, pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā, dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau| atha khau naṃ khippamaiva pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau divasaṃsantattai divasasantattai (sī॰) ayaukaṭāhai dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni nipātaiyya| dandhau, bhikkhavai, udakaphusitānaṃ nipātau, atha khau naṃ khippamaiva parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tassa cai bhikkhunau aivaṃ caratau, aivaṃ viharatau kadāci karahaci satisammausā uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujaniyā, dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau| atha khau naṃ khippamaiva pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cārau ca vihārau ca anubuddhau hauti; yathā carantaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā nānussavanti| tañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ carantaṃ aivaṃ viharantaṃ rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā, bhaugaihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyuṃ – ‘aihi aivaṃ (sī॰), bhau purisa, kiṃ tai imai kāsāvā anudahanti, kiṃ muṇḍau kapālamanucarasi, aihi hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu, puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’ti| sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  aivaṃ carantau aivaṃ viharantau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā| atha mahājanakāyau āgacchaiyya kuddāla-piṭakaṃ ādāya – ‘mayaṃ imaṃ gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ pacchānin‍naṃ karissāma pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāra’nti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu khau sau mahājanakāyau gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ pacchānin‍naṃ karaiyya pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāra’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘gaṅgā, bhantai, nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; sā na sukarā pacchānin‍nā kātuṃ pacchāpauṇā pacchāpabbhārā| yāvadaiva ca pana sau mahājanakāyau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tañ‍cai bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ carantaṃ aivaṃ viharantaṃ rājānau vā rājamahāmattā  vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā bhaugaihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyuṃ – ‘aihi, bhau purisa, kiṃ tai imai kāsāvā anudahanti, kiṃ muṇḍau kapālamanucarasi, aihi hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu, puñ‍ñāni  ca karauhī’ti| sau vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ carantau aivaṃ viharantau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ vivaikanin‍naṃ vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ, tathā kañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) hīnāyāvattissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. kiṃsukaupamasuttaṃ

245. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yainañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca  atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu asantuṭṭhau tassa bhikkhussa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina pañhāvaiyyākaraṇaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), yainañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu asantuṭṭhau tassa bhikkhussa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yainañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu asantuṭṭhau tassa bhikkhussa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yainañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā  nu khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ  niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā, khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu asantuṭṭhau tassa bhikkhussa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, yainañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucaṃ – kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sau bhikkhu maṃ aitadavauca – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’ti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, asantuṭṭhau tassa bhikkhussa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yainañ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sau bhikkhu maṃ aitadavauca – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ…pai॰… catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’ti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, asantuṭṭhau tassa bhikkhussa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃ ( ) (upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ) (ka॰)| kittāvatā  nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhunau dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hautī’’ti?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, purisassa kiṃsukau adiṭṭhapubbau assa| sau yainañ‍ñatarau purisau kiṃsukassa dassāvī tainupasaṅkamaiyya| upasaṅkamitvā taṃ purisaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kīdisau, bhau purisa, kiṃsukau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kāḷakau khau, ambhau purisa, kiṃsukau – saiyyathāpi jhāmakhāṇū’ti| taina khau pana, bhikkhu, samayaina tādisauvassa kiṃsukau yathāpi yathā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) dutiyavārādīsu pana ‘‘yathāpi’’tvaiva dissati tassa purisassa dassanaṃ| atha khau, sau bhikkhu, purisau asantuṭṭhau tassa purisassa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yainañ‍ñatarau purisau kiṃsukassa dassāvī tainupasaṅkamaiyya; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ purisaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kīdisau, bhau purisa, kiṃsukau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘lauhitakau khau, ambhau purisa, kiṃsukau – saiyyathāpi maṃsapaisī’ti| taina khau pana, bhikkhu, samayaina tādisauvassa kiṃsukau yathāpi tassa purisassa dassanaṃ| atha khau sau bhikkhu purisau asantuṭṭhau tassa purisassa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yainañ‍ñatarau purisau kiṃsukassa dassāvī tainupasaṅkamaiyya; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ purisaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kīdisau, bhau purisa, kiṃsukau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aucīrakajātau aujīrakajātau (sī॰), audīrakajātau (pī॰) khau, ambhau purisa, kiṃsukau ādin‍nasipāṭikau – saiyyathāpi sirīsau’ti| taina khau pana, bhikkhu, samayaina tādisauvassa kiṃsukau, yathāpi tassa purisassa dassanaṃ| atha khau sau bhikkhu purisau asantuṭṭhau tassa purisassa pañhavaiyyākaraṇaina, yainañ‍ñatarau purisau kiṃsukassa dassāvī tainupasaṅkamaiyya; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ  purisaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kīdisau, bhau purisa, kiṃsukau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘bahalapattapalāsau sandacchāyau saṇḍacchāyau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau, ambhau purisa, kiṃsukau – saiyyathāpi nigraudhau’ti| taina khau pana, bhikkhu, samayaina tādisauvassa kiṃsukau, yathāpi tassa purisassa dassanaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhu, yathā yathā adhimuttānaṃ taisaṃ sappurisānaṃ dassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ hauti, tathā tathā khau taihi sappurisaihi byākataṃ|
