‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ daḷhuddhāpaṃ daḷhuddāpaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) daḷhapākāratauraṇaṃ chadvāraṃ| tatrassa dauvārikau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī, añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā, ñātānaṃ pavaisaitā| puratthimāya disāya āgantvā sīghaṃ dūtayugaṃ taṃ dauvārikaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kahaṃ, bhau purisa, imassa nagarassa nagarassāmī’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aisau, bhantai, majjhai siṅghāṭakai nisin‍nau’ti| atha khau taṃ sīghaṃ dūtayugaṃ nagarassāmikassa yathābhūtaṃ vacanaṃ niyyātaitvā yathāgatamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya| pacchimāya disāya āgantvā sīghaṃ dūtayugaṃ…pai॰… uttarāya disāya… dakkhiṇāya disāya āgantvā sīghaṃ dūtayugaṃ taṃ dauvārikaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kahaṃ, bhau purisa, imassa nagarassāmī’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aisau, bhantai, majjhai siṅghāṭakai nisin‍nau’ti| atha khau taṃ sīghaṃ dūtayugaṃ nagarassāmikassa yathābhūtaṃ vacanaṃ niyyātaitvā yathāgatamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘upamā  khau myāyaṃ, bhikkhu, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayañ‍caittha atthau  – ‘nagara’nti khau, bhikkhu, imassaitaṃ cātumahābhūtikassa kāyassa adhivacanaṃ mātāpaittikasambhavassa audanakummāsūpacayassa anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammassa| ‘cha dvārā’ti khau, bhikkhu, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘dauvārikau’ti khau, bhikkhu, satiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘sīghaṃ  dūtayuga’nti khau, bhikkhu, samathavipassanānaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘nagarassāmī’ti khau, bhikkhu, viñ‍ñāṇassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘majjhai siṅghāṭakau’ti khau, bhikkhu, catun‍naitaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ adhivacanaṃ – pathavīdhātuyā, āpaudhātuyā, taijaudhātuyā, vāyaudhātuyā| ‘yathābhūtaṃ vacana’nti khau, bhikkhu, nibbānassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘yathāgatamaggau’ti khau, bhikkhu, ariyassaitaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhiyā…pai॰… sammāsamādhissā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vīṇaupamasuttaṃ

246. ‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu uppaj‍jaiyya chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi paṭighaṃ vā (sī॰) caitasau, tatau cittaṃ nivāraiyya| sabhayau caisau maggau sappaṭibhayau ca sakaṇṭakau ca sagahanau ca ummaggau ca kummaggau ca duhitikau ca| asappurisasaivitau caisau maggau, na caisau maggau sappurisaihi saivitau| na tvaṃ aitaṃ arahasīti| tatau cittaṃ nivārayai cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi…pai॰… yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rasaisu…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu uppaj‍jaiyya chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā  mauhau vā paṭighaṃ vāpi caitasau tatau cittaṃ nivāraiyya| sabhayau caisau maggau sappaṭibhayau ca sakaṇṭakau ca sagahanau ca ummaggau ca kummaggau ca duhitikau ca| asappurisasaivitau caisau maggau, na caisau maggau sappurisaihi saivitau| na tvaṃ aitaṃ arahasīti| tatau cittaṃ nivārayai manauviñ‍ñaiyyaihi dhammaihi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kiṭṭhaṃ sampan‍naṃ| kiṭṭhārakkhau kiṭṭhārakkhakau (sī॰) ca pamattau, gauṇau ca kiṭṭhādau aduṃ kiṭṭhaṃ autaritvā  yāvadatthaṃ madaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya pamādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau chasu phassāyatanaisu asaṃvutakārī pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu yāvadatthaṃ madaṃ āpaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kiṭṭhaṃ sampan‍naṃ kiṭṭhārakkhau ca appamattau gauṇau ca kiṭṭhādau aduṃ kiṭṭhaṃ autaraiyya| tamainaṃ kiṭṭhārakkhau nāsāyaṃ suggahitaṃ gaṇhaiyya| nāsāyaṃ suggahitaṃ gahaitvā uparighaṭāyaṃ suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhaiyya| uparighaṭāyaṃ suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā daṇḍaina sutāḷitaṃ tāḷaiyya| daṇḍaina sutāḷitaṃ tāḷaitvā ausaj‍jaiyya| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai  …pai॰… tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, gauṇau kiṭṭhādau aduṃ kiṭṭhaṃ autaraiyya| tamainaṃ kiṭṭhārakkhau nāsāyaṃ suggahitaṃ gaṇhaiyya| nāsāyaṃ suggahitaṃ gahaitvā uparighaṭāyaṃ suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhaiyya| uparighaṭāyaṃ suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā daṇḍaina sutāḷitaṃ tāḷaiyya| daṇḍaina sutāḷitaṃ tāḷaitvā ausaj‍jaiyya| aivañhi  sau, bhikkhavai, gauṇau kiṭṭhādau gāmagatau vā arañ‍ñagatau vā, ṭhānabahulau vā assa nisaj‍jabahulau vā na taṃ kiṭṭhaṃ puna autaraiyya – tamaiva purimaṃ daṇḍasamphassaṃ samanussarantau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yatau khau bhikkhunau chasu phassāyatanaisu cittaṃ udujitaṃ hauti sudujitaṃ, ajjhattamaiva santiṭṭhati, san‍nisīdati, aikaudi hauti, samādhiyati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau vā rājamahāmattassa vā vīṇāya saddau assutapubbau assa| sau vīṇāsaddaṃ suṇaiyya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau, kassa kissa (sī॰ pī॰) nu khau aisau saddau aivaṃrajanīyau aivaṃkamanīyau aivaṃmadanīyau  aivaṃmucchanīyau aivaṃbandhanīyau’ti? tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aisā, khau, bhantai, vīṇā nāma, yassā aisau saddau aivaṃrajanīyau aivaṃkamanīyau aivaṃmadanīyau aivaṃmucchanīyau aivaṃbandhanīyau’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha mai, bhau, taṃ vīṇaṃ āharathā’ti| tassa taṃ vīṇaṃ āharaiyyuṃ| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ khau sā, bhantai, vīṇā yassā aisau saddau aivaṃrajanīyau aivaṃkamanīyau aivaṃmadanīyau aivaṃmucchanīyau aivaṃbandhanīyau’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘alaṃ mai, bhau, tāya vīṇāya, tamaiva mai saddaṃ āharathā’ti| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ khau, bhantai, vīṇā nāma anaikasambhārā mahāsambhārā| anaikaihi sambhāraihi samāraddhā vadati, saiyyathidaṃ – dauṇiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca cammañ‍ca paṭic‍ca daṇḍañ‍ca paṭic‍ca upadhāraṇai ca  paṭic‍ca tantiyau ca paṭic‍ca kauṇañ‍ca paṭic‍ca purisassa ca taj‍jaṃ vāyāmaṃ paṭic‍ca aivāyaṃ, bhantai, vīṇā nāma anaikasambhārā mahāsambhārā| anaikaihi sambhāraihi samāraddhā vadatī’ti| sau taṃ vīṇaṃ dasadhā vā satadhā vā phālaiyya, dasadhā vā satadhā vā taṃ phālaitvā sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karaiyya| sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karitvā agginā ḍahaiyya, agginā ḍahitvā masiṃ karaiyya| masiṃ karitvā mahāvātai vā auphunaiyya aupunaiyya (sī॰ pī॰), auphuṇaiyya (?), nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asatī kirāyaṃ, bhau, vīṇā nāma, yathaivaṃ yaṃ yathaivāyaṃ (sī॰), yathaivayaṃ (pī॰) kiñ‍ci vīṇā nāma aittha ca panāyaṃ janau aittha panāyaṃ janau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aittha ca mahājanau (pī॰ ka॰) ativailaṃ pamattau palaḷitau’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpaṃ samanvaisati saman‍naisati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), samanaisati (pī॰) yāvatā rūpassa gati, vaidanaṃ samanvaisati yāvatā vaidanāya gati, sañ‍ñaṃ samanvaisati yāvatā sañ‍ñāya gati, saṅkhārai samanvaisati yāvatā saṅkhārānaṃ gati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanvaisati  yāvatā viñ‍ñāṇassa gati| tassa rūpaṃ samanvaisatau yāvatā rūpassa gati, vaidanaṃ samanvaisatau…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanvaisatau yāvatā viñ‍ñāṇassa gati| yampissa taṃ hauti  ahanti vā mamanti vā asmīti vā tampi tassa na hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. chappāṇakaupamasuttaṃ

247. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau arugattau pak‍kagattau saravanaṃ pavisaiyya| tassa kusakaṇṭakā caiva pādai vijjhaiyyuṃ, sarapattāni ca gattāni  sarapattāni pak‍kagattāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰), arupak‍kāni gattāni (pī॰ ka॰) vilaikhaiyyuṃ| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, purisau bhiyyausaumattāya tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu gāmagatau vā arañ‍ñagatau vā labhati vattāraṃ – ‘ayañ‍ca sau ayañ‍ca khau (pī॰ ka॰), ayaṃ sau (?) āyasmā aivaṃkārī aivaṃsamācārau asucigāmakaṇṭakau’ti| taṃ kaṇṭakauti taṃ ‘‘asucigāmakaṇḍatau’’ti (ka॰) iti viditvā saṃvarau ca asaṃvarau ca vaiditabbau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṃvarau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati parittacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati parittacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau chappāṇakai gahaitvā nānāvisayai nānāgaucarai daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| ahiṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya  raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| susumāraṃ suṃsumāraṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| pakkhiṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| kuk‍kuraṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| siṅgālaṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| mak‍kaṭaṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhitvā majjhai gaṇṭhiṃ karitvā aussaj‍jaiyya| atha khau, tai, bhikkhavai, chappāṇakā nānāvisayā nānāgaucarā sakaṃ sakaṃ gaucaravisayaṃ āviñchaiyyuṃ āviñ‍jaiyyuṃ (sī॰) – ahi āviñchaiyya ‘vammikaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, susumārau āviñchaiyya ‘udakaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, pakkhī āviñchaiyya ‘ākāsaṃ ḍaissāmī’ti, kuk‍kurau āviñchaiyya ‘gāmaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, siṅgālau āviñchaiyya ‘sīvathikaṃ sivathikaṃ (ka॰) pavaikkhāmī’ti, mak‍kaṭau āviñchaiyya ‘vanaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti| yadā khau tai, bhikkhavai, chappāṇakā jhattā assu kilantā, atha khau yau naisaṃ pāṇakānaṃ balavatarau assa tassa tai anuvattaiyyuṃ, anuvidhāyaiyyuṃ vasaṃ gacchaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhunau kāyagatāsati abhāvitā abahulīkatā, taṃ cakkhu āviñchati manāpiyaisu rūpaisu, amanāpiyā rūpā paṭikūlā haunti…pai॰… manau āviñchati manāpiyaisu dhammaisu, amanāpiyā dhammā paṭikūlā haunti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, asaṃvarau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃvarau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā  rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca  viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti…pai॰… jivhā rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai  na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau chappāṇakai gahaitvā nānāvisayai nānāgaucarai daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| ahiṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| susumāraṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| pakkhiṃ gahaitvā…pai॰… kuk‍kuraṃ gahaitvā… siṅgālaṃ gahaitvā… mak‍kaṭaṃ gahaitvā daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhaiyya| daḷhāya raj‍juyā bandhitvā daḷhai khīlai vā thambhai vā upanibandhaiyya| atha khau tai, bhikkhavai, chappāṇakā nānāvisayā nānāgaucarā sakaṃ sakaṃ gaucaravisayaṃ āviñchaiyyuṃ – ahi āviñchaiyya ‘vammikaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, susumārau āviñchaiyya ‘udakaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, pakkhī āviñchaiyya ‘ākāsaṃ ḍaissāmī’ti, kuk‍kurau āviñchaiyya ‘gāmaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, siṅgālau āviñchaiyya ‘sīvathikaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti, mak‍kaṭau āviñchaiyya ‘vanaṃ pavaikkhāmī’ti| yadā khau tai, bhikkhavai, chappāṇakā jhattā assu kilantā, atha tamaiva khīlaṃ vā thambhaṃ vā upatiṭṭhaiyyuṃ, upanisīdaiyyuṃ, upanipaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhunau kāyagatāsati bhāvitā bahulīkatā, taṃ cakkhu nāviñchati manāpiyaisu rūpaisu, amanāpiyā rūpā nappaṭikūlā haunti…pai॰… jivhā nāviñchati manāpiyaisu rasaisu…pai॰… manau nāviñchati manāpiyaisu dhammaisu, amanāpiyā dhammā nappaṭikūlā haunti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saṃvarau hauti|

‘‘‘daḷhai khīlai vā thambhai vā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāya satiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| tasmātiha vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kāyagatā nau sati bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi khau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

11. yavakalāpisuttaṃ

248. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yavakalāpī cātumahāpathai nikkhittā assa| atha cha purisā āgacchaiyyuṃ byābhaṅgihatthā| tai yavakalāpiṃ chahi byābhaṅgīhi hanaiyyuṃ| aivañhi sā, bhikkhavai, yavakalāpī suhatā assa chahi byābhaṅgīhi hañ‍ñamānā| atha sattamau purisau āgacchaiyya byābhaṅgihatthau| sau taṃ yavakalāpiṃ sattamāya byābhaṅgiyā hanaiyya| aivañhi sā bhikkhavai, yavakalāpī suhatatarā assa, sattamāya byābhaṅgiyā hañ‍ñamānā| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau cakkhusmiṃ hañ‍ñati manāpāmanāpaihi rūpaihi…pai॰… jivhāya hañ‍ñati manāpāmanāpaihi rasaihi…pai॰… manasmiṃ hañ‍ñati  manāpāmanāpaihi dhammaihi| sacai sau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau āyatiṃ punabbhavāya caitaiti, aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau suhatatarau hauti, saiyyathāpi sā yavakalāpī sattamāya byābhaṅgiyā hañ‍ñamānā|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau samupabbūḷhau (sī॰ pī॰) ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau asurai āmantaisi – ‘sacai, mārisā, daivāsurasaṅgāmai samupabyūḷhai asurā jinaiyyuṃ daivā parājinaiyyuṃ, yaina naṃ sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi bandhitvā mama santikai ānaiyyātha asurapura’nti| sak‍kaupi khau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai āmantaisi – ‘sacai, mārisā, daivāsurasaṅgāmai samupabyūḷhai daivā jinaiyyuṃ asurā parājinaiyyuṃ, yaina  naṃ vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi bandhitvā mama santikai ānaiyyātha sudhammaṃ daivasabha’nti| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, saṅgāmai daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiniṃsu| atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi bandhitvā sak‍kassa daivānamindassa santikai ānaisuṃ sudhammaṃ daivasabhaṃ| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi baddhau bandhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) hauti| yadā khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacittissa asurindassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘dhammikā khau daivā, adhammikā asurā, idhaiva dānāhaṃ  daivapuraṃ gacchāmī’ti| atha kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi muttaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati, dibbaihi ca pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti| yadā ca khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacittissa asurindassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘dhammikā khau asurā, adhammikā daivā, tatthaiva dānāhaṃ asurapuraṃ gamissāmī’ti, atha kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi baddhaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| dibbaihi ca pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi parihāyati| aivaṃ sukhumaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacittibandhanaṃ| tatau sukhumataraṃ mārabandhanaṃ| mañ‍ñamānau khau, bhikkhavai, baddhau mārassa, amañ‍ñamānau muttau pāpimatau|

‘‘‘asmī’ti, bhikkhavai, mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘na bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘rūpī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘arūpī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ, ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ| mañ‍ñitaṃ, bhikkhavai, raugau, mañ‍ñitaṃ gaṇḍau, mañ‍ñitaṃ sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘amañ‍ñamānaina amañ‍ñitamānaina (pī॰ ka॰) caitasā viharissāmā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘asmī’ti, bhikkhavai, iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘bhavissa’nti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘na bhavissa’nti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘rūpī bhavissa’nti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘arūpī bhavissa’nti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti iñ‍jitamaitaṃ, ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti  iñ‍jitamaitaṃ| iñ‍jitaṃ, bhikkhavai, raugau, iñ‍jitaṃ gaṇḍau, iñ‍jitaṃ sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘aniñ‍jamānaina aniñ‍jiyamānaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) caitasā viharissāmā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘asmī’ti, bhikkhavai, phanditamaitaṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti phanditamaitaṃ, ‘bhavissa’nti…pai॰… ‘na bhavissa’nti… ‘rūpī bhavissa’nti… ‘arūpī bhavissa’nti… ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti… ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti… ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti phanditamaitaṃ| phanditaṃ, bhikkhavai, raugau, phanditaṃ gaṇḍau, phanditaṃ sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘aphandamānaina aphandiyamānaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) caitasā viharissāmā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘asmī’ti, bhikkhavai, papañ‍citamaitaṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti papañ‍citamaitaṃ, ‘bhavissa’nti…pai॰… ‘na bhavissa’nti… ‘rūpī bhavissa’nti… ‘arūpī bhavissa’nti… ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti… ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti… ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti papañ‍citamaitaṃ| papañ‍citaṃ, bhikkhavai, raugau, papañ‍citaṃ gaṇḍau, papañ‍citaṃ sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘nippapañ‍caina caitasā viharissāmā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘asmī’ti, bhikkhavai, mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘bhavissa’nti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘na bhavissa’nti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘rūpī bhavissa’nti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘arūpī bhavissa’nti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti mānagatamaitaṃ, ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’nti  mānagatamaitaṃ| mānagataṃ, bhikkhavai, raugau, mānagataṃ gaṇḍau, mānagataṃ sal‍laṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘nihatamānaina caitasā viharissāmā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aikādasamaṃ|

āsīvisavaggau aikūnavīsatimau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āsīvisau  rathau kummau, dvai dārukkhandhā avassutau|

dukkhadhammā kiṃsukā vīṇā, chappāṇā yavakalāpīti||

saḷāyatanavaggai catutthapaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa  vagguddānaṃ –

nandikkhayau saṭṭhinayau, samuddau uragaina ca|

catupaṇṇāsakā aitai, nipātaisu pakāsitāti||

saḷāyatanasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



2. vaidanāsaṃyuttaṃ

1. sagāthāvaggau

1. samādhisuttaṃ

249. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanāti|

‘‘samāhitau sampajānau, satau buddhassa sāvakau|

vaidanā ca pajānāti, vaidanānañ‍ca sambhavaṃ||

‘‘yattha caitā nirujjhanti, maggañ‍ca khayagāminaṃ|

vaidanānaṃ khayā bhikkhu, nicchātau parinibbutau’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sukhasuttaṃ

250. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanāti|

‘‘sukhaṃ  vā yadi vā dukkhaṃ, adukkhamasukhaṃ saha|

ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca, yaṃ kiñ‍ci atthi vaiditaṃ||

‘‘aitaṃ  dukkhanti ñatvāna, mausadhammaṃ palaukinaṃ|

phussa phussa vayaṃ passaṃ, aivaṃ tattha viraj‍jatī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. pahānasuttaṃ

251. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sukhāya, bhikkhavai, vaidanāya rāgānusayau pahātabbau, dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau pahātabbau, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau pahātabbau| yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau pahīnau hauti, dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau pahīnau hauti, adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu niranusayau sammaddasau acchaicchi acchaij‍ji (bahūsu) taṇhaṃ, vivattayi vāvattayi (sī॰) saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’’ti|

‘‘sukhaṃ vaidayamānassa vaidiyamānassa (sī॰ pī॰), vaidanaṃ appajānatau|

sau rāgānusayau hauti, anissaraṇadassinau||

‘‘dukkhaṃ vaidayamānassa, vaidanaṃ appajānatau|

paṭighānusayau hauti, anissaraṇadassinau||

‘‘adukkhamasukhaṃ santaṃ, bhūripañ‍ñaina daisitaṃ|

tañ‍cāpi abhinandati, naiva dukkhā pamuc‍cati||

‘‘yatau  ca bhikkhu ātāpī, sampajañ‍ñaṃ na riñ‍cati|

tatau sau vaidanā sabbā, parijānāti paṇḍitau||

‘‘sau vaidanā pariñ‍ñāya, diṭṭhai dhammai anāsavau|

kāyassa bhaidā dhammaṭṭhau, saṅkhyaṃ naupaiti vaidagū’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. pātālasuttaṃ

252. ‘‘assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau yaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘atthi mahāsamuddai pātālau’ti| taṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau asantaṃ avij‍jamānaṃ aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘atthi mahāsamuddai pātālau’ti| sārīrikānaṃ khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhānaṃ vaidanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ yadidaṃ ‘pātālau’ti| assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau sārīrikāya dukkhāya vaidanāya  phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘assutavā puthuj‍janau pātālai na pac‍cuṭṭhāsi, gādhañ‍ca nājjhagā’| sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sārīrikāya dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau naiva saucati, na kilamati, na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘sutavā ariyasāvakau pātālai pac‍cuṭṭhāsi, gādhañ‍ca ajjhagā’’’ti|

‘‘yau  aitā nādhivāsaiti, uppan‍nā vaidanā dukhā|

sārīrikā pāṇaharā, yāhi phuṭṭhau pavaidhati||

‘‘ak‍kandati paraudati, dubbalau appathāmakau|

na sau pātālai pac‍cuṭṭhāsi, athau gādhampi nājjhagā||

‘‘yau  caitā adhivāsaiti, uppan‍nā vaidanā dukhā|

sārīrikā pāṇaharā, yāhi phuṭṭhau na vaidhati|

sa vai pātālai pac‍cuṭṭhāsi, athau gādhampi ajjhagā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. daṭṭhabbasuttaṃ

253. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sukhā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā dukkhatau daṭṭhabbā, dukkhā vaidanā sal‍latau daṭṭhabbā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā anic‍catau daṭṭhabbā| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sukhā vaidanā dukkhatau diṭṭhā hauti, dukkhā vaidanā sal‍latau diṭṭhā hauti, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā anic‍catau  diṭṭhā hauti  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu sammaddasau acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’’ti|

‘‘yau sukhaṃ dukkhatau adda, dukkhamaddakkhi sal‍latau|

adukkhamasukhaṃ santaṃ, addakkhi naṃ anic‍catau||

‘‘sa vai sammaddasau bhikkhu, parijānāti vaidanā|

sau vaidanā pariñ‍ñāya, diṭṭhai dhammai anāsavau|

kāyassa bhaidā dhammaṭṭhau, saṅkhyaṃ naupaiti vaidagū’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sal‍lasuttaṃ

254. ‘‘assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau sukhampi vaidanaṃ vaidayati vaidiyati (sī॰ pī॰), dukkhampi vaidanaṃ vaidayati, adukkhamasukhampi vaidanaṃ vaidayati| sutavā, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sukhampi vaidanaṃ vaidayati, dukkhampi vaidanaṃ vaidayati, adukkhamasukhampi  vaidanaṃ vaidayati| tatra, bhikkhavai, kau visaisau kau adhippayāsau adhippāyau (sī॰ ka॰), adhippāyasau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), adhippāyausau (pī॰) kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janainā’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… assutavā| bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati  paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| sau dvai vaidanā vaidayati – kāyikañ‍ca, caitasikañ‍ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisaṃ sal‍laina vijjhaiyya sal‍laina anuvijjhaiyyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| tamainaṃ dutiyaina sal‍laina anuvaidhaṃ vijjhaiyya sal‍laina anuvijjhaiyyuṃ (sī॰), sal‍laina anuvaidhaṃ vijjhaiyyuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sal‍laina vijjhaiyyuṃ (pī॰)| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, purisau dvisal‍laina vaidanaṃ vaidayati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| sau dvai vaidanā vaidayati – kāyikañ‍ca, caitasikañ‍ca| tassāyaiva khau pana dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau paṭighavā hauti| tamainaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighavantaṃ, yau dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau, sau anusaiti| sau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau kāmasukhaṃ abhinandati| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi sau, bhikkhavai, pajānāti assutavā puthuj‍janau añ‍ñatra kāmasukhā dukkhāya vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ, tassa kāmasukhañ‍ca abhinandatau, yau sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau, sau anusaiti| sau tāsaṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca  nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa tāsaṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ appajānatau, yau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau, sau anusaiti| sau sukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| dukkhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| adukkhamasukhañ‍cai  vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘assutavā puthuj‍janau sañ‍ñuttau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, sañ‍ñuttau dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau na saucati, na kilamati, na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na  sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| sau aikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayati – kāyikaṃ, na caitasikaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisaṃ sal‍laina vijjhaiyya| tamainaṃ dutiyaina sal‍laina anuvaidhaṃ na vijjhaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, purisau aikasal‍laina vaidanaṃ vaidayati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau na saucati, na kilamati, na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| sau aikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayati – kāyikaṃ, na caitasikaṃ| tassāyaiva khau pana dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau paṭighavā na hauti| tamainaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya appaṭighavantaṃ, yau dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau, sau nānusaiti| sau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau kāmasukhaṃ nābhinandati| taṃ kissa haitu? pajānāti hi sau, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau añ‍ñatra kāmasukhā dukkhāya vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ| tassa kāmasukhaṃ nābhinandatau yau sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau, sau nānusaiti| sau tāsaṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavaṃ ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa tāsaṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānatau, yau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau, sau nānusaiti| sau sukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| dukkhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| adukkhamasukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘sutavā ariyasāvakau visañ‍ñuttau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, visañ‍ñuttau dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau, ayaṃ adhippayāsau, idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janainā’’ti|

‘‘na vaidanaṃ vaidayati sapañ‍ñau,

sukhampi dukkhampi bahussutaupi|

ayañ‍ca dhīrassa puthuj‍janaina,

mahā ayaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) visaisau kusalassa hauti||

‘‘saṅkhātadhammassa  bahussutassa,

vipassatau sampassatau (sī॰ pī॰) laukamimaṃ parañ‍ca|

iṭṭhassa dhammā na mathainti cittaṃ,

aniṭṭhatau nau paṭighātamaiti||

‘‘tassānuraudhā athavā viraudhā,

vidhūpitā atthagatā na santi|

padañ‍ca ñatvā virajaṃ asaukaṃ,

sammā pajānāti bhavassa pāragū’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamagailañ‍ñasuttaṃ

255. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina gilānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi  –

‘‘satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau kālaṃ āgamaiyya| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī  viharati…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī viharati…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai  sampajānakārī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānakārī hauti| satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau kālaṃ āgamaiyya| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī|

‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃ satassa sampajānassa appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā, sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍nā khau myāyaṃ sukhā vaidanā| sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau appaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca| ayaṃ khau pana kāyau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| anic‍caṃ khau pana saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā kutau nic‍cā bhavissatī’ti! sau kāyai ca sukhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassī viharati, vayānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| tassa kāyai ca sukhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassinau viharatau, vayānupassinau viharatau, virāgānupassinau viharatau, niraudhānupassinau viharatau, paṭinissaggānupassinau  viharatau, yau kāyai ca sukhāya ca vaidanāya rāgānusayau, sau pahīyati|

‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃ satassa sampajānassa appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍nā khau myāyaṃ dukkhā vaidanā| sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau appaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca| ayaṃ khau pana kāyau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| anic‍caṃ khau pana saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā kutau nic‍cā bhavissatī’ti! sau kāyai ca dukkhāya vaidanāya  anic‍cānupassī viharati, vayānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| tassa kāyai ca dukkhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassinau viharatau…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassinau viharatau, yau kāyai ca dukkhāya ca vaidanāya paṭighānusayau, sau pahīyati|

‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃ satassa sampajānassa appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍nā khau myāyaṃ adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau appaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? imamaiva kāyaṃ paṭic‍ca| ayaṃ khau pana kāyau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| anic‍caṃ khau pana saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ kāyaṃ  paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā  adukkhamasukhā vaidanā kutau nic‍cā bhavissatī’ti! sau kāyai ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassī viharati, vayānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| tassa kāyai ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassinau viharatau…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassinau viharatau, yau kāyai ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau, sau pahīyati|

‘‘sau  sukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā anic‍cāti pajānāti, anajjhausitāti pajānāti, anabhinanditāti pajānāti; dukkhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā anic‍cāti pajānāti, anajjhausitāti pajānāti, anabhinanditāti pajānāti; adukkhamasukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā anic‍cāti pajānāti, anajjhausitāti pajānāti, anabhinanditāti pajānāti| sau sukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati; dukkhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati; adukkhamasukhañ‍cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visañ‍ñuttau naṃ vaidayati| sau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti sītibhavissantīti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya, tassaiva tailassa ca vaṭṭiyā ca pariyādānā anāhārau nibbāyaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyapariyantikaṃ  vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau  ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyagailañ‍ñasuttaṃ

256. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina gilānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau kālaṃ āgamaiyya| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati… cittai cittānupassī viharati… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti…pai॰… bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau hauti| satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau kālaṃ āgamaiyya| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī|

‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃ satassa sampajānassa appamattassa  ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍nā khau myāyaṃ sukhā vaidanā; sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau appaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? imamaiva phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| ayaṃ khau  pana phassau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| anic‍caṃ khau pana saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā kutau nic‍cā bhavissatī’ti! sau phassai ca sukhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassī viharati, vayānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| tassa phassai ca sukhāya ca vaidanāya anic‍cānupassinau viharatau, vayānupassinau viharatau, virāgānupassinau viharatau, niraudhānupassinau viharatau, paṭinissaggānupassinau viharatau yau phassai ca sukhāya ca vaidanāya rāgānusayau, sau pahīyati|

‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃ satassa…pai॰… viharatau uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā…pai॰… uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍nā khau myāyaṃ adukkhamasukhā vaidanā; sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau appaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? imamaiva phassaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… kāyassa  bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya, tassaiva tailassa ca vaṭṭiyā ca pariyādānā anāhārau nibbāyaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. anic‍casuttaṃ

257. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. phassamūlakasuttaṃ

258. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā phassajā phassamūlakā phassanidānā phassapac‍cayā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| sukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā, yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā, sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammati| dukkhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| tassaiva dukkhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā, yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā, sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammati| adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca  uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| tassaiva adukkhamasukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā, yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ kaṭṭhānaṃ saṅghaṭṭanasamaudhānā saṅkhattā tassa samaudhānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saṅghattā tassa samaudhānā (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.62 passitabbaṃ usmā jāyati, taijau abhinibbattati| taisaṃyaiva kaṭṭhānaṃ nānābhāvā vinikkhaipā, yā taj‍jā usmā, sā nirujjhati, sā vūpasammati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imā tissau vaidanā phassajā phassamūlakā phassanidānā phassapac‍cayā| taj‍jaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca taj‍jā vaidanā uppaj‍janti| taj‍jassa phassassa niraudhā taj‍jā vaidanā nirujjhantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sagāthāvaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

samādhi sukhaṃ pahānaina, pātālaṃ daṭṭhabbaina ca|

sal‍laina caiva gailañ‍ñā, anic‍ca phassamūlakāti||

2. rahaugatavaggau

1. rahaugatasuttaṃ

259. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ taṃ dukkhasmi’nti| kiṃ nu khau aitaṃ bhagavatā sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ taṃ dukkhasmi’’’nti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! tissau imā, bhikkhu, vaidanā vuttā mayā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā tissau vaidanā vuttā mayā| vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ, taṃ dukkhasmi’nti| taṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṅkhārānaṃyaiva anic‍cataṃ sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ taṃ dukkhasmi’nti| taṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṅkhārānaṃyaiva khayadhammataṃ…pai॰… vayadhammataṃ…pai॰… virāgadhammataṃ …pai॰… niraudhadhammataṃ…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammataṃ sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ taṃ dukkhasmi’nti| atha khau pana, bhikkhu, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau akkhātau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā niruddhā hauti| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ  samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā niruddhā haunti| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pīti niruddhā hauti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā niruddhā haunti| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca niruddhā haunti| khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau niruddhau hauti, dausau niruddhau hauti, mauhau niruddhau hauti| atha khau, bhikkhu, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa  vācā vūpasantā hauti| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā vūpasantā haunti…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca vūpasantā haunti| khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau vūpasantau hauti, dausau vūpasantau hauti, mauhau vūpasantau hauti| chayimā, bhikkhu, passaddhiyau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā paṭippassaddhā hauti| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pīti paṭippassaddhā hauti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā paṭippassaddhā  haunti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca paṭippassaddhā haunti| khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau paṭippassaddhau hauti, dausau paṭippassaddhau hauti, mauhau paṭippassaddhau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamaākāsasuttaṃ

260. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ākāsai vividhā vātā vāyanti| puratthimāpi vātā vāyanti, pacchimāpi vātā vāyanti, uttarāpi vātā vāyanti, dakkhiṇāpi vātā vāyanti, sarajāpi vātā vāyanti, arajāpi vātā vāyanti, sītāpi vātā vāyanti, uṇhāpi vātā vāyanti, parittāpi vātā vāyanti, adhimattāpi vātā  vāyanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ kāyasmiṃ vividhā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, sukhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati, dukkhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati, adukkhamasukhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘yathāpi vātā ākāsai, vāyanti vividhā puthū|

puratthimā pacchimā cāpi, uttarā atha dakkhiṇā||

‘‘sarajā arajā capi, sītā uṇhā ca aikadā|

adhimattā parittā ca, puthū vāyanti mālutā||

‘‘tathaivimasmiṃ kāyasmiṃ, samuppaj‍janti vaidanā|

sukhadukkhasamuppatti, adukkhamasukhā ca yā||

‘‘yatau  ca bhikkhu ātāpī, sampajañ‍ñaṃ na riñ‍cati sampajānau nirūpadhi (ka॰)|

tatau sau vaidanā sabbā, parijānāti paṇḍitau||

‘‘sau  vaidanā pariñ‍ñāya, diṭṭhai dhammai anāsavau|

kāyassa bhaidā dhammaṭṭhau, saṅkhyaṃ naupaiti vaidagū’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyaākāsasuttaṃ

261. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ākāsai vividhā vātā vāyanti| puratthimāpi vātā vāyanti…pai॰… adhimattāpi vātā vāyanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ kāyasmiṃ vividhā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, sukhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati, dukkhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati, adukkhamasukhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. agārasuttaṃ

262. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āgantukāgāraṃ| tattha puratthimāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, pacchimāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, uttarāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, dakkhiṇāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti| khattiyāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, brāhmaṇāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, vaissāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, suddāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ kāyasmiṃ vividhā vaidanā  uppaj‍janti| sukhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati, dukkhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati, adukkhamasukhāpi vaidanā uppaj‍jati| sāmisāpi sukhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati, sāmisāpi dukkhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati, sāmisāpi adukkhamasukhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati| nirāmisāpi sukhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati, nirāmisāpi dukkhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati, nirāmisāpi adukkhamasukhā vaidanā uppaj‍jatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaānandasuttaṃ

263. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi, aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇanti? tissau imā, ānanda, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, ānanda, vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau; phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ vaidanāya assādau| yā vaidanā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavau| yau vaidanāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ| atha khau panānanda, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau akkhātau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā niruddhā hauti…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ  samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca niruddhā haunti| khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau niruddhau hauti, dausau niruddhau hauti, mauhau niruddhau hauti| atha khau panānanda, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā vūpasantā hauti…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca vūpasantā haunti| khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau vūpasantau hauti, dausau vūpasantau hauti, mauhau vūpasantau hauti| atha khau panānanda, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ paṭippassaddhi akkhātā| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā paṭippassaddhā hauti…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñā paṭippassaddhā hauti| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā paṭippassaddhā hauti| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā paṭippassaddhā hauti| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā paṭippassaddhā hauti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca paṭippassaddhā haunti| khīṇāsavassa  bhikkhunau rāgau paṭippassaddhau hauti, dausau paṭippassaddhau hauti, mauhau paṭippassaddhau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaānandasuttaṃ

264. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā nu khau, ānanda, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavan‍naittikā bhagavampaṭisaraṇā| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavantañ‍ñaiva paṭibhātu aitassa  bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, ānanda, suṇauhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘tissau imā, ānanda, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, ānanda, vaidanā…pai॰… phassasamudayā…pai॰… khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau paṭippassaddhau hauti, dausau paṭippassaddhau hauti, mauhau paṭippassaddhau hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamasambahulasuttaṃ

265. atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| phassasamudayā  vaidanāsamudayau; phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau| ayamaiva  ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ vaidanāya assādau| yā vaidanā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavau| yau vaidanāya  chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘atha khau pana, bhikkhavai, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ niraudhau akkhātau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā niruddhā hauti…pai॰… khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau niruddhau hauti, dausau niruddhau hauti, mauhau niruddhau hauti| atha khau pana, bhikkhavai, mayā anupubbasaṅkhārānaṃ vūpasamau akkhātau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā vūpasantā hauti…pai॰… khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau vūpasantau hauti, dausau vūpasantau hauti, mauhau vūpasantau hauti| chayimā, bhikkhavai, passaddhiyau| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vācā paṭippassaddhā hauti| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā paṭippassaddhā haunti| tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pīti paṭippassaddhā hauti| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā paṭippassaddhā haunti| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca paṭippassaddhā haunti| khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgau paṭippassaddhau hauti, dausau paṭippassaddhau hauti, mauhau paṭippassaddhau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasambahulasuttaṃ

266. atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhikkhavai, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamau  vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…’’ ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, vaidanā…pai॰… phassasamudayā…pai॰…| (yathā purimasuttantai, tathā vitthāraitabbau|)  aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pañ‍cakaṅgasuttaṃ

267. atha  khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati yaināyasmā udāyī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai udāyi, vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘tissau khau, thapati, vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, thapati, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, bhantai udāyi, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā| yāyaṃ, bhantai, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, santasmiṃ aisā paṇītai sukhai vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, thapati, dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā tissau vaidanā  vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| dutiyampi khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, bhantai udāyi, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā| yāyaṃ, bhantai, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, santasmiṃ aisā paṇītai sukhai vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| tatiyampi khau  āyasmā udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, thapati, dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| tatiyampi khau pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, bhantai udāyi, tissau vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā| dvai vaidanā vuttā bhagavatā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā| yāyaṃ, bhantai, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, santasmiṃ aisā paṇītai sukhai vuttā bhagavatā’’ti| naiva sakkhi āyasmā udāyī pañ‍cakaṅgaṃ thapatiṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ, na panāsakkhi pañ‍cakaṅgau thapati āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ| assausi khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmatau udāyissa pañ‍cakaṅgaina thapatinā saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau yāvatakau  āyasmatau udāyissa pañ‍cakaṅgaina thapatinā saddhiṃ ahausi kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘santamaiva, ānanda, pariyāyaṃ pañ‍cakaṅgau  thapati udāyissa bhikkhunau nābbhanumaudi; santañ‍ca panānanda, pariyāyaṃ udāyī bhikkhu pañ‍cakaṅgassa thapatinau nābbhanumaudi| dvaipi mayā, ānanda, vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| tissaupi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| pañ‍capi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| chapi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| aṭṭhārasāpi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| chattiṃsāpi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| aṭṭhasatampi  mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitau khau, ānanda, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitai khau, ānanda, mayā dhammai yai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ, na samanumañ‍ñissanti, na samanujānissanti, na samanumaudissanti, taisaṃ aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharissantīti viharissanti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitau khau, ānanda, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitai khau, ānanda, mayā dhammai yai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ samanumañ‍ñissanti samanujānissanti samanumaudissanti, taisaṃ aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharissantī’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, ānanda, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, ānanda, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, ānanda, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai  khau, ānanda, aivaṃ  vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ  naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā, paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā, nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā, ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ  sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma, ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma, ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| yai khau, ānanda, aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘aitaṃparamaṃ santaṃ sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti – idaṃ naisāhaṃ nānujānāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? atthānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘katamañ‍cānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca? idhānanda, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ khau, ānanda, aitamhā sukhā añ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ānanda, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samaṇau gautamau āha, tañ‍ca sukhasmiṃ  pañ‍ñapaiti| tayidaṃ kiṃsu, tayidaṃ kathaṃsū’ti? aivaṃvādinau, ānanda, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘na khau, āvusau, bhagavā sukhañ‍ñaiva vaidanaṃ sandhāya sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| yattha yattha, āvusau, sukhaṃ upalabbhati, yahiṃ yahiṃ yaṃ hiyaṃ hi sukhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), yahiṃ yahiṃ sukhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.91, taṃ taṃ tathāgatau sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bhikkhusuttaṃ

268. ‘‘dvaipi mayā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina, tissaupi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina, pañ‍capi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina, chapi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina, aṭṭhārasāpi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina, chattiṃsāpi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina, aṭṭhasatampi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitai khau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai yai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ na samanumañ‍ñissanti, na samanujānissanti, na samanumaudissanti, taisaṃ aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ  – bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharissantīti| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ pariyāyadaisitai khau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai yai añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ samanumañ‍ñissanti samanujānissanti samanumaudissanti, taisaṃ  aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharissantīti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā…pai॰… ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ  samaṇau gautamau āha, tañ‍ca sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| tayidaṃ kiṃsu, tayidaṃ kathaṃsū’ti? aivaṃvādinau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘na khau, āvusau, bhagavā sukhañ‍ñaiva vaidanaṃ sandhāya sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| yattha yattha, āvusau, sukhaṃ upalabbhati yahiṃ yahiṃ yaṃ hi yaṃ hi (sī॰ pī॰), taṃ taṃ tathāgatau sukhasmiṃ pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

rahaugatavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

rahaugataṃ  dvai ākāsaṃ, agāraṃ dvai ca ānandā|

sambahulā duvai vuttā, pañ‍cakaṅgau ca bhikkhunāti||

3. aṭṭhasatapariyāyavaggau

1. sīvakasuttaṃ

269. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau mauḷiyasīvakau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mauḷiyasīvakau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santi, bhau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitū’ti| idha idha pana (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhavaṃ gautamau kimāhā’’ti?

‘‘pittasamuṭṭhānānipi  khau, sīvaka, idhaikac‍cāni vaidayitāni uppaj‍janti| sāmampi khau aitaṃ, sīvaka, vaiditabbaṃ aivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) yathā pittasamuṭṭhānānipi idhaikac‍cāni vaidayitāni uppaj‍janti; laukassapi khau aitaṃ, sīvaka, sac‍casammataṃ yathā pittasamuṭṭhānānipi idhaikac‍cāni vaidayitāni uppaj‍janti| tatra, sīvaka, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitū’ti| yañ‍ca sāmaṃ ñātaṃ tañ‍ca atidhāvanti, yañ‍ca laukai sac‍casammataṃ tañ‍ca atidhāvanti| tasmā taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ micchāti vadāmi|

‘‘saimhasamuṭṭhānānipi khau, sīvaka…pai॰… vātasamuṭṭhānānipi khau, sīvaka…pai॰…  san‍nipātikānipi khau, sīvaka…pai॰… utupariṇāmajānipi khau, sīvaka…pai॰… visamaparihārajānipi khau, sīvaka…pai॰… aupak‍kamikānipi khau, sīvaka…pai॰… kammavipākajānipi  khau, sīvaka, idhaikac‍cāni vaidayitāni uppaj‍janti| sāmampi khau aitaṃ, sīvaka, vaiditabbaṃ| yathā kammavipākajānipi idhaikac‍cāni  vaidayitāni uppaj‍janti; laukassapi khau aitaṃ, sīvaka, sac‍casammataṃ| yathā kammavipākajānipi idhaikac‍cāni vaidayitāni uppaj‍janti; tatra, sīvaka, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitū’ti| yañ‍ca sāmaṃ ñātaṃ tañ‍ca atidhāvanti yañ‍ca laukai sac‍casammataṃ tañ‍ca atidhāvanti| tasmā taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ micchāti vadāmīti| aivaṃ vuttai, mauḷiyasīvakau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama …pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’’nti|

‘‘pittaṃ saimhañ‍ca vātau ca, san‍nipātā utūni ca|

visamaṃ aupak‍kamikaṃ, kammavipākaina aṭṭhamī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. aṭṭhasatasuttaṃ

270. ‘‘aṭṭhasatapariyāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhasatapariyāyau, dhammapariyāyau? dvaipi mayā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina; tissaupi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina; pañ‍capi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina; chapi  mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina; aṭṭhārasāpi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina; chattiṃsāpi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina; aṭṭhasatampi mayā vaidanā vuttā pariyāyaina| ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, dvai vaidanā? kāyikā ca caitasikā ca – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dvai vaidanā| katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanā? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca vaidanā? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca vaidanā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, cha vaidanā? cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… manausamphassajā vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, cha vaidanā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhārasa vaidanā? cha saumanassūpavicārā, cha daumanassūpavicārā, cha upaikkhūpavicārā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhārasa vaidanā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, chattiṃsa vaidanā? cha gaihasitāni gaihassitāni (aṭṭha॰) saumanassāni, cha naikkhammasitāni naikkhammassitāni (aṭṭha॰) saumanassāni, cha gaihasitāni daumanassāni, cha naikkhammasitāni  daumanassāni, cha gaihasitā upaikkhā, cha naikkhammasitā upaikkhā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, chattiṃsa vaidanā| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhasataṃ vaidanā? atītā chattiṃsa vaidanā, anāgatā chattiṃsa vaidanā, pac‍cuppan‍nā chattiṃsa vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhasataṃ vaidanā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhasatapariyāyau dhammapariyāyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. añ‍ñatarabhikkhusuttaṃ

271. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamā vaidanāsamudayagāminī paṭipadā? katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti?

‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhu, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhu, vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau| taṇhā vaidanāsamudayagāminī  paṭipadā| phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ vaidanāya assādau; yā vaidanā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavau; yau vaidanāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇa’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pubbasuttaṃ

272. ‘‘pubbaiva  mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘katamā nu khau vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamā vaidanāsamudayagāminī paṭipadā, katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘tissau imā vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| imā vuc‍canti vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau| taṇhā vaidanāsamudayagāminī paṭipadā…pai॰… yau vaidanāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ| idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇa’’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. ñāṇasuttaṃ

273. ‘‘‘imā  vaidanā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘ayaṃ vaidanāsamudayau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi…pai॰… ālaukau udapādi| ‘ayaṃ vaidanāsamudayagāminī paṭipadā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ vaidanāniraudhau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi  …pai॰… ‘ayaṃ  vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ vaidanāya assādau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu…pai॰…  ‘ayaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… ‘idaṃ khau nissaraṇa’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sambahulabhikkhusuttaṃ

274. atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamā vaidanāsamudayagāminī paṭipadā? katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| imā vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau| taṇhā vaidanāsamudayagāminī paṭipadā| phassaniraudhā…pai॰… yau vaidanāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ| idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇa’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

275. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā  vā brāhmaṇā vā imāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti| na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu  vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavaṃ ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti| tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā| tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca, diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

276. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

277. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vaidanaṃ nappajānanti, vaidanāsamudayaṃ nappajānanti, vaidanāniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. suddhikasuttaṃ

278. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau  vaidanā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. nirāmisasuttaṃ

279. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, sāmisā pīti, atthi nirāmisā pīti, atthi nirāmisā nirāmisatarā pīti; atthi sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ, atthi nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ, atthi nirāmisā nirāmisataraṃ sukhaṃ; atthi sāmisā upaikkhā, atthi nirāmisā upaikkhā, atthi nirāmisā nirāmisatarā upaikkhā; atthi sāmisau vimaukkhau, atthi nirāmisau vimaukkhau, atthi nirāmisā nirāmisatarau vimaukkhau| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sāmisā pīti? pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yā khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pīti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāmisā pīti|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā pīti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā pīti|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisatarā pīti? yā khau, bhikkhavai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau uppaj‍jati pīti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisatarā pīti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ? pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisataraṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau uppaj‍jati  sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisataraṃ sukhaṃ|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sāmisā upaikkhā? pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yā khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāmisā upaikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā upaikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā, dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā upaikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisatarā upaikkhā? yā khau, bhikkhavai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau uppaj‍jati upaikkhā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisatarā upaikkhā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sāmisau vimaukkhau? rūpappaṭisaṃyuttau vimaukkhau sāmisau vimaukkhau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisau vimaukkhau? arūpappaṭisaṃyuttau vimaukkhau nirāmisau vimaukkhau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisatarau vimaukkhau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau rāgā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, dausā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau, mauhā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau uppaj‍jati vimaukkhau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nirāmisā nirāmisatarau vimaukkhau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

aṭṭhasatapariyāyavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sīvakaaṭṭhasataṃ  bhikkhu, pubbai ñāṇañ‍ca bhikkhunā|

samaṇabrāhmaṇā tīṇi, suddhikañ‍ca nirāmisanti||

vaidanāsaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



3. mātugāmasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamapaiyyālavaggau

1. mātugāmasuttaṃ

280. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau aikantaamanāpau hauti purisassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na ca rūpavā hauti, na ca bhaugavā hauti, na ca sīlavā hauti, alasau ca hauti, pajañ‍cassa na labhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau aikantaamanāpau hauti purisassa| pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau aikantamanāpau hauti purisassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? rūpavā ca hauti, bhaugavā ca hauti, sīlavā ca hauti, dakkhau ca hauti analasau, pajañ‍cassa labhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau aikantamanāpau hauti purisassā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. purisasuttaṃ

281. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau purisau aikantaamanāpau hauti mātugāmassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na ca rūpavā hauti, na ca bhaugavā hauti, na ca sīlavā hauti, alasau ca hauti, pajañ‍cassa na labhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi  aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau purisau aikantaamanāpau hauti mātugāmassa| pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau purisau aikantamanāpau hauti mātugāmassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? rūpavā ca hauti, bhaugavā ca hauti, sīlavā ca hauti, dakkhau ca hauti analasau, pajañ‍cassa labhati – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau purisau  aikantamanāpau hauti mātugāmassā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. āvaiṇikadukkhasuttaṃ

282. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa āvaiṇikāni dukkhāni, yāni mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihi| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau daharauva samānau patikulaṃ gacchati, ñātakaihi vinā hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa paṭhamaṃ āvaiṇikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihi| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau utunī hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa dutiyaṃ āvaiṇikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihi| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau gabbhinī hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa tatiyaṃ āvaiṇikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihi| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau vijāyati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa catutthaṃ āvaiṇikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihi| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau purisassa pāricariyaṃ upaiti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa pañ‍camaṃ āvaiṇikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihi| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa āvaiṇikāni dukkhāni, yāni mātugāmau pac‍canubhauti, añ‍ñatraiva purisaihī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. tīhidhammaihisuttaṃ

283. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau yaibhuyyaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| majjhanhikasamayaṃ issāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| sāyanhasamayaṃ kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau yaibhuyyaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kaudhanasuttaṃ

284. atha  khau āyasmā anuruddhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, mātugāmaṃ passāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jantaṃ| katīhi nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cahi khau, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, kaudhanau ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. upanāhīsuttaṃ

285. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, upanāhī ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. issukīsuttaṃ

286. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, issukī ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. maccharīsuttaṃ

287. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, maccharī ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi  dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aticārīsuttaṃ

288. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, aticārī ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… upapaj‍jatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dussīlasuttaṃ

289. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, dussīlau ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. appassutasuttaṃ

290. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, appassutau ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. kusītasuttaṃ

291. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, kusītau ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰…  apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. muṭṭhassatisuttaṃ

292. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? assaddhau  ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca hauti, muṭṭhassati ca hauti, duppañ‍ñau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

14. pañ‍cavairasuttaṃ

293. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau…pai॰… nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātī ca hauti, adin‍nādāyī ca hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī ca hauti, musāvādī ca hauti, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhāyī ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

paṭhamapaiyyālavaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mātugāmau  purisau ca, āvaiṇikā tidhammau ca dvai manāpāmanāpāca, āvaiṇikā tīhi anuruddhau (sabbattha)|

kaudhanau upanāhī ca, issukī maccharaina ca|

aticārī ca dussīlau, appassutau ca kusītau|

muṭṭhassati pañ‍cavairaṃ, kaṇhapakkhai pakāsitau||

2. dutiyapaiyyālavaggau

1. ak‍kaudhanasuttaṃ

294. atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, mātugāmaṃ passāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantaṃ| katīhi nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cahi  khau, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau ca hauti, hirimā ca hauti, auttappī ca hauti, ak‍kaudhanau ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anupanāhīsuttaṃ

295. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau ca hauti, hirimā ca hauti, auttappī  ca hauti, anupanāhī ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. anissukīsuttaṃ

296. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhau ca hauti, hirimā ca hauti, auttappī ca hauti, anissukī ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. amaccharīsuttaṃ
