297. amaccharī ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. anaticārīsuttaṃ

298. anaticārī ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. susīlasuttaṃ

299. sīlavā ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bahussutasuttaṃ

300. bahussutau  ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. āraddhavīriyasuttaṃ

301. āraddhavīriyau  ca hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. upaṭṭhitassatisuttaṃ

302. ‘‘upaṭṭhitassati  ca  hauti, pañ‍ñavā ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

imai aṭṭha suttantasaṅkhaipā|

10. pañ‍casīlasuttaṃ

303. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau ca hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau ca hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau ca hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau ca hauti, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau ca hauti – imaihi khau, anuruddha, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dutiyapaiyyālavaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dutiyai ca anuruddhau (sabbattha) ak‍kaudhanau, anupanāhī anissukī|

amaccharī anaticārī, sīlavā ca bahussutau|

vīriyaṃ sati sīlañ‍ca, suk‍kapakkhai pakāsitau||

3. balavaggau

1. visāradasuttaṃ

304. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūpabalaṃ, bhaugabalaṃ, ñātibalaṃ, puttabalaṃ, sīlabalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa balāni| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi balaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau visāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pasayhasuttaṃ

305. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūpabalaṃ, bhaugabalaṃ, ñātibalaṃ, puttabalaṃ, sīlabalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa balāni| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi balaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau sāmikaṃ pasayha agāraṃ ajjhāvasatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. abhibhuyyasuttaṃ

306. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūpabalaṃ, bhaugabalaṃ, ñātibalaṃ, puttabalaṃ, sīlabalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa balāni| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi balaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau sāmikaṃ abhibhuyya vattatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. aikasuttaṃ

307. ‘‘aikaina ca khau, bhikkhavai, balaina saman‍nāgatau purisau mātugāmaṃ abhibhuyya vattati| katamaina aikaina balaina? issariyabalaina abhibhūtaṃ mātugāmaṃ naiva rūpabalaṃ tāyati, na  bhaugabalaṃ tāyati, na ñātibalaṃ tāyati, na puttabalaṃ tāyati, na sīlabalaṃ tāyatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. aṅgasuttaṃ

308. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūpabalaṃ, bhaugabalaṃ, ñātibalaṃ, puttabalaṃ, sīlabalaṃ| rūpabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, na ca bhaugabalaina – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau rūpabalaina ca saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| rūpabalaina  ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, na ca ñātibalaina – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau rūpabalaina ca saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| rūpabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca, na ca puttabalaina – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau rūpabalaina ca saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca, puttabalaina ca – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| rūpabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca, puttabalaina ca, na ca sīlabalaina – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau rūpabalaina ca saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca, puttabalaina ca, sīlabalaina ca – aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa balānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. nāsaintisuttaṃ

309. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūpabalaṃ, bhaugabalaṃ, ñātibalaṃ, puttabalaṃ, sīlabalaṃ| rūpabalaina  ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, na ca sīlabalaina, nāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na vāsainti| rūpabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, na ca sīlabalaina, nāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na vāsainti| rūpabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca, na ca sīlabalaina, nāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na vāsainti| rūpabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhaugabalaina ca, ñātibalaina ca, puttabalaina ca, na ca sīlabalaina, nāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na vāsainti| sīlabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, na ca rūpabalaina, vāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na nāsainti| sīlabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, na ca bhaugabalaina, vāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na nāsainti| sīlabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, na ca ñātibalaina, vāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na nāsainti| sīlabalaina ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saman‍nāgatau hauti, na ca puttabalaina, vāsaintaiva naṃ, kulai na nāsainti| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa balānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. haitusuttaṃ

310. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūpabalaṃ, bhaugabalaṃ, ñātibalaṃ, puttabalaṃ, sīlabalaṃ| na, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau rūpabalahaitu vā bhaugabalahaitu vā ñātibalahaitu vā puttabalahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| sīlabalahaitu khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca mātugāmassa balānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ṭhānasuttaṃ

311. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, ṭhānāni dul‍labhāni akatapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| katamāni pañ‍ca? patirūpai kulai jāyaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ dul‍labhaṃ akatapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gacchaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ dul‍labhaṃ akatapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā, patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gantvā, asapatti agāraṃ ajjhāvasaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ dul‍labhaṃ akatapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā, patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gantvā, asapatti agāraṃ ajjhāvasantī puttavatī assanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ dul‍labhaṃ akatapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā, patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gantvā, asapatti agāraṃ ajjhāvasantī puttavatī samānā sāmikaṃ abhibhuyya vattaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ ṭhānaṃ dul‍labhaṃ akatapuñ‍ñaina  mātugāmaina| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ṭhānāni dul‍labhāni akatapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaināti|

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, ṭhānāni sulabhāni katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| katamāni pañ‍ca? patirūpai kulai jāyaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ sulabhaṃ katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gacchaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ sulabhaṃ katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gantvā asapatti agāraṃ ajjhāvasaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ sulabhaṃ katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai jāyitvā patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gantvā asapatti agāraṃ ajjhāvasantī puttavatī assanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ sulabhaṃ katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| patirūpai kulai  jāyitvā patirūpaṃ kulaṃ gantvā asapatti agāraṃ ajjhāvasantī  puttavatī samānā sāmikaṃ abhibhuyya vattaiyyanti – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ ṭhānaṃ sulabhaṃ katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmaina| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ṭhānāni sulabhāni katapuñ‍ñaina mātugāmainā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pañ‍casīlavisāradasuttaṃ

312. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau visāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau ca hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau ca hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau ca hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau ca hauti, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau ca hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau visāradau agāraṃ ajjhāvasatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vaḍḍhīsuttaṃ

313. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānā ariyasāvikā ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati sārādāyinī ca hauti varādāyinī ca kāyassa| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? saddhāya vaḍḍhati, sīlaina vaḍḍhati, sutaina vaḍḍhati, cāgaina vaḍḍhati, pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi  vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhamānā ariyasāvikā ariyāya vaḍḍhiyā vaḍḍhati, sārādāyinī ca hauti, varādāyinī ca kāyassā’’ti|

‘‘saddhāya sīlaina ca yādha vaḍḍhati,

pañ‍ñāya cāgaina sutaina cūbhayaṃ|

sā tādisī sīlavatī upāsikā,

ādīyati sāramidhaiva attanau’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

balavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

visāradā pasayha abhibhuyya, aikaṃ aṅgaina pañ‍camaṃ|

nāsainti haitu ṭhānañ‍ca, visāradau vaḍḍhinā dasāti||

mātugāmasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



4. jambukhādakasaṃyuttaṃ

1. nibbānapañhāsuttaṃ

314. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau magadhaisu viharati nālakagāmakai| atha khau jambukhādakau paribbājakau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jambukhādakau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘nibbānaṃ, nibbāna’nti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, nibbāna’’nti? ‘‘yau khau, āvusau, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati nibbāna’’nti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau  panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. arahattapañhāsuttaṃ

315. ‘‘‘arahattaṃ, arahatta’nti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu  khau, āvusau, arahatta’’nti? ‘‘yau khau, āvusau, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati arahatta’’nti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa arahattassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa arahattassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau  panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitassa arahattassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aitassa arahattassa sacchikiriyāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau āvusau, maggau, ayaṃ paṭipadā aitassa arahattassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā aitassa arahattassa sacchikiriyāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dhammavādīpañhāsuttaṃ

316. ‘‘kai nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, laukai dhammavādinau, kai laukai suppaṭipan‍nā, kai laukai sugatā’’ti? ‘‘yai khau, āvusau, rāgappahānāya dhammaṃ daisainti, dausappahānāya  dhammaṃ daisainti, mauhappahānāya dhammaṃ daisainti, tai laukai dhammavādinau| yai khau, āvusau, rāgassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nā, dausassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nā, mauhassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nā, tai laukai suppaṭipan‍nā| yaisaṃ khau, āvusau, rāgau pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, dausau pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, mauhau pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau  tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, tai laukai sugatā’’ti|

‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa rāgassa dausassa mauhassa pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa rāgassa dausassa mauhassa pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau, panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitassa rāgassa dausassa mauhassa pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aitassa rāgassa dausassa mauhassa pahānāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aitassa rāgassa dausassa mauhassa pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau  bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitassa rāgassa dausassa mauhassa pahānāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kimatthiyasuttaṃ

317. ‘‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘dukkhassa khau, āvusau, pariñ‍ñatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti?

‘‘ayamaiva  khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau  ayaṃ paṭipadā aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. assāsappattasuttaṃ

318. ‘‘‘assāsappattau, assāsappattau’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, assāsappattau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, assāsappattau hautī’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa assāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa assāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitassa assāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aitassa assāsassa sacchikiriyāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitassa assāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitassa assāsassa sacchikiriyāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paramassāsappattasuttaṃ

319. ‘‘‘paramassāsappattau, paramassāsappattau’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, paramassāsappattau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau  khau, āvusau, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca  atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca  ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti, aittāvatā khau, āvusau, paramassāsappattau hautī’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa paramassāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa paramassāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau pana, āvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitassa paramassāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aitassa paramassāsassa sacchikiriyāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitassa paramassāsassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitassa paramassāsassa sacchikiriyāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vaidanāpañhāsuttaṃ

320. ‘‘‘vaidanā, vaidanā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vaidanā’’ti? ‘‘tissau imāvusau, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, āvusau, tissau vaidanā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau  maggau, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau  sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. āsavapañhāsuttaṃ

321. ‘‘‘āsavau, āsavau’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, āvusau, āsavau’’ti? ‘‘tayau mai, āvusau, āsavā| kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, avij‍jāsavau – imai khau, āvusau, tayau āsavā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitaisaṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitaisaṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitaisaṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau aitaisaṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. avij‍jāpañhāsuttaṃ

322. ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ khau, āvusau, dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya  añ‍ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitissā avij‍jāya pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitissā avij‍jāya pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitissā avij‍jāya pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitissā avij‍jāya pahānāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau  sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitissā avij‍jāya pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitissā avij‍jāya  pahānāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. taṇhāpañhāsuttaṃ

323. ‘‘‘taṇhā, taṇhā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, taṇhā’’ti? ‘‘tissau imā, āvusau, taṇhā| kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā – imā khau, āvusau, tissau taṇhā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau  panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ pahānāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ taṇhānaṃ pahānāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. aughapañhāsuttaṃ

324. ‘‘‘aughau, aughau’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, āvusau, aughau’’ti? ‘‘cattāraumai, āvusau, aughā| kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughau – imai khau, āvusau, cattārau aughā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ aughānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ aughānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ aughānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitaisaṃ aughānaṃ pahānāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ aughānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ aughānaṃ pahānāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. upādānapañhāsuttaṃ

325. ‘‘‘upādānaṃ, upādāna’nti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, upādāna’’nti? ‘‘cattārimāni, āvusau, upādānāni| kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ – imāni khau, āvusau, cattāri upādānānī’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ upādānānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ upādānānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ upādānānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitaisaṃ upādānānaṃ pahānāya, saiyyathidaṃ  – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ upādānānaṃ pahānāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ upādānānaṃ pahānāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. bhavapañhāsuttaṃ

326. ‘‘‘bhavau, bhavau’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, āvusau, bhavau’’ti? ‘‘tayau mai, āvusau, bhavā| kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau – imai khau, āvusau, tayau bhavā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ bhavānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ bhavānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau, panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ bhavānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva  khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitaisaṃ bhavānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ bhavānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitaisaṃ bhavānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

14. dukkhapañhāsuttaṃ

327. ‘‘‘dukkhaṃ, dukkha’nti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, dukkha’’nti? ‘‘tissau imā, āvusau, dukkhatā| dukkhadukkhatā, saṅkhāradukkhatā, vipariṇāmadukkhatā – imā khau, āvusau, tissau dukkhatā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ dukkhatānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ dukkhatānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ dukkhatānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitāsaṃ dukkhatānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ dukkhatānaṃ pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitāsaṃ dukkhatānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

15. sak‍kāyapañhāsuttaṃ

328. ‘‘‘sak‍kāyau, sak‍kāyau’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, āvusau, sak‍kāyau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā sak‍kāyau vuttau bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai khau, āvusau, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā sak‍kāyau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa sak‍kāyassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa sak‍kāyassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘katamau panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā, aitassa sak‍kāyassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitassa sak‍kāyassa pariñ‍ñāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitassa sak‍kāyassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitassa sak‍kāyassa pariñ‍ñāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| pan‍narasamaṃ|

16. duk‍karapañhāsuttaṃ

329. ‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai duk‍kara’’nti? ‘‘pabbaj‍jā khau, āvusau, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai duk‍karā’’ti| ‘‘pabbajitaina panāvusau, kiṃ duk‍kara’’nti? ‘‘pabbajitaina khau, āvusau, abhirati duk‍karā’’ti| ‘‘abhirataina panāvusau, kiṃ duk‍kara’’nti? ‘‘abhirataina khau, āvusau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti duk‍karā’’ti| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ panāvusau, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhu arahaṃ assā’’ti? ‘‘naciraṃ, āvusau’’ti| sauḷasamaṃ|

jambukhādakasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

nibbānaṃ arahattañ‍ca, dhammavādī  kimatthiyaṃ|

assāsau paramassāsau, vaidanā āsavāvij‍jā|

taṇhā aughā upādānaṃ, bhavau dukkhañ‍ca sak‍kāyau||

imasmiṃ dhammavinayai duk‍karanti||


5. sāmaṇḍakasaṃyuttaṃ

1. sāmaṇḍakasuttaṃ

330. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau vaj‍jīsu viharati uk‍kacailāyaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā tīrai| atha khau sāmaṇḍakau sāmaṇḍakāni (sī॰) paribbājakau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sāmaṇḍakau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘nibbānaṃ, nibbāna’nti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, nibbāna’’nti? ‘‘yau khau, āvusau, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati nibbāna’’nti| ‘‘atthi panāvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau atthi paṭipadā, aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

‘‘katamau  panāvusau, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā, aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhaddakau, āvusau, maggau bhaddikā paṭipadā, aitassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya| alañ‍ca panāvusau sāriputta, appamādāyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

(yathā jambukhādakasaṃyuttaṃ, tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

2. duk‍karasuttaṃ

331. ‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai duk‍kara’’nti? ‘‘pabbaj‍jā khau, āvusau, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai duk‍karā’’ti| ‘‘pabbajitaina panāvusau, kiṃ duk‍kara’’nti? ‘‘pabbajitaina khau, āvusau, abhirati duk‍karā’’ti| ‘‘abhirataina panāvusau, kiṃ duk‍kara’’nti? ‘‘abhirataina khau, āvusau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti duk‍karā’’ti| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ panāvusau, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhu arahaṃ assā’’ti? ‘‘naciraṃ, āvusau’’ti| sauḷasamaṃ|

(purimakasadisaṃ uddānaṃ|)

sāmaṇḍakasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



6. mauggal‍lānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamajhānapañhāsuttaṃ

332. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘idha mayhaṃ, āvusau, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, paṭhamaṃ jhāna’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau paṭhamaṃ jhānanti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati paṭhamaṃ jhāna’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau kāmasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ pamādau, paṭhamai jhānai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, paṭhamai jhānai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi aikaudikarauhi (pī॰), paṭhamai jhānai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi  savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārānuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’ti, mamaṃ taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārānuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyajhānapañhāsuttaṃ

333. ‘‘‘dutiyaṃ  jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhāna’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau dutiyaṃ jhānanti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati dutiyaṃ jhāna’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ pamādau, dutiyai jhānai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, dutiyai jhānai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, dutiyai jhānai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārānuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’ti, mamaṃ taṃ  sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārānuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyajhānapañhāsuttaṃ

334. ‘‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhāna’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau tatiyaṃ jhānanti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – idha bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati tatiyaṃ jhānanti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharāmi satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaimi| yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau pītisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha  khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ pamādau, tatiyai jhānai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, tatiyai jhānai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, tatiyai jhānai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharāmi satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaimi, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ āvusau sammā vadamānau vadaiyya…pai॰… mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. catutthajhānapañhāsuttaṃ

335. ‘‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhāna’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau catutthaṃ jhānanti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati catutthaṃ jhāna’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau sukhasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ pamādau, catutthai jhānai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, catutthai jhānai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, catutthai jhānai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ  atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya…pai॰… mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. ākāsānañ‍cāyatanapañhāsuttaṃ

336. ‘‘‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatana’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau ākāsānañ‍cāyatananti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha  bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ  amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati ākāsānañ‍cāyatana’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ pamādau, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya…pai॰… mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanapañhāsuttaṃ

337. ‘‘‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatana’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatananti? tassa  mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatana’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ pamādau, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai  cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya…pai॰… mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanapañhāsuttaṃ

338. ‘‘‘ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatana’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatananti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatana’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pamādau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai  cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai  cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya…pai॰… mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanapañhāsuttaṃ

339. ‘‘‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatana’nti vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatananti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatana’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti|

‘‘atha  khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pamādau, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya…pai॰… mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. animittapañhāsuttaṃ

340. ‘‘‘animittau  caitausamādhi, animittau caitausamādhī’ti vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau animittau caitausamādhīti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu sabbanimittānaṃ  amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati animittau caitausamādhī’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau nimittānusāri viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna! mā, brāhmaṇa, animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ pamādau, animittai caitausamādhismiṃ cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, animittai caitausamādhismiṃ cittaṃ aikaudiṃ karauhi, animittai caitausamādhismiṃ cittaṃ samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārānuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’ti, mamaṃ taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārānuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sak‍kasuttaṃ

341. atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya; aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau pañ‍cahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ  yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ buddhaṃ saraṇagamanaṃ (sī॰) hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu buddhaṃ saraṇagamanahaitu (sī॰) khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| dhammasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, daivānaminda, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| dhammasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅgha…pai॰… sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau chahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau sattahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha  khau sak‍kau daivānamindau aṭṭhahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau asītiyā daivatāsahassaihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā  mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| dhammasaraṇagamanahaitu  khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, daivānaminda, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti…pai॰… sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

atha  khau sak‍kau daivānamindau pañ‍cahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau  bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ  laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ  laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai  sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

atha  khau sak‍kau daivānamindau chahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰…| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau sattahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰…| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau aṭṭhahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰…| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau asītiyā daivatāsahassaihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ  vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu  khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu  khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa  mauggal‍lāna, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau pañ‍cahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami  …pai॰… aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| dhammasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu  khau, daivānaminda, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘sādhu  khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina  yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau chahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau sattahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha  khau sak‍kau daivānamindau aṭṭhahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau asītiyā daivatāsahassaihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| buddhasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘sādhu  khau, daivānaminda, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti…pai॰… sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti…pai॰… sādhu khau mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅghasaraṇagamanaṃ hauti| saṅghasaraṇagamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau pañ‍cahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅghai  avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti…pai॰…|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

atha  khau sak‍kau daivānamindau chahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau sattahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau aṭṭhahi daivatāsataihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau asītiyā daivatāsahassaihi saddhiṃ yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca  –

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā  dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau  añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai  añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, daivānaminda, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, daivānaminda, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘svākkhātau  bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai  sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu  khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai añ‍ñai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. candanasuttaṃ

342. atha khau candanau nandanau (sī॰) daivaputtau…pai॰…|

atha khau suyāmau daivaputtau…pai॰…|

atha khau santusitau daivaputtau…pai॰…|

atha khau sunimmitau daivaputtau…pai॰…|

atha khau vasavatti daivaputtau…pai॰…|

(yathā sak‍kasuttaṃ tathā imai pañ‍ca paiyyālā vitthāraitabbā)| aikādasamaṃ|

mauggal‍lānasaṃyuttaṃ  samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

savitak‍kāvitak‍kañ‍ca, sukhaina ca upaikkhakau|

ākāsañ‍caiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ākiñ‍caṃ naivasañ‍ñinā|

animittau ca sak‍kau ca, candanaikādasaina cāti||


7. cittasaṃyuttaṃ

1. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

343. aikaṃ  samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū macchikāsaṇḍai viharanti ambāṭakavanai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti? tatraikac‍caihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi aivaṃ byākataṃ hauti – ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā cā’’ti| aikac‍caihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi aivaṃ byākataṃ hauti – ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina cittau gahapati migapathakaṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausi  khau cittau gahapati sambahulānaṃ kira thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ  san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti? tatraikac‍caihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi aivaṃ byākataṃ – ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā cā’’ti| aikac‍caihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi aivaṃ byākataṃ ‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nānanti| atha khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, sambahulānaṃ kira thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti? aikac‍caihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi  aivaṃ byākataṃ – ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā cā’’ti| aikac‍caihi thairaihi bhikkhūhi aivaṃ byākataṃ ‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, āvusau, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, gahapatī’’ti|

‘‘‘saṃyaujana’nti vā, bhantai, ‘saṃyaujaniyā dhammā’ti vā imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca| taina hi, bhantai, upamaṃ vau karissāmi| upamāyapidhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, kāḷau ca balībaddau audātau ca balībaddau  aikaina dāmaina vā yauttaina vā saṃyuttā assu| yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘kāḷau balībaddau audātassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ, audātau balībaddau kāḷassa balībaddassa saṃyaujana’nti, sammā nu khau sau vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, gahapati! na khau, gahapati, kāḷau balībaddau audātassa balībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ, napi audātau balībaddau kāḷassa baḷībaddassa saṃyaujanaṃ; yaina khau tai aikaina dāmaina vā yauttaina vā saṃyuttā taṃ tattha saṃyaujana’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhantai, na cakkhu rūpānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na rūpā cakkhussa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| na sautaṃ saddānaṃ… na ghānaṃ gandhānaṃ… na jivhā rasānaṃ… na kāyau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na phauṭṭhabbā kāyassa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| na manau dhammānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, na dhammā manassa saṃyaujanaṃ; yañ‍ca tattha tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandarāgau taṃ tattha saṃyaujana’’nti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati, yassa tai gambhīrai buddhavacanai pañ‍ñācakkhu kamatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamaisidattasuttaṃ

344. aikaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū macchikāsaṇḍai viharanti ambāṭakavanai| atha khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca  – ‘‘adhivāsaintu mai, bhantai, thairā svātanāya bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisuṃ khau thairā bhikkhū tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau cittau gahapati thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha  khau thairā bhikkhū tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina cittassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃsu|

atha khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, dhātunānatta’nti, bhantai thaira, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā thairau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, dhātunānatta’nti, bhantai thaira, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti? dutiyampi khau āyasmā thairau tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, dhātunānatta’nti, bhantai thaira, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti? tatiyampi khau āyasmā thairau tuṇhī ahausi|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā isidattau tasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai sabbanavakau hauti| atha khau āyasmā isidattau āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākaraumahaṃ, bhantai thaira, cittassa gahapatinau aitaṃ pañha’’nti? ‘‘byākarauhi tvaṃ, āvusau isidatta, cittassa gahapatinau aitaṃ pañha’’nti| ‘‘aivañhi tvaṃ, gahapati, pucchasi – ‘dhātunānattaṃ, dhātunānattanti, bhantai thaira, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhātunānattaṃ, vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘idaṃ khau, gahapati, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu…pai॰… manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aittāvatā khau, gahapati, dhātunānattaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti|

atha khau cittau gahapati āyasmatau isidattassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā thairai bhikkhū paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū bhuttāvinau aunītapattapāṇinau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā thairau āyasmantaṃ  isidattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu khau taṃ, āvusau isidatta, aisau pañhau paṭibhāsi, naisau pañhau maṃ paṭibhāsi| tainahāvusau isidatta, yadā añ‍ñathāpi yadā añ‍ñadāpi (sī॰ pī॰) añ‍ñadāpi (?) aivarūpau pañhau āgacchaiyya, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭibhāsaiyyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyaisidattasuttaṃ

345. aikaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū macchikāsaṇḍai viharanti ambāṭakavanai| atha khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaintu mai, bhantai thairā, svātanāya bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisuṃ khau thairā bhikkhū tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau cittau gahapati thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina cittassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃsu|

atha  khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yā imā, bhantai thaira, anaikavihitā diṭṭhiyau laukai uppaj‍janti – ‘sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā, anantavā laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā| yāni cimāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajālai bhaṇitāni; imā nu khau, bhantai, diṭṭhiyau kismiṃ sati haunti, kismiṃ asati na hauntī’’ti?

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā thairau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau cittau gahapati…pai॰… tatiyampi khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yā imā, bhantai thaira, anaikavihitā diṭṭhiyau laukai  uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā, anantavā  laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā| yāni cimāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajālai bhaṇitāni; imā nu khau, bhantai, diṭṭhiyau kismiṃ sati haunti, kismiṃ asati na hauntī’’ti? tatiyampi khau āyasmā thairau tuṇhī ahausi|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā isidattau tasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai sabbanavakau hauti| atha khau āyasmā isidattau āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākaraumahaṃ, bhantai thaira, cittassa gahapatinau aitaṃ pañha’’nti? ‘‘byākarauhi tvaṃ, āvusau isidatta, cittassa gahapatinau aitaṃ pañha’’nti| ‘‘aivañhi  tvaṃ, gahapati, pucchasi – ‘yā imā, bhantai thaira, anaikavihitā diṭṭhiyau laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā…pai॰…; imā nu khau, bhantai, diṭṭhiyau kismiṃ  sati haunti, kismiṃ asati na hauntī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yā imā, gahapati, anaikavihitā diṭṭhiyau laukai uppaj‍janti – ‘sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā anantavā laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā| yāni cimāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajālai bhaṇitāni; imā khau, gahapati, diṭṭhiyau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā sati haunti, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā asati na hauntī’’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, gahapati, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani  vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ  pana, bhantai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, gahapati, sutavā  ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti|

‘‘kutau, bhantai, ayyau isidattau āgacchatī’’ti? ‘‘avantiyā khau, gahapati, āgacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘atthi, bhantai, avantiyā isidattau nāma kulaputtau amhākaṃ adiṭṭhasahāyau pabbajitau? diṭṭhau sau āyasmatā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, gahapatī’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau sau, bhantai, āyasmā aitarahi viharatī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā isidattau tuṇhī ahausi| ‘‘ayyau nau, bhantai, isidattau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, gahapatī’’ti| ‘‘abhiramatu, bhantai, ayyau isidattau macchikāsaṇḍai| ramaṇīyaṃ ambāṭakavanaṃ| ahaṃ ayyassa isidattassa ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| ‘‘kalyāṇaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapatī’’ti|

atha khau cittau gahapati āyasmatau isidattassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā thairai bhikkhū paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū bhuttāvinau aunītapattapāṇinau  uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā thairau āyasmantaṃ isidattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu khau taṃ, āvusau isidatta, aisau pañhau paṭibhāsi| naisau pañhau maṃ paṭibhāsi| tainahāvusau isidatta, yadā añ‍ñathāpi aivarūpau pañhau āgacchaiyya, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭibhāsaiyyā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā isidattau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya macchikāsaṇḍamhā pak‍kāmi| yaṃ macchikāsaṇḍamhā pak‍kāmi, tathā pak‍kantauva ahausi, na puna pac‍cāgacchīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. mahakapāṭihāriyasuttaṃ

346. aikaṃ  samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū macchikāsaṇḍai viharanti ambāṭakavanai| atha  khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaintu mai, bhantai thairā, svātanāya gaukulai bhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisuṃ khau thairā bhikkhū tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau cittau gahapati thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina cittassa gahapatinau gaukulaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃsu|

atha khau cittau gahapati thairai bhikkhū paṇītaina sappipāyāsaina sahatthā santappaisi  sampavāraisi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū bhuttāvinau aunītapattapāṇinau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu| cittaupi khau gahapati ‘saisakaṃ vissaj‍jaithā’ti vatvā thairai bhikkhū piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| taina khau pana samayaina uṇhaṃ hauti kuthitaṃ kuṭṭhitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); tai ca thairā bhikkhū pavailiyamānaina mañ‍ñai kāyaina gacchanti, yathā taṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuttāvinau|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahakau tasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai sabbanavakau hauti| atha khau āyasmā mahakau āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu khvassa, bhantai thaira, sītakau ca vātau vāyaiyya, abbhasampilāpau abbhasaṃbilāpau (sī॰), abbhasaṃvilāpau (pī॰) ca assa, daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusāyaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khvassa, āvusau mahaka, yaṃ sītakau ca vātau vāyaiyya, abbhasampilāpau ca assa, daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusāyaiyyā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahakau tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ  abhisaṅkhari abhisaṅkhāsi (sī॰) yathā sītakau ca vātau vāyi, abbhasampilāpau ca assa āsi (?), daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusi| atha khau cittassa gahapatinau aitadahausi – ‘‘yau khau imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai sabbanavakau bhikkhu tassāyaṃ aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahakau ārāmaṃ sampāpuṇitvā āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alamaittāvatā, bhantai thairā’’ti? ‘‘alamaittāvatā, āvusau mahaka! katamaittāvatā, āvusau mahaka! pūjitamaittāvatā, āvusau mahakā’’ti| atha khau thairā bhikkhū yathāvihāraṃ agamaṃsu| āyasmāpi mahakau sakaṃ vihāraṃ agamāsi|

atha khau cittau gahapati yaināyasmā mahakau  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahakaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ mahakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, ayyau mahakau uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaitū’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gahapati, ālindai uttarāsaṅgaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā tiṇakalāpaṃ aukāsaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau cittau gahapati āyasmatau mahakassa paṭissutvā ālindai uttarāsaṅgaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā tiṇakalāpaṃ aukāsaisi| atha khau āyasmā mahakau vihāraṃ pavisitvā sūcighaṭikaṃ datvā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhari yathā tālacchiggaḷaina ca aggaḷantarikāya ca ac‍ci nikkhamitvā tiṇāni jhāpaisi, uttarāsaṅgaṃ na jhāpaisi| atha khau cittau gahapati uttarāsaṅgaṃ papphauṭaitvā saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| atha khau āyasmā mahakau vihārā nikkhamitvā cittaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alamaittāvatā, gahapatī’’ti?

‘‘alamaittāvatā, bhantai mahaka! katamaittāvatā, bhantai, mahaka! pūjitamaittāvatā, bhantai mahaka! abhiramatu, bhantai, ayyau mahakau macchikāsaṇḍai| ramaṇīyaṃ ambāṭakavanaṃ| ahaṃ ayyassa mahakassa ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| ‘‘kalyāṇaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapatī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahakau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya macchikāsaṇḍamhā pak‍kāmi| yaṃ macchikāsaṇḍamhā pak‍kāmi, tathā pak‍kantauva ahausi; na puna pac‍cāgacchīti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamakāmabhūsuttaṃ

347. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā kāmabhū macchikāsaṇḍai viharati ambāṭakavanai| atha khau cittau gahapati yaināyasmā kāmabhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ kāmabhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā kāmabhū aitadavauca –

‘‘vuttamidaṃ, gahapati –

‘‘nailaṅgau saitapacchādau, aikārau vattatī rathau|

anīghaṃ passa āyantaṃ appattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), chin‍nasautaṃ abandhana’’nti||

‘‘imassa nu khau, gahapati, saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ nu khau aitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā bhāsita’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, gahapatī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, muhuttaṃ āgamaihi yāvassa atthaṃ paikkhāmī’’ti| atha khau cittau gahapati muhuttaṃ tuṇhī hutvā āyasmantaṃ kāmabhuṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘nailaṅga’nti  khau, bhantai, sīlānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘saitapacchādau’ti khau, bhantai, vimuttiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘aikārau’ti khau, bhantai, satiyā aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘vattatī’ti khau, bhantai, abhik‍kamapaṭik‍kamassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘rathau’ti khau, bhantai, imassaitaṃ cātumahābhūtikassa kāyassa adhivacanaṃ mātāpaittikasambhavassa audanakummāsūpacayassa anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammassa| rāgau khau, bhantai, nīghau, dausau nīghau, mauhau nīghau| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu ‘anīghau’ti vuc‍cati| ‘āyanta’nti  khau, bhantai, arahatau aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘sautau’ti khau, bhantai, taṇhāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sā khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu ‘chin‍nasautau’ti vuc‍cati| rāgau khau, bhantai, bandhanaṃ, dausau bandhanaṃ, mauhau bandhanaṃ| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu ‘abandhanau’ti vuc‍cati| iti khau, bhantai, yaṃ taṃ bhagavatā vuttaṃ –

‘‘nailaṅgau saitapacchādau, aikārau vattatī rathau|

anīghaṃ passa āyantaṃ, chin‍nasautaṃ abandhana’’nti||

‘‘imassa  khau, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! yassa tai gambhīrai buddhavacanai pañ‍ñācakkhu kamatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyakāmabhūsuttaṃ

348. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā kāmabhū macchikāsaṇḍai viharati ambāṭakavanai| atha khau cittau gahapati yaināyasmā kāmabhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ kāmabhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, saṅkhārā’’ti? ‘‘tayau khau, gahapati, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau cittau gahapati āyasmatau kāmabhussa bhāsitaṃ  abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ kāmabhuṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, kāyasaṅkhārau, katamau vacīsaṅkhārau, katamau cittasaṅkhārau’’ti? ‘‘assāsapassāsā khau, gahapati, kāyasaṅkhārau, vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārau, sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau cittau gahapati…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘kasmā pana, bhantai, assāsapassāsā kāyasaṅkhārau, kasmā vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārau, kasmā sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārau’’ti? ‘‘assāsapassāsā khau, gahapati, kāyikā| aitai dhammā kāyappaṭibaddhā, tasmā assāsapassāsā kāyasaṅkhārau| pubbai khau, gahapati, vitak‍kaitvā vicāraitvā pacchā vācaṃ bhindati, tasmā vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārau| sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā| aitai dhammā cittappaṭibaddhā, tasmā sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

sādhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpatti hautī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, gahapati, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jissa’nti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā| atha khvassa pubbaiva tathā cittaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti yaṃ taṃ tathattāya upanaitī’’ti|

sādhu  …pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa  pana, bhantai, bhikkhunau katamai dhammā paṭhamaṃ nirujjhanti, yadi vā kāyasaṅkhārau, yadi vā vacīsaṅkhārau, yadi vā cittasaṅkhārau’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa khau, gahapati, bhikkhunau vacīsaṅkhārau paṭhamaṃ nirujjhati, tatau kāyasaṅkhārau, tatau cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

sādhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, matau kālaṅkatau, yau cāyaṃ bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau, imaisaṃ kiṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ gahapati, matau kālaṅkatau tassa kāyasaṅkhārau niruddhau paṭippassaddhau, vacīsaṅkhārau niruddhau paṭippassaddhau, cittasaṅkhārau niruddhau paṭippassaddhau, āyu parikkhīṇau, usmā vūpasantā, indriyāni viparibhin‍nāni| yau ca khvāyaṃ, gahapati, bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau, tassapi kāyasaṅkhārau niruddhau paṭippassaddhau, vacīsaṅkhārau niruddhau paṭippassaddhau, cittasaṅkhārau niruddhau paṭippassaddhau, āyu aparikkhīṇau, usmā avūpasantā, indriyāni vippasan‍nāni| yvāyaṃ, gahapati, matau kālaṅkatau, yau cāyaṃ bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau, idaṃ naisaṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti|
