sādhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, gahapati, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahantassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahissa’nti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā  vuṭṭhitau’ti vā| atha khvassa pubbaiva tathā cittaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti, yaṃ taṃ tathattāya upanaitī’’ti|

sādhu, bhantai…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahantassa pana, bhantai, bhikkhunau katamai dhammā paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍janti, yadi vā kāyasaṅkhārau, yadi vā vacīsaṅkhārau, yadi vā cittasaṅkhārau’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahantassa, gahapati, bhikkhunau cittasaṅkhārau paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati, tatau kāyasaṅkhārau, tatau vacīsaṅkhārau’’ti|

sādhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitaṃ pana, bhantai, bhikkhuṃ kati phassā phusanti’’? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitaṃ  khau, gahapati, bhikkhuṃ tayau phassā phusanti – suñ‍ñatau phassau, animittau phassau, appaṇihitau phassau’’ti|

sādhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitassa pana, bhantai, bhikkhunau kiṃnin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti, kiṃpauṇaṃ, kiṃpabbhāra’’nti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitassa khau, gahapati, bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāra’’nti|

‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau cittau gahapati āyasmatau kāmabhussa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ kāmabhuṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi  – ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā pana, bhantai, kati dhammā bahūpakārā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau tvaṃ, gahapati, yaṃ paṭhamaṃ pucchitabbaṃ taṃ pucchasi| api ca tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā khau, gahapati, dvai dhammā bahūpakārā – samathau ca vipassanā cā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. gaudattasuttaṃ

349. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā gaudattau macchikāsaṇḍai viharati ambāṭakavanai| atha  khau cittau gahapati yaināyasmā gaudattau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ gaudattaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā gaudattau aitadavauca – ‘‘yā cāyaṃ, gahapati, appamāṇā caitauvimutti, yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti, yā ca suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti, yā ca animittā caitauvimutti, imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti? ‘‘atthi, bhantai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca| atthi pana, bhantai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti|

‘‘katamau ca, bhantai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca? idha, bhantai, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā  dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ catutthiṃ (?)| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya  sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ  maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), abyābajjhaina (?) pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhantai, appamāṇā caitauvimutti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhantai, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti? idha, bhantai, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma, ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhantai, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhantai, suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti? idha, bhantai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā  attaniyaina vā’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhantai, suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhantai, animittā caitauvimutti? idha, bhantai, bhikkhu sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhantai, animittā caitauvimutti| ayaṃ khau, bhantai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā nānatthā caiva  nānābyañ‍janā ca|

‘‘katamau ca, bhantai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ? rāgau, bhantai, pamāṇakaraṇau, dausau pamāṇakaraṇau, mauhau pamāṇakaraṇau| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yāvatā khau, bhantai, appamāṇā caitauvimuttiyau, akuppā tāsaṃ caitauvimutti aggamakkhāyati| sā khau pana akuppā caitauvimutti suñ‍ñā rāgaina, suñ‍ñā dausaina, suñ‍ñā mauhaina| rāgau khau, bhantai, kiñ‍canaṃ, dausau kiñ‍canaṃ, mauhau kiñ‍canaṃ| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yāvatā khau, bhantai, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimuttiyau, akuppā tāsaṃ caitauvimutti aggamakkhāyati| sā khau  pana akuppā caitauvimutti suñ‍ñā rāgaina, suñ‍ñā dausaina, suñ‍ñā mauhaina| rāgau khau, bhantai, nimittakaraṇau, dausau nimittakaraṇau, mauhau nimittakaraṇau| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yāvatā khau, bhantai, animittā caitauvimuttiyau, akuppā tāsaṃ caitauvimutti aggamakkhāyati| sā khau pana akuppā caitauvimutti suñ‍ñā rāgaina, suñ‍ñā dausaina, suñ‍ñā mauhaina| ayaṃ khau, bhantai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma  imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! yassa tai gambhīrai buddhavacanai pañ‍ñācakkhu kamatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. nigaṇṭhanāṭaputtasuttaṃ

350. taina khau pana samayaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nātaputtau (sī॰) macchikāsaṇḍaṃ  anuppattau hauti mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ| assausi khau cittau gahapati – ‘‘nigaṇṭhau kira nāṭaputtau macchikāsaṇḍaṃ anuppattau mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhi’’nti| atha khau cittau gahapati sambahulaihi upāsakaihi saddhiṃ yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaina nāṭaputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘saddahasi tvaṃ, gahapati, samaṇassa gautamassa – atthi avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi, atthi vitak‍kavicārānaṃ niraudhau’’ti?

‘‘na khvāhaṃ aittha, bhantai, bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmi| atthi avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi, atthi vitak‍kavicārānaṃ niraudhau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau ul‍laukaitvā sakaṃ parisaṃ apalaukaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), aulaukaitvā (sī॰ aṭṭha॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰) aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ bhavantau passantu, yāva ujukau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva asaṭhau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva amāyāvī cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, vātaṃ vā sau jālaina bādhaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, yau vitak‍kavicārai  niraudhaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, sakamuṭṭhinā vā sau gaṅgāya sautaṃ āvāraitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, yau vitak‍kavicārai niraudhaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhantai, katamaṃ nu khau paṇītataraṃ – ñāṇaṃ vā saddhā vā’’ti? ‘‘saddhāya khau, gahapati, ñāṇaṃyaiva paṇītatara’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahaṃ khau, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahaṃ khau, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahaṃ khau, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| na sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau kassa añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā saddhāya gamissāmi? atthi avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi, atthi vitak‍kavicārānaṃ niraudhau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sakaṃ parisaṃ apalaukaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ bhavantau passantu, yāva anujukau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva saṭhau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva māyāvī cāyaṃ cittau gahapatī’’ti|

‘‘idānaiva khau tai idānaiva ca pana (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) mayaṃ, bhantai, bhāsitaṃ – ‘aivaṃ ājānāma  idaṃ bhavantau passantu, yāva ujukau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva asaṭhau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva amāyāvī cāyaṃ cittau gahapatī’ti| idānaiva ca pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhāsitaṃ – ‘aivaṃ ājānāma idaṃ bhavantau passantu, yāva anujukau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva saṭhau cāyaṃ cittau gahapati, yāva māyāvī cāyaṃ cittau gahapatī’ti| sacai tai, bhantai, purimaṃ sac‍caṃ, pacchimaṃ tai micchā| sacai pana tai, bhantai, purimaṃ micchā, pacchimaṃ tai sac‍caṃ| imai khau pana, bhantai, dasa sahadhammikā pañhā āgacchanti| yadā naisaṃ atthaṃ ājānaiyyāsi, atha maṃ paṭiharaiyyāsi saddhiṃ nigaṇṭhaparisāya| aikau pañhau, aikau uddaisau, aikaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ| dvai pañhā, dvai uddaisā, dvai vaiyyākaraṇāni| tayau pañhā, tayau uddaisā, tīṇi vaiyyākaraṇāni| cattārau pañhā, cattārau uddaisā, cattāri  vaiyyākaraṇāni| pañ‍ca pañhā, pañ‍ca uddaisā, pañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇāni| cha pañhā, cha uddaisā, cha vaiyyākaraṇāni| satta pañhā, satta uddaisā, satta vaiyyākaraṇāni| aṭṭha pañhā, aṭṭha  uddaisā, aṭṭha vaiyyākaraṇāni| nava pañhā, nava uddaisā, nava vaiyyākaraṇāni| dasa pañhā, dasa uddaisā, dasa vaiyyākaraṇānī’’ti| atha khau cittau gahapati nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ imai dasa sahadhammikai pañhai āpucchitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. acailakassapasuttaṃ

351. taina  khau pana samayaina acailau kassapau macchikāsaṇḍaṃ anuppattau hauti cittassa gahapatinau purāṇagihisahāyau| assausi khau cittau gahapati – ‘‘acailau kira kassapau macchikāsaṇḍaṃ anuppattau amhākaṃ purāṇagihisahāyau’’ti| atha khau cittau gahapati yaina acailau kassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā acailaina kassapaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cittau gahapati acailaṃ kassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīvaciraṃ pabbajitassa, bhantai, kassapā’’ti? ‘‘tiṃsamattāni khau mai, gahapati, vassāni pabbajitassā’’ti| ‘‘imaihi pana tai, bhantai, tiṃsamattaihi vassaihi atthi kauci uttarimanussadhammā uttarimanussadhammau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘imaihi khau mai, gahapati, tiṃsamattaihi vassaihi pabbajitassa natthi kauci uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau, añ‍ñatra naggaiyyā ca muṇḍaiyyā ca pāvaḷanipphauṭanāya cā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, cittau gahapati acailaṃ kassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! dhammassa svākkhātatā sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati yatra hi nāma tiṃsamattaihi vassaihi na kauci  uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau abhavissa phāsuvihārau, añ‍ñatra naggaiyyā ca muṇḍaiyyā ca pāvaḷanipphauṭanāya cā’’ti!

‘‘tuyhaṃ pana, gahapati, kīvaciraṃ  upāsakattaṃ upagatassā’’ti? ‘‘mayhampi khau pana, bhantai, tiṃsamattāni vassāni upāsakattaṃ upagatassā’’ti| ‘‘imaihi pana tai, gahapati, tiṃsamattaihi vassaihi atthi kauci uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau  adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘gihinaupi siyā, bhantai| ahañhi, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā  …pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi, sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| sacai khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhagavatā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) paṭhamataraṃ kālaṃ karaiyyaṃ, anacchariyaṃ khau panaitaṃ yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘natthi taṃ saṃyaujanaṃ yaina saṃyaujanaina saṃyuttau cittau gahapati puna imaṃ laukaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, acailau kassapau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! dhammassa svākkhātatā, yatra hi nāma gihī audātavasanau gihī audātavasanā (sī॰ pī॰) aivarūpaṃ uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ adhigamissati adhigamissanti (sī॰ pī॰) phāsuvihāraṃ| labhaiyyāhaṃ, gahapati, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

atha khau cittau gahapati  acailaṃ kassapaṃ ādāya yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, acailau kassapau amhākaṃ purāṇagihisahāyau| imaṃ thairā pabbājaintu upasampādaintu| ahamassa ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| alattha khau acailau kassapau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau ca panāyasmā kassapau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā kassapau arahataṃ ahausīti| navamaṃ|

10. gilānadassanasuttaṃ

352. taina  khau pana samayaina cittau gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau sambahulā ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu  adhivatthā daivatā saṅgamma samāgamma cittaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘paṇidhaihi, gahapati, anāgatamaddhānaṃ rājā assaṃ cak‍kavattī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, cittau gahapati tā ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā aitadavauca – ‘‘tampi anic‍caṃ, tampi addhuvaṃ, tampi pahāya gamanīya’’nti| aivaṃ  vuttai, cittassa gahapatinau mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā  cittaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘satiṃ, ayyaputta, upaṭṭhapaihi, mā vippalapī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ tāhaṃ vadāmi yaṃ maṃ tumhai aivaṃ vadaitha – ‘satiṃ, ayyaputta, upaṭṭhapaihi, mā vippalapī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau tvaṃ, ayyaputta, vadaisi – ‘tampi anic‍caṃ, tampi addhuvaṃ, tampi pahāya gamanīya’’’nti| ‘‘tathā hi pana maṃ ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘paṇidhaihi, gahapati, anāgatamaddhānaṃ rājā assaṃ cak‍kavattī’ti| tāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘tampi anic‍caṃ…pai॰… tampi pahāya gamanīya’’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tā, ayyaputta, ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā atthavasaṃ sampassamānā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘paṇidhaihi, gahapati, anāgatamaddhānaṃ rājā assaṃ cak‍kavattī’’’ti? ‘‘tāsaṃ khau ārāmadaivatānaṃ vanadaivatānaṃ rukkhadaivatānaṃ ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthānaṃ daivatānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ khau cittau gahapati, sīlavā sīlavantau (ka॰) kalyāṇadhammau| sacai paṇidahissati – anāgatamaddhānaṃ rājā assaṃ cak‍kavattī’ti, ‘tassa khau ayaṃ ijjhissati, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā dhammikau dhammikaṃ phalaṃ anupassatī’ti| imaṃ khau tā ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā atthavasaṃ sampassamānā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘paṇidhaihi, gahapati, anāgatamaddhānaṃ rājā assaṃ  cak‍kavattī’ti| tāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘tampi anic‍caṃ, tampi addhuvaṃ, tampi pahāya gamanīya’’’nti|

‘‘taina  hi, ayyaputta, amhaipi auvadāhī’’ti| ‘‘tasmā hi vau aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – buddhai  avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci kulai daiyyadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ appaṭivibhattaṃ bhavissati sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihīti aivañhi vau sikkhitabba’’nti| atha khau cittau gahapati mittāmac‍cai ñātisālauhitai buddhai ca dhammai ca saṅghai ca cāgai ca samādapaitvā kālamakāsīti| dasamaṃ|

cittasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

saṃyaujanaṃ  dvai isidattā, mahakau kāmabhūpi ca|

gaudattau ca nigaṇṭhau ca, acailaina gilānadassananti||


8. gāmaṇisaṃyuttaṃ

1. caṇḍasuttaṃ

353. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha  khau caṇḍau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau caṇḍau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cau caṇḍau caṇḍautvaiva yaina midhaikac‍cau caṇḍautaiva (sī॰ pī॰) saṅkhaṃ gacchati| kau pana, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cau sauratau sauratautvaiva yaina midhaikac‍cau suratautaiva (sī॰ pī॰) saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cassa rāgau appahīnau hauti| rāgassa appahīnattā parai kaupainti, paraihi kaupiyamānau kaupaṃ pātukarauti| sau caṇḍautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| dausau appahīnau hauti| dausassa appahīnattā parai kaupainti, paraihi kaupiyamānau kaupaṃ pātukarauti| sau caṇḍautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| mauhau appahīnau hauti| mauhassa appahīnattā parai kaupainti, paraihi kaupiyamānau kaupaṃ pātukarauti| sau caṇḍautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cau caṇḍau caṇḍautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati’’|

‘‘idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cassa rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahīnattā parai na kaupainti, paraihi kaupiyamānau kaupaṃ na pātukarauti| sau sauratautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| dausau pahīnau hauti| dausassa pahīnattā parai na kaupainti, paraihi kaupiyamānau kaupaṃ  na pātukarauti| sau sauratautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| mauhau pahīnau hauti| mauhassa pahīnattā parai na kaupainti, paraihi kaupiyamānau kaupaṃ na pātukarauti| sau sauratautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cau sauratau sauratautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, caṇḍau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā  vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. tālapuṭasuttaṃ

354. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau tālapuṭau tālaputtau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) naṭagāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau tālapuṭau naṭagāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ naṭānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai sac‍cālikaina janaṃ hāsaiti ramaiti, sau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pahāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ| mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| dutiyampi khau tālapuṭau naṭagāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ naṭānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai sac‍cālikaina janaṃ hāsaiti ramaiti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pahāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ| mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| tatiyampi khau tālapuṭau naṭagāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ naṭānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai sac‍cālikaina janaṃ hāsaiti ramaiti, sau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pahāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti?

‘‘addhā khau tyāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, na labhāmi nālatthaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) – ‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ, mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’ti| api ca tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| pubbai khau, gāmaṇi, sattā avītarāgā rāgabandhanabaddhā| taisaṃ naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai yai dhammā rajanīyā tai upasaṃharati bhiyyausaumattāya| pubbai khau, gāmaṇi, sattā avītadausā dausabandhanabaddhā| taisaṃ naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai yai dhammā  dausanīyā tai  upasaṃharati bhiyyausaumattāya| pubbai khau, gāmaṇi, sattā avītamauhā mauhabandhanabaddhā| taisaṃ naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai yai dhammā mauhanīyā tai upasaṃharati bhiyyausaumattāya| sau attanā mattau pamattau parai madaitvā pamādaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pahāsau nāma nirayau tattha upapaj‍jati| sacai khau panassa aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘yau sau naṭau raṅgamajjhai samaj‍jamajjhai sac‍cālikaina janaṃ hāsaiti ramaiti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pahāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti, sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhikassa khau panāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, purisapuggalassa dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, tālapuṭau naṭagāmaṇi, paraudi, assūni pavattaisi| ‘‘aitaṃ khau tyāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, nālatthaṃ – ‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, aitaṃ raudāmi yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha; api cāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbakaihi ācariyapācariyaihi naṭaihi dīgharattaṃ nikatau vañ‍citau paluddhau – ‘yau sau naṭau raṅgamajjhai  samaj‍jamajjhai sac‍cālikaina janaṃ hāsaiti ramaiti sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā pahāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’’ti| ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā  maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| alattha khau tālapuṭau naṭagāmaṇi bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau ca panāyasmā tālapuṭau…pai॰… arahataṃ ahausīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. yaudhājīvasuttaṃ

355. atha khau yaudhājīvau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau yaudhājīvau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ yaudhājīvānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau yaudhājīvau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ  ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau yaudhājīvau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ yaudhājīvānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau yaudhājīvau saṅgāmai  ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti?

‘‘addhā  khau tyāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, na labhāmi – ‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’ti| api ca tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| yau sau, gāmaṇi, yaudhājīvau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tassa taṃ cittaṃ pubbai gahitaṃ hīnaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) duk‍kaṭaṃ duppaṇihitaṃ – ‘imai sattā hañ‍ñantu vā bajjhantu vā ucchij‍jantu vā vinassantu vā mā vā ahaisuṃ iti vā’ti| tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti; sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitau nāma nirayau tattha upapaj‍jatīti| sacai khau panassa aivaṃ diṭṭhi hauti – ‘yau sau yaudhājīvau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti, sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhikassa khau panāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, purisapuggalassa dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, yaudhājīvau gāmaṇi paraudi, assūni pavattaisi| ‘‘aitaṃ khau tyāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, nālatthaṃ – ‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, aitaṃ raudāmi yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha; apicāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbakaihi ācariyapācariyaihi yaudhājīvaihi dīgharattaṃ nikatau vañ‍citau paluddhau – ‘yau sau yaudhājīvau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’’ti| ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. hatthārauhasuttaṃ

356. atha  khau hatthārauhau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gatanti| catutthaṃ|

5. assārauhasuttaṃ

357. atha khau assārauhau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau assārauhau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ assārauhānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau assārauhau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau assārauhau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, pubbakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ assārauhānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘yau sau assārauhau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ  parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti?

‘‘addhā khau tyāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, na labhāmi – ‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’ti| api ca khau tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| yau sau, gāmaṇi, assārauhau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati tassa  taṃ cittaṃ pubbai gahitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ duppaṇihitaṃ – ‘imai sattā hañ‍ñantu vā bajjhantu vā ucchij‍jantu vā vinassantu vā mā ahaisuṃ iti vā’ti| tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitau nāma nirayau tattha upapaj‍jati| sacai khau panassa aivaṃ diṭṭhi hauti – ‘yau sau assārauhau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti, sāssa hauti micchādiṭṭhi| micchādiṭṭhikassa khau panāhaṃ gāmaṇi, purisapuggalassa dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, assārauhau gāmaṇi paraudi, assūni pavattaisi| ‘‘aitaṃ khau tyāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, nālatthaṃ – ‘alaṃ, gāmaṇi, tiṭṭhataitaṃ; mā maṃ aitaṃ pucchī’’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, aitaṃ raudāmi yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha| apicāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbakaihi ācariyapācariyaihi assārauhaihi dīgharattaṃ nikatau vañ‍citau paluddhau – ‘yau  sau assārauhau saṅgāmai ussahati vāyamati, tamainaṃ ussahantaṃ vāyamantaṃ parai hananti pariyāpādainti, sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parajitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’’ti| ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. asibandhakaputtasuttaṃ

358. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha  khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhantai, pacchā bhūmakā kāmaṇḍalukā saivālamālikā udakaurauhakā aggiparicārakā| tai mataṃ kālaṅkataṃ uyyāpainti nāma sañ‍ñāpainti nāma saggaṃ nāma auk‍kāmainti| bhagavā pana, bhantai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pahauti tathā kātuṃ yathā sabbau laukau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, gāmaṇi, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, idhassa purisau pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhikau| tamainaṃ mahā janakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya – ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatū’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu sau purisau mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu  vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikā anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, purisau mahatiṃ puthusilaṃ gambhīrai udakarahadai pakkhipaiyya| tamainaṃ mahā janakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya  pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya – ‘ummuj‍ja, bhau puthusilai, up‍lava, bhau puthusilai, thalamup‍lava, bhau puthusilai’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu sā puthusilā mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikā anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā ummuj‍jaiyya vā up‍lavaiyya vā thalaṃ vā up‍lavaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhikau| kiñ‍cāpi taṃ mahā janakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya – ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatū’’’ti, atha khau sau purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, idhassa idha (ka॰), idha cassa (?) purisau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau musāvādā paṭiviratau pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau  samphappalāpā paṭiviratau anabhijjhālu abyāpan‍nacittau sammādiṭṭhikau| tamainaṃ mahā janakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya – ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatū’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu sau purisau mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikā anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, purisau sappikumbhaṃ vā tailakumbhaṃ vā gambhīrai udakarahadai augāhaitvā bhindaiyya| tatra yāssa sakkharā vā kaṭhalā vā sā adhaugāmī adhaugāminī (?) assa; yañ‍ca khvassa tatra sappi vā tailaṃ vā taṃ uddhaṃ gāmi  assa| tamainaṃ mahā janakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya – ‘ausīda, bhau sappitaila, saṃsīda, bhau sappitaila, adhau gaccha, bhau sappitailā’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api  nu taṃ sappitailaṃ mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikā anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā ausīdaiyya vā saṃsīdaiyya vā adhau vā gacchaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau, musāvādā paṭiviratau, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau, anabhijjhālu, abyāpan‍nacittau, sammādiṭṭhikau, kiñ‍cāpi taṃ mahā janakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya – ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatū’ti, atha khau sau purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. khaittūpamasuttaṃ

359. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nanu, bhantai, bhagavā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, gāmaṇi, tathāgatau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharatī’’ti| ‘‘atha kiñ‍carahi, bhantai, bhagavā aikac‍cānaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, aikac‍cānaṃ nau tathā sak‍kac‍caṃ dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti? ‘‘taina  hi, gāmaṇi, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, idhassu idha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kassakassa gahapatinau tīṇi khaittāni – aikaṃ khaittaṃ aggaṃ, aikaṃ khaittaṃ majjhimaṃ, aikaṃ khaittaṃ hīnaṃ jaṅgalaṃ ūsaraṃ  pāpabhūmi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, asu kassakau gahapati bījāni patiṭṭhāpaitukāmau kattha paṭhamaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiyya, yaṃ vā aduṃ khaittaṃ aggaṃ, yaṃ vā aduṃ khaittaṃ majjhimaṃ, yaṃ vā aduṃ khaittaṃ hīnaṃ jaṅgalaṃ ūsaraṃ pāpabhūmī’’ti? ‘‘asu, bhantai, kassakau gahapati bījāni patiṭṭhāpaitukāmau yaṃ aduṃ khaittaṃ aggaṃ tattha patiṭṭhāpaiyya| tattha patiṭṭhāpaitvā yaṃ aduṃ khaittaṃ majjhimaṃ tattha patiṭṭhāpaiyya| tattha patiṭṭhāpaitvā yaṃ aduṃ khaittaṃ hīnaṃ jaṅgalaṃ ūsaraṃ pāpabhūmi tattha patiṭṭhāpaiyyapi, naupi patiṭṭhāpaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? antamasau gaubhattampi bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, yaṃ aduṃ khaittaṃ aggaṃ; aivamaiva mayhaṃ bhikkhubhikkhuniyau| taisāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? aitai hi, gāmaṇi, maṃdīpā maṃlaiṇā maṃtāṇā maṃsaraṇā viharanti| saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, yaṃ aduṃ khaittaṃ majjhimaṃ; aivamaiva mayhaṃ upāsakaupāsikāyau| taisaṃ pāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? aitai hi, gāmaṇi, maṃdīpā maṃlaiṇā maṃtāṇā maṃsaraṇā viharanti| saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, yaṃ aduṃ khaittaṃ hīnaṃ jaṅgalaṃ  ūsaraṃ pāpabhūmi; aivamaiva mayhaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇaparibbājakā| taisaṃ pāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāma aikaṃ padampi ājānaiyyuṃ taṃ naisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, purisassa tayau udakamaṇikā – aikau udakamaṇikau acchiddau ahārī aparihārī, aikau udakamaṇikau acchiddau hārī parihārī, aikau udakamaṇikau chiddau hārī parihārī| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, asu purisau udakaṃ nikkhipitukāmau kattha paṭhamaṃ nikkhipaiyya, yau vā sau udakamaṇikau acchiddau ahārī aparihārī, yau vā sau udakamaṇikau acchiddau hārī parihārī, yau vā sau udakamaṇikau chiddau hārī parihārī’’ti? ‘‘asu, bhantai, purisau udakaṃ nikkhipitukāmau, yau sau udakamaṇikau acchiddau ahārī aparihārī tattha nikkhipaiyya, tattha nikkhipitvā, yau sau udakamaṇikau acchiddau hārī parihārī tattha nikkhipaiyya, tattha nikkhipitvā, yau sau udakamaṇikau chiddau hārī  parihārī tattha nikkhipaiyyapi, naupi nikkhipaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? antamasau bhaṇḍadhauvanampi bhavissatī’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, yau sau udakamaṇikau acchiddau ahārī aparihārī; aivamaiva mayhaṃ bhikkhubhikkhuniyau| taisāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ  sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? aitai hi, gāmaṇi, maṃdīpā maṃlaiṇā maṃtāṇā maṃsaraṇā viharanti| saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, yau sau udakamaṇikau acchiddau hārī parihārī; aivamaiva mayhaṃ upāsakaupāsikāyau| taisāhaṃ  dhammaṃ daisaimi – ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? aitai hi, gāmaṇi, maṃdīpā maṃlaiṇā maṃtāṇā maṃsaraṇā viharanti| saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, yau sau udakamaṇikau chiddau hārī parihārī; aivamaiva mayhaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇaparibbājakā| taisāhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? appaiva nāma aikaṃ padampi ājānaiyyuṃ, taṃ naisaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. saṅkhadhamasuttaṃ

360. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi nigaṇṭhasāvakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau asibandhakaputtaṃ gāmaṇiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, gāmaṇi, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau  sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yau kauci pāṇaṃ atipātaiti, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau, yau kauci adin‍naṃ ādiyati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau, yau kauci kāmaisu micchā carati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau, yau kauci musā bhaṇati sabbau, sau āpāyikau nairayikau| yaṃbahulaṃ yaṃbahulaṃ viharati, taina taina nīyatī’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti| ‘‘‘yaṃbahulaṃ yaṃbahulañ‍ca, gāmaṇi, viharati, taina taina  nīyati’, aivaṃ santai na kauci āpāyikau nairayikau bhavissati, yathā nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa vacanaṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau pāṇātipātī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, katamau bahutarau samayau, yaṃ vā sau pāṇamatipātaiti, yaṃ vā sau pāṇaṃ nātipātaitī’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau pāṇātipātī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, appatarau sau samayau yaṃ sau pāṇamatipātaiti, atha khau svaiva bahutarau samayau yaṃ sau pāṇaṃ nātipātaitī’’ti| ‘‘‘yaṃbahulaṃ yaṃbahulañ‍ca, gāmaṇi, viharati taina taina nīyatī’ti, aivaṃ santai na kauci āpāyikau nairayikau bhavissati, yathā nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa vacanaṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau adin‍nādāyī rattiyā  vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, katamau bahutarau samayau, yaṃ vā sau adin‍naṃ ādiyati, yaṃ vā sau adin‍naṃ nādiyatī’’ti| ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau adin‍nādāyī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya appatarau sau samayau, yaṃ sau adin‍naṃ ādiyati, atha khau svaiva bahutarau samayau, yaṃ sau adin‍naṃ nādiyatī’’ti| ‘‘‘yaṃbahulaṃ yaṃbahulañ‍ca, gāmaṇi, viharati taina taina nīyatī’ti, aivaṃ santai na kauci āpāyikau nairayikau bhavissati, yathā nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa vacanaṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau kāmaisumicchācārī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, katamau bahutarau samayau, yaṃ vā sau kāmaisu micchā carati, yaṃ vā sau kāmaisu micchā na caratī’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau kāmaisumicchācārī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, appatarau sau samayau yaṃ sau kāmaisu micchā carati, atha khau svaiva bahutarau samayau, yaṃ sau kāmaisu micchā na caratī’’ti| ‘‘‘yaṃbahulaṃ  yaṃbahulañ‍ca, gāmaṇi, viharati taina taina nīyatī’ti, aivaṃ santai na kauci āpāyikau nairayikau bhavissati, yathā nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa vacanaṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau musāvādī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, katamau bahutarau samayau, yaṃ vā sau musā bhaṇati, yaṃ vā sau musā na bhaṇatī’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau musāvādī rattiyā vā divasassa vā samayāsamayaṃ upādāya, appatarau sau samayau, yaṃ sau musā bhaṇati, atha khau svaiva bahutarau samayau, yaṃ sau musā na bhaṇatī’’ti| ‘‘‘yaṃbahulaṃ yaṃbahulañ‍ca, gāmaṇi, viharati taina taina nīyatī’ti, aivaṃ santai na kauci āpāyikau nairayikau bhavissati, yathā nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa vacanaṃ’’|

‘‘idha, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau satthā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhī (ka॰) – ‘yau kauci pāṇamatipātaiti, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau, yau kauci adin‍naṃ ādiyati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau, yau kauci kāmaisu micchā carati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau, yau kauci musā bhaṇati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikau’ti| tasmiṃ khau pana, gāmaṇi, satthari sāvakau abhippasan‍nau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayhaṃ khau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – yau kauci pāṇamatipātaiti, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikauti| atthi khau pana mayā pāṇau atipātitau, ahampamhi āpāyikau nairayikauti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati| taṃ, gāmaṇi, vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| mayhaṃ khau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – yau kauci adin‍naṃ ādiyati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikauti| atthi khau pana mayā adin‍naṃ ādin‍naṃ ahampamhi āpāyikau nairayikauti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati| taṃ, gāmaṇi, vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ  appaṭinissaj‍jitvā yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| mayhaṃ khau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – yau kauci kāmaisu micchā carati, sabbau sau  āpāyikau nairayikau’ti| atthi khau pana mayā kāmaisu micchā ciṇṇaṃ| ‘ahampamhi āpāyikau nairayikau’ti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati| taṃ, gāmaṇi, vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| mayhaṃ khau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – yau kauci musā bhaṇati, sabbau sau āpāyikau nairayikauti| atthi khau pana mayā musā bhaṇitaṃ| ‘ahampamhi  āpāyikau nairayikau’ti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati| taṃ, gāmaṇi, vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘idha  pana, gāmaṇi, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau anaikapariyāyaina pāṇātipātaṃ garahati vigarahati, ‘pāṇātipātā viramathā’ti cāha| adin‍nādānaṃ garahati vigarahati, ‘adin‍nādānā viramathā’ti cāha| kāmaisumicchācāraṃ garahati, vigarahati ‘kāmaisumicchācārā viramathā’ti cāha| musāvādaṃ garahati vigarahati ‘musāvādā viramathā’ti cāha| tasmiṃ khau pana, gāmaṇi, satthari sāvakau abhippasan‍nau hauti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘bhagavā khau anaikapariyāyaina pāṇātipātaṃ garahati  vigarahati, pāṇātipātā viramathāti cāha| atthi khau pana mayā pāṇau atipātitau yāvatakau vā tāvatakau vā| yau khau pana mayā pāṇau atipātitau yāvatakau vā tāvatakau vā, taṃ na suṭṭhu, taṃ na sādhu| ahañ‍caiva ahañ‍cai (?) khau pana tappac‍cayā vippaṭisārī assaṃ| na maitaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ pāpakammaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) akataṃ bhavissatī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya tañ‍caiva pāṇātipātaṃ pajahati| āyatiñ‍ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘‘bhagavā khau anaikapariyāyaina adin‍nādānaṃ garahati vigarahati, adin‍nādānā viramathāti cāha| atthi khau pana mayā adin‍naṃ ādin‍naṃ yāvatakaṃ vā tāvatakaṃ vā| yaṃ khau pana mayā adin‍naṃ ādin‍naṃ yāvatakaṃ vā tāvatakaṃ vā taṃ na suṭṭhu, taṃ na sādhu| ahañ‍caiva khau pana tappac‍cayā vippaṭisārī assaṃ, na maitaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ akataṃ bhavissatī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya tañ‍caiva adin‍nādānaṃ  pajahati| āyatiñ‍ca adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘‘bhagavā khau pana anaikapariyāyaina kāmaisumicchācāraṃ garahati vigarahati, kāmaisumicchācārā viramathāti cāha| atthi khau pana mayā kāmaisu micchā ciṇṇaṃ yāvatakaṃ vā tāvatakaṃ vā| yaṃ khau pana  mayā kāmaisu micchā ciṇṇaṃ yāvatakaṃ vā tāvatakaṃ vā taṃ  na suṭṭhu, taṃ na sādhu| ahañ‍caiva khau pana tappac‍cayā vippaṭisārī assaṃ, na  maitaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ akataṃ bhavissatī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya tañ‍caiva kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pajahati, āyatiñ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘‘bhagavā khau pana anaikapariyāyaina musāvādaṃ garahati vigarahati, musāvādā viramathāti cāha| atthi khau pana mayā musā bhaṇitaṃ yāvatakaṃ vā tāvatakaṃ vā| yaṃ khau pana mayā musā bhaṇitaṃ yāvatakaṃ vā tāvatakaṃ vā taṃ na suṭṭhu, taṃ na sādhu| ahañ‍caiva khau pana tappac‍cayā vippaṭisārī assaṃ, na maitaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ akataṃ bhavissatī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya tañ‍caiva musāvādaṃ pajahati, āyatiñ‍ca musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpassa kammassa samatik‍kamau hauti|

‘‘sau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti| adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti| kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti| musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti| pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti| pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti| samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| abhijjhaṃ pahāya  anabhijjhālu hauti| byāpādappadausaṃ  pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau hauti| micchādiṭṭhiṃ pahāya sammādiṭṭhikau hauti|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, balavā saṅkhadhamau appakasirainaiva catuddisā viñ‍ñāpaiyya; aivamaiva khau, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ bhāvitāya maittāya caitauvimuttiyā aivaṃ bahulīkatāya yaṃ pamāṇakataṃ kammaṃ, na taṃ tatrāvasissati, na taṃ tatrāvatiṭṭhati|

‘‘sa  khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau  paṭissatau karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰…| upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, balavā saṅkhadhamau appakasirainaiva catuddisā viñ‍ñāpaiyya; aivamaiva khau, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ bhāvitāya upaikkhāya caitauvimuttiyā aivaṃ bahulīkatāya yaṃ pamāṇakataṃ kammaṃ  na taṃ tatrāvasissati, na taṃ tatrāvatiṭṭhatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kulasuttaṃ

361. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina nāḷandā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai|

taina khau pana samayaina nāḷandā dubbhikkhā hauti dvīhitikā saitaṭṭhikā salākāvuttā| taina khau pana samayaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nāḷandāyaṃ paṭivasati mahatiyā nigaṇṭhaparisāya saddhiṃ| atha khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi nigaṇṭhasāvakau yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau asibandhakaputtaṃ gāmaṇiṃ nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, gāmaṇi, samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaihi| aivaṃ tai kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchissati – ‘asibandhakaputtaina gāmaṇinā samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃmahiddhikassa aivaṃmahānubhāvassa vādau āraupitau’’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ panāhaṃ, bhantai, samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃmahiddhikassa aivaṃmahānubhāvassa vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’’ti? ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, gāmaṇi, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ  vadaihi – ‘nanu, bhantai, bhagavā anaikapariyāyaina kulānaṃ  anuddayaṃ anudayaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) vaṇṇaiti, anurakkhaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anukampaṃ vaṇṇaitī’ti? sacai khau, gāmaṇi, samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarauti – ‘aivaṃ, gāmaṇi, tathāgatau anaikapariyāyaina kulānaṃ anuddayaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anurakkhaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anukampaṃ vaṇṇaitī’ti, tamainaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘atha kiñ‍carahi, bhantai, bhagavā dubbhikkhai dvīhitikai saitaṭṭhikai salākāvuttai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ cārikaṃ carati? ucchaidāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau, anayāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau, upaghātāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau’ti! imaṃ khau tai, gāmaṇi, samaṇau gautamau ubhataukauṭikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau naiva sakkhati sakkhiti (sī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.83 uggilituṃ, naiva sakkhati augilitu’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘nanu, bhantai, bhagavā anaikapariyāyaina kulānaṃ anuddayaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anurakkhaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anukampaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, gāmaṇi, tathāgatau anaikapariyāyaina kulānaṃ anuddayaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anurakkhaṃ vaṇṇaiti, anukampaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti| ‘‘atha kiñ‍carahi, bhantai, bhagavā dubbhikkhai dvīhitikai saitaṭṭhikai salākāvuttai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ cārikaṃ  carati? ucchaidāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau, anayāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau, upaghātāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau’’ti| ‘‘itau sau, gāmaṇi, aikanavutikappai aikanavutau kappau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aikanavutikappau (pī॰) yamahaṃ anussarāmi, nābhijānāmi kiñ‍ci kulaṃ pak‍kabhikkhānuppadānamattaina upahatapubbaṃ| atha khau yāni tāni kulāni aḍḍhāni mahaddhanāni mahābhaugāni pahūtajātarūparajatāni pahūtavittūpakaraṇāni pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñāni, sabbāni tāni dānasambhūtāni caiva sac‍casambhūtāni ca sāmañ‍ñasambhūtāni ca| aṭṭha khau, gāmaṇi, haitū, aṭṭha pac‍cayā kulānaṃ upaghātāya| rājatau vā kulāni upaghātaṃ gacchanti, cauratau vā kulāni upaghātaṃ gacchanti, aggitau vā kulāni upaghātaṃ gacchanti, udakatau vā kulāni upaghātaṃ gacchanti, nihitaṃ vā ṭhānā vigacchati nihitaṃ vā nādhigacchanti (sī॰ pī॰), duppayuttā vā kammantā vipaj‍janti, kulai vā kulaṅgārauti kulānaṃ vā kulaṅgārau (sī॰) uppaj‍jati, yau tai bhaugai vikirati  vidhamati viddhaṃsaiti, anic‍catāyai aṭṭhamīti| imai khau, gāmaṇi, aṭṭha haitū, aṭṭha pac‍cayā kulānaṃ upaghātāya| imaisu khau, gāmaṇi, aṭṭhasu haitūsu, aṭṭhasu pac‍cayaisu saṃvij‍jamānaisu yau maṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau, anayāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau, upaghātāya bhagavā kulānaṃ paṭipan‍nau’ti, taṃ, gāmaṇi, vācaṃ appahāya taṃ cittaṃ appahāya taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaj‍jitvā yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti| aivaṃ  vuttai, asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. maṇicūḷakasuttaṃ

362. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina rājantaipurai rājaparisāya san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ, sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajata’’nti!

taina khau pana samayaina maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇi tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇi taṃ parisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā ayyau ayyā (sī॰ pī॰) aivaṃ avacuttha| na kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ, na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, nappaṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatā’’ti| asakkhi khau maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇi taṃ parisaṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ| atha khau maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, rājantaipurai rājaparisāya san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ, sādiyanti samaṇā  sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajata’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ parisaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘mā ayyau aivaṃ avacuttha| na kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ  jātarūparajataṃ, na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, nappaṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā  jātarūparajataṃ, nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatā’ti| asakkhiṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, taṃ parisaṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ| kac‍cāhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau haumi, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhāmi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraumi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti?

‘‘taggha tvaṃ, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva mai hausi, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhasi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarausi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati| na hi, gāmaṇi, kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ, na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, nappaṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ, nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatā| yassa khau, gāmaṇi, jātarūparajataṃ kappati, pañ‍capi tassa kāmaguṇā kappanti| yassa pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā kappanti ( ) (tassapi jātarūparajataṃ kappati,) (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aikaṃsainaitaṃ, gāmaṇi, dhāraiyyāsi assamaṇadhammau asakyaputtiyadhammauti| api cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ vadāmi – tiṇaṃ tiṇatthikaina pariyaisitabbaṃ, dāru dārutthikaina pariyaisitabbaṃ, sakaṭaṃ sakaṭatthikaina pariyaisitabbaṃ, purisau purisatthikaina  pariyaisitabbau pariyaisitabbau’’ti (?)| natvaivāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, kainaci pariyāyaina ‘jātarūparajataṃ sāditabbaṃ pariyaisitabba’nti vadāmī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. bhadrakasuttaṃ

363. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā mal‍laisu viharati uruvailakappaṃ nāma mal‍lānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhadrakau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau bhadrakau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā dukkhassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaitū’’ti| ‘‘ahañ‍cai ahañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tai, gāmaṇi, atītamaddhānaṃ ārabbha  dukkhassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaiyyaṃ – ‘aivaṃ ahausi atītamaddhāna’nti, tatra tai siyā kaṅkhā, siyā vimati| ahaṃ cai ahañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tai, gāmaṇi, anāgatamaddhānaṃ ārabbha dukkhassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaiyyaṃ – ‘aivaṃ bhavissati anāgatamaddhāna’nti, tatrāpi tai siyā kaṅkhā, siyā vimati| api cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, idhaiva nisin‍nau  aitthaiva tai nisin‍nassa dukkhassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau bhadrakau gāmaṇi bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, atthi tai uruvailakappai manussā yaisaṃ tai vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘atthi mai, bhantai, uruvailakappai manussā yaisaṃ mai vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti| ‘‘atthi pana tai, gāmaṇi, uruvailakappai manussā yaisaṃ tai  vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā nuppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘atthi mai, bhantai, uruvailakappai manussā yaisaṃ mai vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā nuppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti| ‘‘kau nu khau, gāmaṇi, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tai aikac‍cānaṃ uruvailakappiyānaṃ manussānaṃ vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘yaisaṃ mai, bhantai, uruvailakappiyānaṃ manussānaṃ vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā, atthi mai taisu chandarāgau| yaisaṃ pana, bhantai, uruvailakappiyānaṃ manussānaṃ vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā nuppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā, natthi mai taisu chandarāgau’’ti| ‘‘iminā tvaṃ, gāmaṇi, dhammaina diṭṭhaina viditaina akālikaina pattaina pariyaugāḷhaina atītānāgatai nayaṃ naihi – ‘yaṃ khau kiñ‍ci atītamaddhānaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍ji uppaj‍jati (sabbattha) sabbaṃ taṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ| chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassa| yampi hi kiñ‍ci anāgatamaddhānaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jissati, sabbaṃ taṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ| chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassā’’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ subhāsitamidaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bhantai, bhagavatā yaṅkiñ‍ci atītamaddhānaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jati, sabbantaṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ, chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassāti, yaṅkiñ‍ci anāgatamaddhānaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jissati sabbantaṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ, chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassā’’ti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ  uppaj‍jati, sabbaṃ taṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ| chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassā’ti| ‘‘yaṅkiñ‍ci atītamaddhānaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jati, sabbantaṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ, chandau hi mūlaṃ dūkkhassāti, yaṅkiñ‍ci anāgatamaddhānaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jissati, sabbantaṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ, chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassā’’ti (syā॰ kaṃ) atthi mai, bhantai, ciravāsī nāma kumārau bahi āvasathai āvaisanai (?) paṭivasati| sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, kālassaiva vuṭṭhāya purisaṃ uyyaujaimi uyyaujaisiṃ (ka॰) – ‘gaccha, bhaṇai, ciravāsiṃ kumāraṃ jānāhī’ti| yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhantai, sau purisau nāgacchati, tassa mai hautaiva añ‍ñathattaṃ – ‘mā haiva ciravāsissa kumārassa kiñ‍ci ābādhayitthā’’’ti ābādhayaithāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, ciravāsissa kumārassa vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘ciravāsissa mai, bhantai, kumārassa vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā jīvitassapi siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, kiṃ pana mai nuppaj‍jissanti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti| ‘‘imināpi khau aitaṃ, gāmaṇi, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jati, sabbaṃ taṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ| chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, yadā tai ciravāsimātā ciravāsissa mātā (sī॰ pī॰) adiṭṭhā ahausi, assutā ahausi, tai ciravāsimātuyā chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘dassanaṃ vā tai, gāmaṇi, āgamma savanaṃ vā aivaṃ tai ahausi – ‘ciravāsimātuyā chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, ciravāsimātuyā tai vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘ciravāsimātuyā  mai, bhantai, vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā jīvitassapi siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ, kiṃ pana mai nuppaj‍jissanti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti! ‘‘imināpi khau aitaṃ, gāmaṇi, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jati, sabbaṃ taṃ chandamūlakaṃ chandanidānaṃ| chandau hi mūlaṃ dukkhassā’’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. rāsiyasuttaṃ

364. atha  khau rāsiyau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rāsiyau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhajīviṃ aikaṃsaina upavadati upak‍kausatī’ti upak‍kausati upavadatīti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.381)| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhajīviṃ aikaṃsaina upavadati upak‍kausatī’ti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti? ‘‘yai  tai, gāmaṇi, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhajīviṃ aikaṃsaina upavadati upak‍kausatī’ti, na mai tai vuttavādinau, abbhācikkhanti ca pana maṃ tai asatā tucchā abhūtaina’’|

‘‘dvaimai, gāmaṇi, antā pabbajitaina na saivitabbā – yau cāyaṃ kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau hīnau gammau pauthuj‍janikau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, yau cāyaṃ attakilamathānuyaugau dukkhau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau| aitai tai, gāmaṇi, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā  – cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| katamā ca sā, gāmaṇi, majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā – cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau sā, gāmaṇi, majjhimā paṭipadā  tathāgataina abhisambuddhā – cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tayau khau mai, gāmaṇi, kāmabhauginau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati, sāhasaina adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaiti na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina| adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati na puñ‍ñāni karauti| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati  sāhasaina| adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi| dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi na  attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi| dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati, sāhasainapi asāhasainapi| dhammādhammaina bhaugai  pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti|

‘‘idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina| dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina| dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina| dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| tai ca bhaugai gadhitau gathitau (sī॰) mucchitau ajjhaupan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina| dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina  attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| tai ca bhaugai agadhitau amucchitau anajjhaupan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina, adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina na attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| katamaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? adhammaina  bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na attānaṃ sukhaiti na pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina, adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau, aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina  pāsaṃsau? attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaina, adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasaina  attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau, dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau? adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaināti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? attānaṃ  sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau, imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi, dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi na attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na  puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau, tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau, imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi, dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, dvīhi  ṭhānaihi gārayhau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina  gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasainapi asāhasainapi, dhammādhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā sāhasainapi asāhasainapi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau? adhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati sāhasaināti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina, na attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau, dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? na attānaṃ sukhaiti, na pīṇaitīti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau, imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti, na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau? na saṃvibhajati, na puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti, tai ca bhaugai gadhitau mucchitau ajjhaupan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau? tai ca bhaugai gadhitau mucchitau ajjhaupan‍nau anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jatīti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ kāmabhaugī dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaina, dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisitvā asāhasaina attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karauti| tai  ca bhaugai agadhitau amucchitau anajjhaupan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī catūhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau| katamaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? dhammaina bhaugai pariyaisati asāhasaināti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| saṃvibhajati puñ‍ñāni karautīti, iminā tatiyaina  ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| tai ca bhaugai agadhitau amucchitau anajjhaupan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jatīti, iminā catutthaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, kāmabhaugī imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tayaumai, gāmaṇi, tapassinau lūkhajīvinau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau tapassī lūkhajīvī saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘appaiva nāma kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigacchaiyyaṃ, appaiva nāma uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ  sacchikaraiyya’nti| sau attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ nādhigacchati, uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ na sacchikarauti|

‘‘idha pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau tapassī lūkhajīvī saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘appaiva nāma kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigacchaiyyaṃ, appaiva nāma uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikaraiyya’nti| sau attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, kusalañhi khau dhammaṃ adhigacchati, uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ na sacchikarauti|

‘‘idha  pana, gāmaṇi, aikac‍cau tapassī lūkhajīvī saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘appaiva nāma kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigacchaiyyaṃ, appaiva nāma uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikaraiyya’nti| sau attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ adhigacchati, uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarauti|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ tapassī lūkhajīvī attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ nādhigacchati, uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ na sacchikarauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, tapassī lūkhajīvī tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau| katamaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaitīti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ nādhigacchatīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ na sacchikarautīti, iminā tatiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, tapassī lūkhajīvī, imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ tapassī lūkhajīvī attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, kusalañhi khau dhammaṃ adhigacchati, uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ na sacchikarauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, tapassī lūkhajīvī dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau, aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau? attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaitīti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ na sacchikarautīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau? kusalañhi khau dhammaṃ adhigacchatīti, iminā  aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, tapassī lūkhajīvī imaihi  dvīhi ṭhānaihi gārayhau, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yvāyaṃ tapassī lūkhajīvī attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaiti, kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ adhigacchati, uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarauti| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, tapassī lūkhajīvī aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau, dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau| katamaina aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau? attānaṃ ātāpaiti paritāpaitīti, iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau| katamaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau? kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ adhigacchatīti, iminā paṭhamaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| uttari ca manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ sacchikarautīti, iminā dutiyaina ṭhānaina pāsaṃsau| ayaṃ, gāmaṇi, tapassī lūkhajīvī iminā aikaina ṭhānaina gārayhau, imaihi dvīhi ṭhānaihi pāsaṃsau|

‘‘tissau imā, gāmaṇi, sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| katamā tissau? yaṃ rattau rāgādhikaraṇaṃ attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti| rāgai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāya caitaiti, na parabyābādhāya caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti| sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| yaṃ duṭṭhau dausādhikaraṇaṃ attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi  caitaiti| dausai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāya caitaiti, na parabyābādhāya caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti| sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā  akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| yaṃ mūḷhau mauhādhikaraṇaṃ attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti| mauhai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāya caitaiti, na parabyābādhāya caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti| sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| imā khau, gāmaṇi, tissau sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, rāsiyau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. pāṭaliyasuttaṃ

365. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kauliyaisu viharati uttaraṃ nāma uttarakaṃ nāma (sī॰) kauliyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau pāṭaliyau gāmaṇi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pāṭaliyau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘samaṇau gautamau māyaṃ jānātī’ti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau māyaṃ jānātī’ti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau  vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ  ṭhānaṃ āgacchati? anabbhācikkhitukāmā hi mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavanta’’nti| ‘‘yai tai, gāmaṇi, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau māyaṃ jānātī’ti, vuttavādinau caiva mai, tai na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatīti, sac‍caṃyaiva  kira, bhau, mayaṃ taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ na saddahāma – ‘samaṇau gautamau māyaṃ jānātīti, samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau māyāvī’ti| yau nu khau, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ māyaṃ jānāmī’ti, sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ māyāvī’ti| tathaiva taṃ bhagavā hauti, tathaiva taṃ sugata hautī’’ti| taina hi, gāmaṇi, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi; yathā tai khamaiyya, tathā taṃ byākaraiyyāsi –

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, jānāsi tvaṃ kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakai bhaṭai’’ti? ‘‘jānāmahaṃ, bhantai, kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakai bhaṭai’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, kimatthiyā kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭā’’ti? ‘‘yai ca, bhantai, kauliyānaṃ caurā tai ca paṭisaidhaituṃ, yāni ca kauliyānaṃ dūtaiyyāni tāni ca vahātuṃ tāni ca pahātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tāni ca yātuṃ (katthaci), tāni cāvahātuṃ (?), aitadatthiyā, bhantai, kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭā’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, jānāsi tvaṃ kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakai bhaṭai sīlavantai vā tai dussīlai vā’’ti? ‘‘jānāmahaṃ, bhantai, kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakai bhaṭai dussīlai pāpadhammai; yai ca laukai dussīlā pāpadhammā kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭā taisaṃ añ‍ñatarā’’ti| ‘‘yau nu khau, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘pāṭaliyau gāmaṇi jānāti kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakai bhaṭai dussīlai pāpadhammai, pāṭaliyaupi gāmaṇi dussīlau pāpadhammau’ti, sammā nu khau sau vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai! añ‍ñai, bhantai, kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭā, añ‍ñauhamasmi| añ‍ñathādhammā kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭā, añ‍ñathādhammauhamasmī’’ti| ‘‘tvañhi  nāma, gāmaṇi, lacchasi – ‘pāṭaliyau gāmaṇi jānāti kauliyānaṃ lambacūḷakai bhaṭai dussīlai pāpadhammai, na ca pāṭaliyau gāmaṇi dussīlau pāpadhammau’ti, kasmā tathāgatau na lacchati – ‘tathāgatau māyaṃ jānāti, na ca tathāgatau māyāvī’ti? māyaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, māyāya  ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca māyāvī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi’’|

‘‘pāṇātipātaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, pāṇātipātassa ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca pāṇātipātī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| adin‍nādānaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, adin‍nādānassa ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca adin‍nādāyī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| kāmaisumicchācāraṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, kāmaisumicchācārassa  ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca kāmaisumicchācārī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| musāvādaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, musāvādassa ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca musāvādī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| pisuṇavācaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, pisuṇavācāya ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca pisuṇavācau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| pharusavācaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, pharusavācāya ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca pharusavācau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| samphappalāpaṃ  cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, samphappalāpassa ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca samphappalāpī kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| abhijjhaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, abhijjhāya ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca abhijjhālu kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| byāpādapadausaṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, byāpādapadausassa ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau ca byāpan‍nacittau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi| micchādiṭṭhiṃ cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, pajānāmi, micchādiṭṭhiyā ca vipākaṃ, yathāpaṭipan‍nau  ca micchādiṭṭhikau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca pajānāmi|

‘‘santi  hi, gāmaṇi, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yau kauci pāṇamatipātaiti, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| yau kauci adin‍naṃ ādiyati, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai  dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| yau kauci kāmaisu micchā carati, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| yau kauci musā bhaṇati, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’’’ti|

‘‘dissati khau pana, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau mālī kuṇḍalī sunhātau sunahātau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) suvilittau kappitakaisamassu itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraintau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi mālī kuṇḍalī sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’ti? tamainaṃ  aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau rañ‍ñau pac‍catthikaṃ pasayha jīvitā vauraupaisi| tassa rājā attamanau abhihāramadāsi| taināyaṃ purisau mālī kuṇḍalī sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu, itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’’’ti|

‘‘dissati khau, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathiyāya rathiyaṃ rathikāya rathikaṃ (sī॰) siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā, dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā, dakkhiṇatau  nagarassa sīsaṃ chij‍jamānau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi, daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindatī’ti chij‍jatīti (katthaci)? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau rājavairī itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā jīvitā vauraupaisi, taina naṃ rājānau gahaitvā aivarūpaṃ kammakāraṇaṃ kāraintī’’’ti|
