‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu tai aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti| ‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yau kauci pāṇamatipātaiti, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti, sac‍caṃ vā tai āhaṃsu musā vā’’ti? ‘‘musā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai tucchaṃ musā vilapanti, sīlavantau vā tai dussīlā vā’’ti? ‘‘dussīlā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai dussīlā  pāpadhammā micchāpaṭipan‍nā vā tai sammāpaṭipan‍nā vā’’ti? ‘‘micchāpaṭipan‍nā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai micchāpaṭipan‍nā micchādiṭṭhikā vā tai sammādiṭṭhikā vā’’ti? ‘‘micchādiṭṭhikā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai micchādiṭṭhikā kal‍laṃ nu taisu pasīditu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘dissati khau pana, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau mālī kuṇḍalī…pai॰… itthikāmaihi  rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraintau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi mālī kuṇḍalī…pai॰… itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’ti? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau rañ‍ñau pac‍catthikassa pasayha ratanaṃ ahāsi pasayha adin‍naṃ ratanaṃ ādiyi (ka॰)| tassa rājā attamanau abhihāramadāsi| taināyaṃ purisau mālī kuṇḍalī…pai॰… itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’’’ti|

‘‘dissati khau, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau daḷhāya raj‍juyā…pai॰… dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chij‍jamānau tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi daḷhāya raj‍juyā…pai॰… dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindatī’ti? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau gāmā vā arañ‍ñā vā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyi| taina naṃ rājānau gahaitvā aivarūpaṃ kammakāraṇaṃ kāraintī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu tai aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti| ‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yau kauci adin‍naṃ ādiyati, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti, sac‍caṃ vā tai āhaṃsu musā vāti…pai॰… kal‍laṃ nu taisu pasīditu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘dissati khau pana, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau mālī kuṇḍalī…pai॰… itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraintau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi mālī kuṇḍalī…pai॰… itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’ti? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau rañ‍ñau pac‍catthikassa dāraisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍ji| tassa rājā attamanau abhihāramadāsi| taināyaṃ purisau mālī kuṇḍalī…pai॰… itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’’’ti|

‘‘dissati  khau, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau daḷhāya raj‍juyā…pai॰… dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chij‍jamānau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi daḷhāya raj‍juyā…pai॰… dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindatī’ti? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kulitthīsu kulakumārīsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍ji, taina naṃ rājānau gahaitvā aivarūpaṃ kammakāraṇaṃ kāraintī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu tai aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti| ‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yau kauci kāmaisu micchā carati, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti, sac‍caṃ vā tai āhaṃsu musā vāti…pai॰… kal‍laṃ nu taisu pasīditu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘dissati  khau pana, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau mālī kuṇḍalī sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraintau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi  mālī kuṇḍalī sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’ti? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau rājānaṃ musāvādaina hāsaisi| tassa rājā attamanau abhihāramadāsi| taināyaṃ purisau mālī kuṇḍalī sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu itthikāmaihi rājā mañ‍ñai paricāraitī’’’ti|

‘‘dissati khau, gāmaṇi, idhaikac‍cau daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chij‍jamānau| tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau kiṃ akāsi daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā, dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā, dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindatī’ti? tamainaṃ aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ambhau! ayaṃ purisau gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā musāvādaina atthaṃ bhañ‍ji, taina naṃ rājānau gahaitvā aivarūpaṃ kammakāraṇaṃ kāraintī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu tai aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘diṭṭhañ‍ca nau, bhantai, sutañ‍ca suyyissati cā’’ti| ‘‘tatra, gāmaṇi, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yau kauci musā bhaṇati, sabbau sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai  dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti, sac‍caṃ vā tai āhaṃsu musā  vā’’ti? ‘‘musā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai tucchaṃ musā vilapanti sīlavantau vā tai dussīlā vā’’ti? ‘‘dussīlā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai dussīlā pāpadhammā micchāpaṭipan‍nā vā tai sammāpaṭipan‍nā vā’’ti? ‘‘micchāpaṭipan‍nā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai micchāpaṭipan‍nā micchādiṭṭhikā vā tai sammādiṭṭhikā vā’’ti? ‘‘micchādiṭṭhikā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yai pana tai micchādiṭṭhikā kal‍laṃ nu taisu pasīditu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! atthi mai, bhantai, āvasathāgāraṃ| tattha atthi mañ‍cakāni, atthi āsanāni, atthi udakamaṇikau, atthi tailappadīpau| tattha yau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā vāsaṃ upaiti, taināhaṃ yathāsatti yathābalaṃ saṃvibhajāmi| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, cattārau satthārau nānādiṭṭhikā nānākhantikā nānārucikā, tasmiṃ āvasathāgārai vāsaṃ upagacchuṃ’’|

‘‘aikau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau| natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’’’ti|

‘‘aikau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi  ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā, yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’’’ti|

‘‘aikau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ  karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇaṃ caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau  chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttaraṃ caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’’ti|

‘‘aikau satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ  chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇaṃ caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| uttaraṃ caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina atthi puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, ahudaiva kaṅkhā, ahu vicikicchā – ‘kausu nāma imaisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sac‍caṃ āha, kau musā’’’ti?

‘‘alañhi tai, gāmaṇi, kaṅkhituṃ, alaṃ vicikicchituṃ| kaṅkhanīyai ca pana tai ṭhānai vicikicchā uppan‍nā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ pasan‍nauhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati| pahauti mai bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathāhaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyya’’nti|

‘‘atthi, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi| aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi| katamau ca, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi? idha, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau  hauti, adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti, kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau  hauti, pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, abhijjhaṃ pahāya anabhijjhālu hauti, byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, micchādiṭṭhiṃ pahāya sammādiṭṭhikau hauti|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā, sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ‘sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ yauhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) na kiñ‍ci  kañ‍ci (?) byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha ubhayattha mai (?) ma॰ ni॰ 2.95 pāḷiyā saṃsandaitabbaṃ kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti paraṃ maraṇā na upapaj‍jissāmīti (?)| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā  catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā, sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā  pavaidaintī’ti| ‘sacai tassa  bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina  pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ  aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ  pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| ‘sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti paraṃ maraṇā na upapaj‍jissāmīti (?)| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa  khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau maittāsahagatai caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya  sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina  avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – karautau kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ  puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina atthi puñ‍ñaṃ atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamauti| sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ  samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau karuṇāsahagatai  caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau upaikkhāsahagatai caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina  caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā natthi pitā natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintīti| sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ  khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau upaikkhāsahagatai caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina  avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā atthi pitā atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintīti| sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa  khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau upaikkhāsahagatai caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – karautau kārayatau, chaidatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ  karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ  pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| ‘sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsi|

‘‘sa khau sau, gāmaṇi, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vigatābhijjhau vigatabyāpādau asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau upaikkhāsahagatai caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yvāyaṃ satthā aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – karautau kārayatau, chindatau  chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau, saucayatau saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ  haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau  ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, atthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau, yajantau yajāpaintau, atthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina atthi puñ‍ñaṃ, atthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| ‘sacai tassa bhautau satthunau sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, apaṇṇakatāya mayhaṃ, yvāhaṃ na kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā? ubhayamaittha kaṭaggāhau, yaṃ camhi kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau, yañ‍ca kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti| tassa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ayaṃ khau, gāmaṇi, dhammasamādhi| tatra cai tvaṃ cittasamādhiṃ paṭilabhaiyyāsi, aivaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahaiyyāsī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, pāṭaliyau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| tairasamaṃ|

gāmaṇisaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

caṇḍau  puṭau yaudhājīvau, hatthassau asibandhakau|

daisanā saṅkhakulaṃ maṇicūḷaṃ, bhadrarāsiyapāṭalīti||


9. asaṅkhatasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. kāyagatāsatisuttaṃ

366. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘asaṅkhatañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asaṅkhatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? kāyagatāsati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau’’|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ vau mayā asaṅkhataṃ, daisitau asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha nijjhāyatha (ka॰), bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. samathavipassanāsuttaṃ

367. ‘‘asaṅkhatañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asaṅkhatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? samathau ca vipassanā ca| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. savitak‍kasavicārasuttaṃ

368. ‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? savitak‍kasavicārau samādhi, avitak‍kavicāramattau samādhi, avitak‍kaavicārau samādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. suñ‍ñatasamādhisuttaṃ

369. ‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? suñ‍ñatau samādhi, animittau samādhi, appaṇihitau samādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. satipaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

370. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sammappadhānasuttaṃ

371. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? cattārau sammappadhānā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. iddhipādasuttaṃ

372. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? cattārau iddhipādā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. indriyasuttaṃ

373. ‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? pañ‍cindriyāni| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. balasuttaṃ

374. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? pañ‍ca balāni| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. baujjhaṅgasuttaṃ

375. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? satta baujjhaṅgā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

11. maggaṅgasuttaṃ

376. ‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ vau mayā asaṅkhataṃ, daisitau asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kāyau  samathau savitak‍kau, suñ‍ñatau satipaṭṭhānā|

sammappadhānā iddhipādā, indriyabalabaujjhaṅgā|

maggaina aikādasamaṃ, tassuddānaṃ pavuc‍cati||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. asaṅkhatasuttaṃ

377. ‘‘asaṅkhatañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asaṅkhatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? samathau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ vau mayā asaṅkhataṃ, daisitau asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanīti|

‘‘asaṅkhatañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asaṅkhatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? vipassanā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ vau mayā asaṅkhataṃ…pai॰… ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanīti|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? savitak‍kau  savicārau samādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? avitak‍kau  vicāramattau samādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? suñ‍ñatau samādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? animittau samādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? appaṇihitau samādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cittai cittānupassī…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ  pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati  cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā  asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau …pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vīmaṃsasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhābalaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vīriyabalaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satibalaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samādhibalaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau…pai॰… katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāvaiti  …pai॰… sammāvācaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammākammantaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāājīvaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsatiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… asaṅkhatañ‍ca vau bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi asaṅkhatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhataṃ…pai॰…? katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ vau mayā asaṅkhataṃ, daisitau asaṅkhatagāmimaggau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā  vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anatasuttaṃ

378. ‘‘anatañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, anatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anataṃ…pai॰…’’| (yathā asaṅkhataṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)| dutiyaṃ|

3-32. anāsavādisuttaṃ

379-408. ‘‘anāsavañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anāsavagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anāsavaṃ…pai॰… sac‍cañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi sac‍cagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sac‍caṃ…pai॰… pārañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi pāragāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāraṃ…pai॰… nipuṇañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi nipuṇagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nipuṇaṃ…pai॰… sududdasañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi sududdasagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sududdasaṃ…pai॰… ajaj‍jarañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi ajaj‍jaragāmiñ‍ca  maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ajaj‍jaraṃ…pai॰… dhuvañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi dhuvagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhuvaṃ…pai॰… apalaukitañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi apalaukitagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, apalaukitaṃ…pai॰… anidassanañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anidassanagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anidassanaṃ…pai॰… nippapañ‍cañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi nippapañ‍cagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nippapañ‍caṃ…pai॰…?

‘‘santañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi santagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, santaṃ…pai॰… amatañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi amatagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, amataṃ…pai॰… paṇītañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi paṇītagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paṇītaṃ…pai॰… sivañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi sivagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sivaṃ…pai॰… khaimañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi khaimagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, khaimaṃ…pai॰… taṇhākkhayañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi taṇhākkhayagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, taṇhākkhayaṃ…pai॰…?

‘‘acchariyañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi acchariyagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, acchariyaṃ…pai॰… abbhutañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi abbhutagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, abbhutaṃ…pai॰… anītikañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anītikagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anītikaṃ…pai॰… anītikadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anītikadhammagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anītikadhammaṃ…pai॰… nibbānañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi nibbānagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nibbānaṃ…pai॰… abyāpajjhañ‍ca abyāpajjhañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi abyāpajjhagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, abyāpajjhaṃ…pai॰… virāgañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi virāgagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, virāgau…pai॰…?

‘‘suddhiñ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi suddhigāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, suddhi…pai॰… muttiñ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi muttigāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, mutti…pai॰… anālayañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anālayagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anālayau…pai॰… dīpañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi dīpagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dīpaṃ…pai॰… laiṇañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi laiṇagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, laiṇaṃ…pai॰… tāṇañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi tāṇagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, tāṇaṃ…pai॰… saraṇañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi saraṇagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saraṇaṃ…pai॰…anusāsanī’’ti? bāttiṃsatimaṃ|

33. parāyanasuttaṃ

409. ‘‘parāyanañ‍ca  parāyaṇañ‍ca (pī॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi parāyanagāmiñ‍ca maggaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, parāyanaṃ? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau  mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, parāyanaṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, parāyanagāmī maggau? kāyagatāsati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, parāyanagāmimaggau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, daisitaṃ vau mayā parāyanaṃ, daisitau parāyanagāmimaggau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| (yathā asaṅkhataṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)| taittiṃsatimaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

asaṅkhataṃ anataṃ anāsavaṃ, sac‍cañ‍ca pāraṃ nipuṇaṃ sududdasaṃ|

ajaj‍jaraṃ dhuvaṃ apalaukitaṃ, anidassanaṃ nippapañ‍ca santaṃ||

amataṃ  paṇītañ‍ca sivañ‍ca khaimaṃ, taṇhākkhayau acchariyañ‍ca abbhutaṃ|

anītikaṃ anītikadhammaṃ, nibbānamaitaṃ sugataina daisitaṃ||

abyāpajjhau  virāgau ca, suddhi mutti anālayau|

dīpau laiṇañ‍ca tāṇañ‍ca, saraṇañ‍ca parāyananti||

asaṅkhatasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



10. abyākatasaṃyuttaṃ

1. khaimāsuttaṃ

410. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina khaimā bhikkhunī kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānā antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca sākaitaṃ tauraṇavatthusmiṃ vāsaṃ upagatā hauti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau sākaitā sāvatthiṃ gacchantau, antarā ca sākaitaṃ antarā ca sāvatthiṃ tauraṇavatthusmiṃ aikarattivāsaṃ upagacchi| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tauraṇavatthusmiṃ tathārūpaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā jāna yamahaṃ aj‍ja payirupāsaiyya’’nti|

‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau sau purisau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa paṭissutvā kaivalakappaṃ tauraṇavatthuṃ āhiṇḍantau anvāhiṇḍantau (sī॰) nāddasa tathārūpaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā yaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau payirupāsaiyya| addasā khau sau purisau khaimaṃ bhikkhuniṃ tauraṇavatthusmiṃ vāsaṃ upagataṃ| disvāna yaina rājā pasainadi kausalau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘natthi khau, daiva, tauraṇavatthusmiṃ tathārūpau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yaṃ daivau payirupāsaiyya| atthi ca khau, daiva, khaimā nāma bhikkhunī, tassa bhagavatau sāvikā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| tassā khau pana ayyāya  aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau  – ‘paṇḍitā, viyattā maidhāvinī bahussutā cittakathā kalyāṇapaṭibhānā’ti| taṃ daivau payirupāsatū’’ti|

atha  khau rājā pasainadi kausalau yaina khaimā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā khaimaṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā pasainadi kausalau khaimaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ayyai, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, bhagavatā – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panayyai, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ayyai, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, bhagavatā – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panayyai, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti| ‘‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ayyai, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, iti puṭṭhā samānā – ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, bhagavatā – hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panayyai, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhā samānā – ‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, ayyai, hauti ca  na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhā samānā – ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, bhagavatā – hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panayyai, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhā samānā – ‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kau nu khau, ayyai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’’ti?

‘‘taina hi, mahārāja, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, atthi tai kauci gaṇakau vā muddikau vā saṅkhāyakau vā yau pahauti gaṅgāya vālukaṃ vālikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) gaṇaituṃ – aittakā aittikā (sī॰) vālukā iti vā, aittakāni vālukasatāni iti vā, aittakāni vālukasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni vālukasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, ayyai’’| ‘‘atthi pana tai kauci gaṇakau vā muddikau vā saṅkhāyakau vā yau pahauti mahāsamuddai  udakaṃ gaṇaituṃ – aittakāni udakāḷhakāni iti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasatāni  iti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, ayyai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘mahāyyai, samuddau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, yaina rūpai tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau  pañ‍ñāpaiyya taṃ rūpaṃ tathāgatassa pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| rūpasaṅkhāyavimuttau khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti|

‘‘yāya vaidanāya tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya,  sā vaidanā tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| vaidanāsaṅkhāyavimuttau, mahārāja, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti|

‘‘yāya sañ‍ñā tathāgataṃ…pai॰… yaihi saṅkhāraihi tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya, tai saṅkhārā tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| saṅkhārasaṅkhāyavimuttau khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti|

‘‘yaina viñ‍ñāṇai tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatassa pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ  anuppādadhammaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇasaṅkhāyavimuttau khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaitī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau khaimāya bhikkhuniyā  bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā khaimaṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau aparaina samayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ  khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, mayā – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, mayā – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, mahārāja, mayā – hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi…pai॰…| ‘‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘‘‘aitampi khau, mahārāja, abyākataṃ mayā – naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘taina hi, mahārāja, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, atthi tai kauci gaṇakau vā muddikau vā saṅkhāyakau vā yau pahauti gaṅgāya vālukaṃ gaṇaituṃ  – aittakā vālukā iti vā…pai॰… aittakāni vālukasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘atthi pana tai kauci gaṇakau vā muddikau vā saṅkhāyakau vā yau pahauti mahāsamuddai udakaṃ gaṇaituṃ – aittakāni udakāḷhakāni iti vā…pai॰… aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘mahā, bhantai, samuddau gambhīrau  appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, yaina rūpaina tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya, taṃ rūpaṃ tathāgatassa pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ  anuppādadhammaṃ| rūpasaṅkhāyavimuttau  khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti| yāya vaidanāya…pai॰… yāya sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… yaihi saṅkhāraihi…pai॰…’’|

‘‘yaina viñ‍ñāṇaina tathāgataṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya, taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatassa pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇasaṅkhāyavimuttau khau, mahārāja, tathāgatau gambhīrau appamaiyyau duppariyaugāhau – saiyyathāpi mahāsamuddau| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaiti, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na upaitī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yatra hi nāma satthu caiva satthunau caiva (sī॰) sāvikāya ca atthaina atthau byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṃsandissati, samaissati, na viraudhayissati vihāyissati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), vigāyissati (ka॰) yadidaṃ aggapadasmiṃ| aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ khaimaṃ bhikkhuniṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ apucchiṃ| sāpi mai ayyā aitaihi padaihi aitaihi byañ‍janaihi aitamatthaṃ byākāsi, saiyyathāpi bhagavā| acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yatra hi nāma satthu caiva sāvikāya ca atthaina atthau byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṃsandissati, samaissati, na viraudhayissati yadidaṃ aggapadasmiṃ| handa dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma| bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassa dāni tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā pasainadi kausalau bhagavatau  bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā  anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmīti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anurādhasuttaṃ

411. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā anurādhau bhagavatau avidūrai arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharati| atha khau sambahulā añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā yaināyasmā anurādhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā anurādhaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantaṃ anurādhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, āvusau anurādha, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau imaisu catūsu ṭhānaisu pañ‍ñāpayamānau  pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, āvusau, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantaṃ anurādhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sau cāyaṃ yau cāyaṃ (sī॰) bhikkhu navau bhavissati acirapabbajitau, thairau vā pana bālau abyattau’’ti| atha khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantaṃ anurādhaṃ navavādaina ca bālavādaina ca apasādaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha  khau āyasmatau anurādhassa acirapak‍kantaisu añ‍ñatitthiyaisu paribbājakaisu aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau maṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā uttariṃ pucchaiyyuṃ, kathaṃ byākaramānau nu khvāhaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau assaṃ, na  ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyaṃ, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti? atha  khau āyasmā anurādhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anurādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau avidūrai arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharāmi| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā mayā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, āvusau anurādha, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau imaisu catūsu ṭhānaisu pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyai paribbājakai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘‘yau sau, āvusau, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sau cāyaṃ bhikkhu navau bhavissati acirapabbajitau thairau vā pana bālau abyattau’’ti| atha khau maṃ, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā navavādaina ca bālavādaina ca  apasādaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, acirapak‍kantaisu taisu añ‍ñatitthiyaisu  paribbājakaisu aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau maṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā uttariṃ pucchaiyyuṃ, kathaṃ byākaramānau nu khvāhaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau assaṃ, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyaṃ, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti?…pai॰… sañ‍ñā  …pai॰… saṅkhārā…pai॰… ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, anurādha, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na  maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yai kaici saṅkhārā…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na  maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, anurādha, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sañ‍ñaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saṅkhārai tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpasmiṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘añ‍ñatra rūpā tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanāya…pai॰… añ‍ñatra vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰…  añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… saṅkhāraisu…pai॰… añ‍ñatra saṅkhāraihi…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpaṃ, vaidanaṃ, sañ‍ñaṃ, saṅkhārai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, ayaṃ sau arūpī avaidanau asañ‍ñī asaṅkhārau aviñ‍ñāṇau tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aittha ca tai, anurādha, diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sac‍catau thaitatau tathāgatai anupalabbhiyamānai tathāgatau anupalabbhiyamānau (syā॰ ka॰), tathāgatai anupalabbhamānai (?) kal‍laṃ nu tai taṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya (sī॰) – yau sau, āvusau, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anurādha! pubbai cāhaṃ, anurādha, aitarahi ca dukkhañ‍caiva pañ‍ñāpaimi dukkhassa ca niraudha’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamasāriputtakauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

412. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau, āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau  sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi  khau, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau, ‘abyākataṃ  khau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā – hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi…pai॰… ‘kiṃ panāvusau, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘aitampi khau, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| kau nu khau, āvusau, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti khau, āvusau, rūpagatamaitaṃ| na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, rūpagatamaitaṃ| hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, rūpagatamaitaṃ| naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, rūpagatamaitaṃ| hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti khau, āvusau, vaidanāgatamaitaṃ| na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, vaidanāgatamaitaṃ| hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, vaidanāgatamaitaṃ| naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, vaidanāgatamaitaṃ| hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti khau, āvusau, sañ‍ñāgatamaitaṃ| na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, sañ‍ñāgatamaitaṃ| hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, sañ‍ñāgatamaitaṃ| naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, sañ‍ñāgatamaitaṃ| hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti khau, āvusau, saṅkhāragatamaitaṃ| na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, saṅkhāragatamaitaṃ| hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, saṅkhāragatamaitaṃ| naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, saṅkhāragatamaitaṃ| hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti khau, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇagatamaitaṃ| na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, viñ‍ñāṇagatamaitaṃ| hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, viñ‍ñāṇagatamaitaṃ| naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti, viñ‍ñāṇagatamaitaṃ| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasāriputtakauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

413. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau, āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… (sāyaiva  pucchā) ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, āvusau, ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, rūpasamudayaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, rūpaniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’tipissa hauti|

‘‘rūpañ‍ca khau, āvusau, jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, rūpasamudayaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, rūpaniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. tatiyasāriputtakauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

414. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau, āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… (sāyaiva pucchā) ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘rūpai khau, āvusau, avigatarāgassa avigatacchandassa  avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa avigatataṇhassa ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… saṅkhāraisu…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai avigatarāgassa avigatacchandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa avigatataṇhassa ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva  hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| rūpai ca khau, āvusau, vigatarāgassa…pai॰… vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… saṅkhāraisu…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai vigatarāgassa vigatacchandassa vigatapaimassa vigatapipāsassa vigatapariḷāhassa vigatataṇhassa ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. catutthasāriputtakauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

415. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau, āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākauṭṭhikaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti…pai॰… ‘kiṃ panāvusau, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘aitampi khau, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi’’| ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘rūpārāmassa  khau, āvusau, rūparatassa rūpasammuditassa rūpaniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| vaidanārāmassa khau, āvusau, vaidanāratassa vaidanāsammuditassa, vaidanāniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… sañ‍ñārāmassa khau, āvusau…pai॰… saṅkhārārāmassa khau āvusau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇārāmassa khau, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇaratassa viñ‍ñāṇasammuditassa viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti’’|

‘‘na rūpārāmassa khau, āvusau, na rūparatassa na rūpasammuditassa, rūpaniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| na vaidanārāmassa khau, āvusau…pai॰… na sañ‍ñārāmassa khau, āvusau…pai॰… na saṅkhārārāmassa khau, āvusau…pai॰… na viñ‍ñāṇārāmassa khau, āvusau, na viñ‍ñāṇaratassa na viñ‍ñāṇasammuditassa, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰…  ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, āvusau| bhavārāmassa khau, āvusau, bhavaratassa bhavasammuditassa, bhavaniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| na  bhavārāmassa  khau, āvusau, na bhavaratassa na bhavasammuditassa, bhavaniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| ayampi khau, āvusau, pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, āvusau| upādānārāmassa khau, āvusau, upādānaratassa upādānasammuditassa, upādānaniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| na upādānārāmassa khau, āvusau, na upādānaratassa na upādānasammuditassa, upādānaniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti  tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva, hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| ayampi khau āvusau, pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, āvusau| taṇhārāmassa khau, āvusau, taṇhāratassa taṇhāsammuditassa, taṇhāniraudhaṃ ajānatau apassatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa hauti| na taṇhārāmassa khau, āvusau, na taṇhāratassa na taṇhāsammuditassa, taṇhāniraudhaṃ jānatau passatau yathābhūtaṃ, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti…pai॰ … ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipissa na hauti| ayampi khau, āvusau, pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘aittha dāni, āvusau sāriputta, itau uttari kiṃ icchasi? taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttassa, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunau vaṭṭaṃ vattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) vaddhaṃ (pī॰) natthi pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

416. atha  khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahāmauggal‍lānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ  mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, sassatau laukau’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘sassatau laukau’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, asassatau laukau’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘asassatau laukau’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, antavā laukau’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘antavā laukau’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, anantavā laukau’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘anantavā laukau’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ  sarīra’’nti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘kau  nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā, anantavā laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā? kau  pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – sassatau laukautipi, asassatau laukautipi, antavā laukautipi, anantavā laukautipi, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīrantipi, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīrantipi, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇātipi, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇātipi, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇātipi, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇātipī’’ti?

‘‘añ‍ñatitthiyā khau, vaccha, paribbājakā cakkhuṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassanti…pai॰… jivhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassanti…pai॰… manaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassanti| tasmā añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – sassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā| tathāgatau ca khau, vaccha, arahaṃ  sammāsambuddhau cakkhuṃ  ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… jivhaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… manaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| tasmā tathāgatassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – sassatau laukautipi…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇātipī’’ti|

atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sassatau laukau’’ti? abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, mayā – ‘sassatau laukau’ti…pai॰…| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘kau  nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina bhautau gautamassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipī’’ti?

‘‘añ‍ñatitthiyā khau, vaccha, paribbājakā cakkhuṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassanti…pai॰… jivhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassanti…pai॰… manaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassanti| tasmā añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā| tathāgatau ca khau, vaccha, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau cakkhuṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… jivhaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati…pai॰… manaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| tasmā  tathāgatassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi, ‘asassatau laukau’tipi, ‘antavā laukau’tipi, ‘anantavā laukau’tipi, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’ntipi, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’ntipi, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yatra hi nāma satthu ca satthussa ca (sī॰ pī॰), satthu caiva (khaimāsuttai) sāvakassa ca atthaina atthau byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṃsandissati samaissati na viraudhayissati, yadidaṃ aggapadasmiṃ| idānāhaṃ, bhau gautama, samaṇaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ upasaṅkamitvā  aitamatthaṃ apucchiṃ| samaṇaupi mai mauggal‍lānau aitaihi padaihi aitaihi byañ‍janaihi  tamatthaṃ byākāsi, saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ gautamau| acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yatra hi nāma satthu ca sāvakassa ca atthaina atthau byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṃsandissati samaissati na viraudhayissati, yadidaṃ aggapadasmi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. vacchagauttasuttaṃ

417. atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sassatau laukau’’ti? abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, mayā – ‘sassatau laukau’ti…pai॰…| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ mayā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina bhautau gautamassa  aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipī’’ti?

‘‘añ‍ñatitthiyā khau, vaccha, paribbājakā rūpaṃ attatau samanupassanti, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassanti…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassanti, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tasmā añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ  aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā| tathāgatau ca khau, vaccha, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… na sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… na saṅkhārai…pai॰… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na  viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tasmā tathāgatassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipī’’ti|

atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau uṭṭhāyāsanā yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahāmauggal‍lānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, sassatau laukau’’ti? abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘sassatau laukau’ti…pai॰…| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, ‘naiva  hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhau mauggal‍lāna, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā? kau pana, bhau mauggal‍lāna, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipī’’ti?

‘‘añ‍ñatitthiyā  khau, vaccha, paribbājakā rūpaṃ attatau samanupassanti, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassanti…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassanti, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tasmā añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhānaṃ aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā| tathāgatau ca khau, vaccha, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… na  sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… na saṅkhārai…pai॰… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ  vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tasmā tathāgatassa aivaṃ puṭṭhassa na aivaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ hauti – ‘sassatau laukau’tipi, ‘asassatau laukau’tipi, ‘antavā laukau’tipi, ‘anantavā laukau’tipi, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’ntipi, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’ntipi, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau mauggal‍lāna, abbhutaṃ, bhau mauggal‍lāna! yatra hi nāma satthu ca sāvakassa ca atthaina atthau byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṃsandissati, samaissati, na viraudhayissati, yadidaṃ aggapadasmiṃ| idānāhaṃ, bhau mauggal‍lāna, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ apucchiṃ| samaṇaupi mai gautamau aitaihi padaihi aitaihi byañ‍janaihi aitamatthaṃ byākāsi, saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ mauggal‍lānau| acchariyaṃ, bhau mauggal‍lāna, abbhutaṃ, bhau mauggal‍lāna! yatra hi nāma satthu ca sāvakassa ca atthaina atthau byañ‍janaina byañ‍janaṃ saṃsandissati samaissati na viraudhayissati, yadidaṃ aggapadasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kutūhalasālāsuttaṃ

418. atha  khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘purimāni, bhau gautama, divasāni purimatarāni sambahulānaṃ nānātitthiyānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ kutūhalasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘ayaṃ khau pūraṇau kassapau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa| saupi sāvakaṃ abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkataṃ upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’ti| yaupissa  sāvakau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau tampi sāvakaṃ abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkataṃ upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’’’ti|

‘‘ayampi khau makkhali gausālau…pai॰… ayampi khau nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau…pai॰… ayampi khau sañ‍cayau sañ‍jayau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bailaṭṭhaputtau…pai॰… ayampi khau pakudhau pakuddhau (pī॰) kac‍cānau…pai॰… ayampi khau ajitau kaisakambalau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa| saupi sāvakaṃ abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkataṃ upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’ti| yaupissa sāvakau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau tampi sāvakaṃ abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkataṃ  upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’’’ti|

‘‘ayampi khau samaṇau gautamau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa| saupi sāvakaṃ abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkataṃ upapattīsu byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra  upapan‍nau’ti| yaupissa yau ca khvassa (pī॰) sāvakau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau tañ‍ca sāvakaṃ abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkataṃ upapattīsu na byākarauti – ‘asu amutra upapan‍nau, asu amutra upapan‍nau’ti| api ca khau naṃ aivaṃ byākarauti – ‘acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhau gautama, ahu daiva kaṅkhā, ahu vicikicchā – ‘kathaṃ nāma kathañhi nāma (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) kathaṃ kathaṃ nāma (chak‍kaṅguttarai pañ‍camavaggai dutiyasuttai) samaṇassa gautamassa dhammau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau’’’ti dhammābhiñ‍ñaiyyāti (pī॰ ka॰) dhammau… añ‍ñaiyyau (chak‍kaṅguttarai)?

‘‘alañhi tai, vaccha, kaṅkhituṃ, alaṃ vicikicchituṃ| kaṅkhanīyai ca pana tai ṭhānai vicikicchā uppan‍nā| saupādānassa khvāhaṃ, vaccha, upapattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi nau anupādānassa| saiyyathāpi, vaccha, aggi saupādānau jalati, nau anupādānau; aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, vaccha, saupādānassa upapattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi, nau anupādānassā’’ti|
