‘‘yasmiṃ, bhau gautama, samayai ac‍ci vātaina khittā dūrampi gacchati, imassa pana bhavaṃ gautamau kiṃ upādānasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitī’’ti? ‘‘yasmiṃ khau, vaccha, samayai ac‍ci vātaina khittā dūrampi gacchati, tamahaṃ vātūpādānaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi| vātau hissa, vaccha, tasmiṃ samayai upādānaṃ hautī’’ti| ‘‘yasmiñ‍ca  pana, bhau gautama, samayai imañ‍ca kāyaṃ nikkhipati, sattau ca añ‍ñataraṃ kāyaṃ anupapan‍nau hauti, imassa pana bhavaṃ gautamau kiṃ upādānasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitī’’ti? ‘‘yasmiṃ khau, vaccha, samayai imañ‍ca kāyaṃ nikkhipati, sattau ca añ‍ñataraṃ kāyaṃ anupapan‍nau hauti, tamahaṃ taṇhūpādānaṃ vadāmi| taṇhā hissa, vaccha, tasmiṃ samayai upādānaṃ hautī’’ti hautīti…pai॰… (ka॰)| navamaṃ|

10. ānandasuttaṃ

419. atha  khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, atthattā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, natthattā’’ti? dutiyampi khau bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau acirapak‍kantai vacchagauttai paribbājakai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, bhagavā vacchagauttassa paribbājakassa pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na byākāsī’’ti? ‘‘ahañ‍cānanda, vacchagauttassa paribbājakassa ‘atthattā’ti puṭṭhau samānau ‘atthattā’ti byākaraiyyaṃ, yai tai, ānanda, samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā taisamaitaṃ saddhiṃ taisamaitaṃ laddhi (sī॰) abhavissa| ahañ‍cānanda, vacchagauttassa paribbājakassa ‘natthattā’ti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthattā’ti byākaraiyyaṃ, yai tai, ānanda, samaṇabrāhmaṇā  ucchaidavādā taisamaitaṃ saddhiṃ abhavissa| ahañ‍cānanda, vacchagauttassa paribbājakassa ‘atthattā’ti puṭṭhau samānau ‘atthattā’ti byākaraiyyaṃ, api  nu mai taṃ, ānanda, anulaumaṃ abhavissa ñāṇassa uppādāya – ‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘ahañ‍cānanda, vacchagauttassa paribbājakassa  ‘natthattā’ti puṭṭhau samānau ‘natthattā’ti byākaraiyyaṃ, sammūḷhassa, ānanda, vacchagauttassa paribbājakassa bhiyyau sammauhāya abhavissa – ‘ahuvā mai nūna pubbai attā, sau aitarahi natthī’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. sabhiyakac‍cānasuttaṃ

420. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sabhiyau kac‍cānau ñātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaināyasmā sabhiyau kac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sabhiyaina kac‍cānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ sabhiyaṃ kac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhau, kac‍cāna, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau kac‍cāna, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau kac‍cāna, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhau kac‍cāna, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau kac‍cāna, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau kac‍cāna, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau kac‍cāna, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, vaccha, bhagavatā – hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau kac‍cāna, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘aitampi khau, vaccha, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – naiva hauti na  na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vadaisi| kau nu khau, bhau kac‍cāna, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaitaṃ abyākataṃ samaṇaina gautamainā’’ti? ‘‘yau ca, vaccha, haitu, yau ca pac‍cayau pañ‍ñāpanāya rūpīti vā arūpīti vā sañ‍ñīti vā asañ‍ñīti vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīti vā, sau ca haitu, sau ca pac‍cayau sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhaiyya| kaina naṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya rūpīti vā arūpīti vā sañ‍ñīti vā asañ‍ñīti vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīti vā’’ti| ‘‘kīvaciraṃ pabbajitausi, bhau kac‍cānā’’ti? ‘‘naciraṃ, āvusau, tīṇi vassānī’’ti| ‘‘yassapa’ssa, āvusau, aitamaittakaina aittakamaiva taṃpa’ssa bahu, kau pana vādau aivaṃ kau pana vādau aiva (sī॰ pī॰) abhik‍kantai’’ti! aikādasamaṃ|

abyākatasaṃyuttaṃ  samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

khaimāthairī  anurādhau, sāriputtauti kauṭṭhikau|

mauggal‍lānau ca vacchau ca, kutūhalasālānandau|

sabhiyau aikādasamanti|

saḷāyatanavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

saḷāyatanavaidanā, mātugāmau jambukhādakau|

sāmaṇḍakau mauggal‍lānau, cittau gāmaṇi saṅkhataṃ|

abyākatanti dasadhāti||

saḷāyatanavaggasaṃyuttapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
